Abstract: Multi UAV missions in complex environments require the system to understand both the surrounding scene and the intent of a human operator while maintaining feasible task allocation as mission conditions change. This paper presents AgenticSwarm, an agentic framework for semantic perception and adaptive task allocation in heterogeneous multi UAV missions. An agent interprets aerial imagery and natural language instructions to construct a grounded mission representation that links perceived objects and regions with task requirements, capability constraints, and mission dependencies. This information augments a constrained task allocation process in which obstacle aware path feasibility, energy consumption, and protected return home requirements are incorporated before assignment. During execution, changes such as UAV failure, battery degradation, or task modification trigger residual mission reconstruction from the current system state, while completed work and reconnaissance progress are retained. AgenticSwarm is evaluated across five diverse Gazebo environments and an indoor real test environment, demonstrating its ability to connect semantic reasoning with constrained allocation and adaptive multi UAV mission execution. Compared with a Grounding DINO+SAM~2.1 perception baseline, the SAM3-based pipeline improves class-aware recall by 25.2 percentage points (pp) and semantic label accuracy by 29.5 pp. Ablating residual mission replanning increases mean repeated work from 0% to 61.7% and post-event recovery time by 58.6%, highlighting the contribution of adaptive replanning to mission execution.

</details>


### [71] [A Novel Path-Tracking Algorithm for Automated Tractor-Trailer Forward and Backward Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21718)
*Alexandre Lombard,Florent Perronnet,Nicolas Gaud,Abdeljalil Abbas-Turki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向单挂车铰接式车辆的几何路径跟踪算法，通过在挂车层施加横向控制并结合牵引车转向角的短时域预测调整，实现前进与倒车工况下的稳定收敛，无需逐车标定或训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全自主拖拉机-挂车系统在物流、农业与工业场景中日益普及，对精确鲁棒的路径跟踪能力要求很高。但牵引车与挂车之间的铰接带来额外非线性，显著增加横向与纵向控制难度，倒车工况尤为突出。

Method: 方法为几何式控制：在挂车层设计横向控制律，同时对牵引车转向角进行短时域（short-horizon）预测性调整，二者结合以保证前进与后退运动均能稳定收敛至期望路径；该方案无需逐车标定，也无需训练。

Result: 在高保真物理仿真器中的仿真研究表明，该控制器在精度、稳定性以及对扰动的鲁棒性方面可匹配或优于经典方法与当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何式路径跟踪算法为铰接式单挂车车辆提供了一种无需标定与训练、适用于前进和倒车的高精度鲁棒跟踪方案，具备替代经典与先进方法的潜力。

Abstract: Fully autonomous tractor--trailer systems are increasingly deployed in logistics, agriculture, and industrial environments, where precise and robust path-tracking capabilities are essential. However, the articulation between the tractor and the trailer introduces additional nonlinearities and significantly complicates lateral and longitudinal control, particularly during reversing maneuvers. This paper introduces a novel path-tracking algorithm specifically designed for articulated vehicles with a single trailer. The proposed method combines a lateral control law applied at the trailer level with a short-horizon predictive adjustment of the tractor steering angle, ensuring stable convergence toward the desired path in both forward and backward motion. The approach is geometry-based and requires no per-vehicle calibration or training. Simulation studies in a high-fidelity physics simulator demonstrate the ability of the controller to match or outperform classical and state-of-the-art methods in terms of accuracy, stability, and robustness to disturbances.

</details>


### [72] [ZeroTouch: Tactile-Supervised Visual Contact Estimation for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21726)
*Dmitriy Kosenkov,Daniia Zinniatullina,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Iana Zhura,Mikhail Derevianchenko,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZeroTouch 通过腕部RGB、夹爪状态与局部重力方向，以触觉为特权监督预测接触形变、六轴力旋量与抓取相关压缩目标，部署时无需触觉硬件，物理评估成功率显著超越 OpenVLA 与 SmolVLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠抓取需要估计动态物理交互并选择依赖抓取的压缩目标；触觉传感器能提供直接交互测量，但部署时需专用硬件，成本与集成难度高。因此需要一种在训练阶段利用触觉、部署阶段无需触觉的替代方案。

Method: 提出 ZeroTouch 触觉监督框架：输入为腕部RGB观测、夹爪状态和局部重力方向，输出为稠密接触形变、瞬时六轴力旋量以及抓取相关的期望压缩目标；触觉测量仅在训练时作为特权监督信号，部署时不使用。

Result: 完整验证集上，法向力 MAE 由仅状态基线的 2.017 N 降至 0.531 N。物理评估每条件 20 次试验：未见物体成功率 95%、已见物体/未见抓取 80%、内容/负载偏移 90%；相同协议下 OpenVLA 为 25%/40%/55%，SmolVLA 为 10%/25%/35%。

Conclusion: ZeroTouch 表明触觉监督可在无需部署触觉硬件的前提下实现高精度力估计与强泛化抓取能力，在多种分布偏移条件下均大幅优于现有视觉-语言-动作基线。

Abstract: Reliable robotic grasping benefits from estimating the evolving physical interaction and selecting a grasp-dependent compression target. Tactile sensors provide direct interaction measurements but require dedicated hardware at deployment. We introduce ZeroTouch, a tactile-supervised framework that predicts dense contact deformation, the instantaneous six-axis wrench, and a grasp-dependent desired compression target from wrist RGB observations, gripper state, and local gravity direction. Tactile measurements are used only as privileged supervision during training and are not required at deployment. On the full validation set, the complete architecture reduces normal-force MAE from 2.017 N for a state-only baseline to 0.531 N. In physical evaluation with 20 trials per condition, ZeroTouch achieves 95% success on an unseen object, 80% in a seen-object/unseen-grasp condition, and 90% under a content/load shift. Under the same evaluation protocol, OpenVLA achieves 25%, 40%, and 55%, while SmolVLA achieves 10%, 25%, and 35%, respectively.

</details>


### [73] [Visual Proactivity: Enhancing Human-Robot Collaboration Through Intent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21729)
*Valerio Bo,Edison Bejarano,Anaís Garrell,Alberto Sanfeliu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"视觉主动性"（visual proactivity）方法，让机器人通过视觉反馈传达自身意图，在人-机器人交接场景中经用户研究验证其能提升协作的透明度与流畅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人从执行重复任务转向与人协作，理解人类意图至关重要。现有研究多聚焦于机器人如何推断和响应人类意图，却较少关注机器人如何传达自身意图。为增强人机协作的透明度与流畅性，需要让机器人主动表达意图并引导人类行为。

Method: 提出"视觉主动性"方法，使机器人借助视觉反馈传达意图。在人-机器人交接（handover）场景中设计并评估了反应型（reactive）、预期型（anticipatory）和主动型（proactive）三类机器人行为，并通过用户研究验证人类对这三类行为的感知。

Result: 用户研究验证了人类对反应型、预期型和主动型行为的感知，结果表明有效的视觉主动性能够促进更好的人机对齐与协调。

Conclusion: 视觉主动性为更直观的人机协作提供了新路径，通过让机器人主动传达意图来影响人类行为，提升协作透明度与流畅度。

Abstract: As robots transition from performing repetitive tasks to collaborating with humans, understanding human intent becomes crucial to effective interaction. Anticipation enables robots to predict human actions, while proactivity allows them to take initiative and guide human behavior toward optimal outcomes. Although research has largely focused on how robots infer and respond to human intentions, less attention has been paid to how robots communicate their own intent. This paper introduces visual proactivity, a novel, simple yet effective approach that enables robots to communicate their intentions through visual feedback, influencing human behavior and enhancing transparency and fluency. We develop and evaluate proactive robotic behaviors in a human-to-robot handover scenario, where a user study validates human perception of reactive, anticipatory, and proactive behaviors. The results demonstrate that effective visual proactivity fosters better alignment and coordination, paving the way for more intuitive human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [74] [When Should Robots Intervene? Balancing Engagement and Intrusiveness in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21734)
*Lavinia Hriscu,Valerio Bo,Alberto Sanfeliu,Anaís Garrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文比较了"持续寻求互动"与"情境感知"两种机器人干预策略，通过32人模拟医院环境实验发现：高互动频率不一定提升参与度，感知支持与侵扰性之间存在权衡，受物理距离与用户努力程度影响，有效参与依赖自适应、情境敏感的干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务导向场景下的人机交互设计需在用户参与度与可接受的机器人侵扰性之间取得平衡，但不同机器人干预策略如何影响用户体验、互动动态、侵扰感知与支持感尚缺乏实证依据。

Method: 对比两种机器人干预策略：持续寻求参与的连续策略，以及基于用户状态与任务情境选择性干预的情境感知策略；两者均依赖多模态行为线索（身体朝向、注意信号）驱动机器人行为。在模拟医院环境中开展32名被试的用户研究进行评估。

Result: 更高的互动频率并不必然带来更好的参与度；观察到一个系统性的权衡关系——感知支持与侵扰性之间的此消彼长，并受到物理距离与用户努力程度等因素的影响。

Conclusion: 有效的人机交互参与依赖于自适应、情境敏感的干预策略；研究为情境感知干预优于单纯增加互动频率提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Designing effective Human-Robot Interaction in task-oriented settings requires carefully balancing user engagement with socially acceptable levels of robot intrusiveness. In this paper, we examine how different robot intervention strategies shape user experience, interaction dynamics, perceived intrusiveness, and sense of support. We compare two approaches: a continuous engagement-seeking robot strategy, and a context-aware strategy that selectively intervenes based on the user's state and task context. Both approaches rely on multimodal behavioral cues, including body orientation and attentional signals, to guide robot actions. We evaluate these strategies in a user study with 32 participants performing a task in a simulated hospital environment. Our findings show that higher interaction frequency does not necessarily lead to better engagement. Instead, we observe a systematic trade-off between perceived support and intrusiveness, influenced by factors such as physical proximity and user effort. These results provide empirical evidence that effective engagement in HRI depends on adaptive, context-sensitive intervention policies.

</details>


### [75] [Sandwich-Residuals: Parameter-Efficient Test-time Adaptation of World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21740)
*Krishnam Soni,Aditya Sehgal,Vedant Dave,Elmar Rueckert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Sandtch-Residuals 轻量测试时自适应方法，冻结预训练世界模型，仅在线学习预测器周围的小型残差修正，以极少参数量取得优于冻结模型、接近内部块自适应的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型可用于规划，但在测试条件偏离训练分布时预测不可靠。现有测试时自适应方法需更新预训练模型的大量参数（常达数百万），且需人为选择适配哪些内部组件，代价高且不便。

Method: Sandtch-Residuals 保持预训练世界模型完全冻结，仅在预测器周围学习小型残差修正；残差利用模型的自监督预测误差在线优化，不需要奖励、标签或源域数据；相比内部块自适应，适配参数量减少 97–99%。

Result: 在 AdaJEPA 基准的 21 种条件下，成功率达到冻结模型的 1.3 倍，同时保留最强 AdaJEPA 变体 95% 的性能；在复合偏移下提升至冻结模型的 1.9 倍，并与内部块自适应相当；在 DINO-WM 的 3D 操作任务上验证了同一适配原则。

Conclusion: 世界模型的有效测试时自适应不一定需要修改预训练内部权重，轻量残差修正即可实现高效、可扩展的适配。

Abstract: Latent world models enable planning by predicting the effects of actions in a learned representation space, but their predictions can become unreliable when test-time conditions differ from training. Existing test-time adaptation methods address this by updating parts of the pretrained model, often modifying millions of parameters and requiring a choice of which internal components to adapt. We introduce Sandwich-Residuals, a lightweight alternative that keeps the pretrained world model frozen and learns only small residual corrections around the predictor. The residuals are optimized online using the model's self-supervised prediction error and require no rewards, labels, or source-domain data. Across 21 conditions on the AdaJEPA benchmark, our method achieves $1.3\times$ the success rate of the frozen model while retaining 95% of the performance of the strongest AdaJEPA variant and adapting 97-99% fewer parameters. Under compound shifts, this advantage increases to $1.9\times$ the success rate of the frozen model, while remaining comparable to internal block adaptation. We further demonstrate the same adaptation principle on a DINO-WM model for 3-D manipulation. These results suggest that effective test-time adaptation of world models does not necessarily require modifying their pretrained internal weights.

</details>


### [76] [Understanding Engagement and Intrusiveness in Assistive Human-Robot Interaction Using Individual Traits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21744)
*Valerio Bo,Lavinia Hriscu,Alberto Sanfeliu,Anaís Garrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究探讨机器人介入频率与用户个性特质如何共同影响感知侵入性与参与度，实验发现参与度整体稳定，但情感反应与侵入感因个性而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在陌生任务中，用户需同时理解任务要求与协调机器人，机器人助力尤为关键。既有研究对机器人空间距离（proxemics）在用户参与中的作用结论不一：部分研究认为更近距离增强互动，另一些则报告会产生侵入感。作者据此提出，侵入性感知不仅取决于空间距离，还取决于机器人介入的频率，并受个性与人口统计学等个体特质强烈影响。

Method: 开展实验，32名被试与两台辅助机器人互动；两台机器人提供相似的任务支持，但介入策略不同。考察条件间的参与度、情感反应与感知侵入性，并结合个性特质分析互动动态。

Result: 整体参与度在各条件下保持稳定；情感反应与感知侵入性随个性特质显著变化；个性对互动动态的影响方式还依赖机器人行为（介入策略）而不同。

Conclusion: 有效的人机交互应考虑个体差异，依据每位用户独特的情感与行为特征来定制机器人行为，以在维持参与度的同时避免侵入感。

Abstract: Robot assistance is particularly crucial in unfamiliar tasks, where users must understand task requirements while coordinating with the robot. Previous research offers mixed evidence on the role of robot proxemics in user engagement: some studies suggest closer proximity enhances interaction, while others report it can feel intrusive. In this work, we argue that perceptions of intrusiveness depend not only on proxemics but also on the frequency of robot interventions, and are strongly influenced by individual traits such as personality and demographics. We conducted an experiment with 32 participants who interacted with two assistive robots that provided similar task support but differed in their intervention strategies. Results indicate that overall engagement remains stable across conditions, yet affective responses and perceived intrusiveness vary significantly with personality traits. Moreover, personality shapes interaction dynamics differently depending on the robot's behavior. These findings emphasize that effective human-robot interaction should account for individual differences, tailoring robot behavior to maintain engagement while respecting each user's unique affective and behavioral profile.

</details>


### [77] [ForceTwin: Physics-informed Digital Twins for Robotic Manipulation from Instrumented Human Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21751)
*Tim Engelbracht,René Zurbrügg,Mayank Mittal,Marco Hutter,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum,Zuria Bauer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceTwin 通过人手持力传感夹爪探测铰接物体，辨识包含惯性、摩擦与状态相关机构力的物理数字孪生，并用于阻抗控制与全身开门策略，性能显著优于 VLM 先验与纯运动学基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作铰接物体不仅需要运动学信息，还需理解决定其运动的物理属性（惯性、摩擦、弹簧/闭门器等机构），这些属性随构型与速度变化且无法从外观直接观测；外观相同的门操作力度可能差异巨大。现有数字孪生流程主要恢复运动学或依赖视觉与语言先验赋予静态物理参数，易产生物理上不合理的估计，导致状态相关机构动力学未被辨识，也无法表示在标准资产格式中。

Method: 提出 ForceTwin，从带传感器的人机交互中辨识铰接物体的物理信息数字孪生。人使用手持力传感夹爪探测物体，获得同步的位姿与交互力，据此估计铰接结构、参数化动力学（惯性、库仑摩擦、粘性阻尼）以及一个结构化神经残差，用于捕捉状态相关的机构力。所得孪生模型用作阻抗控制的前馈动力学模型，并用于训练全身开门通行策略。

Result: 惯性参数误差相比 VLM 先验降低近一半；作为前馈动力学模型用于 Spot 与 Franka FR3 的阻抗控制时，在九组"物体-本体"组合上达到 87% 目标完成率，而 VLM 先验为 60%、纯运动学孪生为 57%；在机构较强、两个基线均停滞的物体上提升最大。此外，所辨识孪生被用于训练全身开门通行策略并部署到真实世界。

Conclusion: 基于力感知交互辨识的物理信息数字孪生能够有效捕捉状态相关的机构动力学，显著提升跨本体操作与全身策略的真实世界部署性能，突显了从交互力中辨识物理参数相较于视觉/语言先验与纯运动学建模的必要性与优势。

Abstract: Manipulating objects requires understanding not only their motion, but also the physical properties that determine it. For articulated objects, these include inertia, friction, and mechanisms such as springs or door closers, whose effects can vary with configuration and velocity. Such properties are not directly observable from appearance: visually identical doors may require very different effort to manipulate. Existing digital-twin pipelines recover primarily kinematics or assign static physical parameters from visual and language priors, which can yield physically implausible estimates. As a result, state-dependent mechanism dynamics remain unidentified and are not represented in standard asset formats. We present ForceTwin, a system for identifying physics-informed digital twins of articulated objects from instrumented human interaction. A person probes an object using a handheld force-sensing gripper, providing synchronized poses and interaction forces from which we estimate the articulation, parametric dynamics including inertia, Coulomb friction, viscous damping, and a structured neural residual capturing state-dependent mechanism forces. ForceTwin nearly halves the inertial-parameter error of a VLM prior. As a feedforward dynamics model for impedance control on a Spot and a Franka FR3, ForceTwin achieves 87% goal completion across nine object-embodiment pairs, compared with 60% using VLM-prior and 57% using kinematics-only twins, with the largest gains on objects whose strong mechanisms cause both baselines to stall. We further use the identified twins to train whole-body door-traversal policies and deploy them in the real world. Project Page: https://timengelbracht.github.io/forcetwin-website/

</details>


### [78] [PSR: Predictive Sensorimotor Representation Learning for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21753)
*Shengbao Li,Peng Xu,Chao Tang,Hao Wei,Jiaheng Wang,Hong Yin,Jiangtao Chen,Jinxuan Zhu,Zhong Zhou,Mengfan Wang,Tingguang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出预测性感知运动表征（PSR）学习框架，通过多模态Transformer预测未来交互动力学，构建层次化预测表征并注入动作流，实例化为PSR-VLA，在六个真实接触富集任务上达91.7%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触富集操作要求策略超越视觉观测，对接触力、机器人构型与交互历史进行推理以生成精确动作。现有方法仅被动地以力反馈为条件，不主动预测未来接触动力学，限制了高精度动作生成能力。

Method: 提出PSR学习框架：预训练阶段，用多模态Transformer联合预测未来交互动力学，学习层次化预测表征；该表征随后用于增强动作流，使策略在多个深度上利用接触相关线索。进一步将PSR嵌入视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，得到PSR-VLA。

Result: 在六个真实世界接触富集操作任务上评估，PSR-VLA总体成功率达91.7%，分别比π0.5、ForceVLA-π0.5和ForceVLA2-π0.5提升30.0、22.5和19.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果验证了PSR在力感知、接触富集操作中的有效性，表明主动预测接触动力学并构建层次化预测表征有助于提升高精度操作性能。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to generate precise actions by reasoning over contact forces, robot configurations, and interaction histories beyond visual observations. Existing methods passively condition on force feedback rather than actively predicting future contact dynamics, limiting their ability to generate high-precision actions. To address this problem, we introduce Predictive Sensorimotor Representation (PSR) learning, a framework that learns a hierarchy of predictive representations from multimodal sensorimotor signals and integrates them into the action stream of a visuomotor policy. Specifically, during a pretraining stage, a multimodal Transformer is trained to learn a hierarchy of predictive representations by jointly forecasting future interaction dynamics. The learned hierarchy subsequently augments the action stream, enabling the resulting policy to exploit contact-relevant cues at multiple depths. We further instantiate PSR within a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model, resulting in PSR-VLA, and evaluate it on six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks. Experimental results show that PSR-VLA achieves 91.7% overall success, improving over $π_{0.5}$, ForceVLA-$π_{0.5}$, and ForceVLA2-$π_{0.5}$ by 30.0, 22.5, and 19.2 percentage points, respectively. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed PSR for force-aware, contact-rich manipulation. Videos of the tasks and stability tests are available at https://psr-vla.pages.dev/.

</details>


### [79] [CRISP: Contact-Rich Robotic Simulation Platform with Extensive Geometries and Contact Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21761)
*Somang Lee,Sunkyung Park,Jinhee Yun,Seoki An,Dongjun Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRISP 是一个面向复杂多接触场景的高保真物理仿真平台，融合多种几何表示、基于优化的碰撞检测与增广拉格朗日接触求解器，并通过与先进平台对比及复杂机器人操作任务验证了其物理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仿真（如紧公差操作）要求高物理保真度，需同时具备表达力强的几何与接触建模，以及准确鲁棒的数值求解。现有仿真器要么几何表示支持有限、接触建模简化，要么数值方法在精度或鲁棒性上受限，难以满足接触密集与尖锐交互的仿真需求。

Method: 提出 CRISP 仿真平台：支持多样几何表示，采用基于优化的精确碰撞检测；将接触建模与鲁棒的增广拉格朗日（augmented Lagrangian）接触求解器相结合，无需有问题的松弛（relaxations）即可在复杂几何上一致地检测接触信息并精确求解多接触约束。

Result: 与当前先进平台进行对比，验证了 CRISP 的物理保真度；并通过复杂机器人操作场景展示了其能力。平台已开源（https://github.com/INRoL/crisp）。

Conclusion: CRISP 通过几何表示、优化式碰撞检测与增广拉格朗日求解器的集成，实现了多接触约束的高效、准确、鲁棒求解，适用于接触密集与尖锐交互的机器人仿真，为高保真机器人操作仿真提供了可用工具。

Abstract: We present CRISP (Contact-RIch Simulation Platform), a high-fidelity physics engine tailored for complex multi-contact simulations such as tight-tolerance robotic manipulation. Achieving high physical fidelity in robotic simulation requires both expressive modeling of geometry and contact interactions, as well as accurate numerical resolution via robust collision detection and contact solvers. However, existing simulators often either rely on limited support for geometric representations and simplified modeling of contact interactions, or employ numerical resolution methods whose accuracy or robustness is inherently constrained. Accordingly, we develop a new simulator that supports diverse geometric representations with accurate optimization-based collision detection, and combines contact modeling with robust augmented Lagrangian-based contact solvers. This integration enables efficient and consistent detection of contact information across complex geometries while accurately resolving multi-contact constraints without problematic relaxations, which is essential for simulating contact-intensive and sharp interactions. We validate the physical fidelity of our simulator against state-of-the-art platforms and further demonstrate its capabilities through complex robotic manipulation scenarios. CRISP is publicly available at https://github.com/INRoL/crisp.

</details>


### [80] [Scaling Vision-Language Reward Learning for Robot Manipulation in Parallel Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21767)
*Lobna Joualy,Eric Demeester,Nikolaos Tsiogkas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPID通过GPU并行采样、数据感知策略更新、单请求偏好标注、奖励自动稳定与代表性图像采样，将VLM偏好奖励学习训练时间从9.18小时降至1.15小时，API调用从19,840降至896，成功率由86.3%升至98.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用视觉语言模型（VLM）替代人工标注进行偏好奖励学习时，顺序API请求与单环境数据采集导致训练慢且成本高，亟需提升效率并降低API开销。

Method: 提出RAPID系统，将GPU并行rollout与数据感知策略更新耦合，并引入单请求偏好标注、自动奖励稳定化以及代表性图像采样；在IsaacLab的五个Franka Panda操作任务上评估，并进行Gemma 3 12B与GPT-4.1 mini的离线评测。

Result: 在匹配两阶段提示下，平均运行时间由9.18小时降至3.13小时；启用全部组件后训练1.15小时完成，每轮API调用由19,840降至896，总体最终成功率由86.3%升至98.7%，端到端加速8.0倍，API使用减少95.5%；离线评测显示单请求提示在两个模型上均降低标注延迟与成本。

Conclusion: RAPID能显著加速VLM偏好奖励学习并大幅降低API成本，同时提升任务成功率，验证了并行采样、自适应更新与单请求标注等组件的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can replace human annotators in preference-based reward learning, but sequential API requests and single-environment data collection make training slow and costly. We present RAPID (Reward learning with Adaptive Parallel Image Diversity), a system that couples GPU-parallel rollout with data-aware policy updates, single-request preference labeling, automatic reward stabilization, and representative image sampling. We evaluate these components on five Franka Panda manipulation tasks in IsaacLab. Parallel rollout and adaptive updates provide the first substantial reduction in training time: under matched two-stage prompting, mean runtime falls from 9.18 to 3.13 hours. With all RAPID components enabled, training completes in 1.15 hours using 896 rather than 19,840 API calls per run, and aggregate final success rises from 86.3\% to 98.7\%. This represents an 8.0$\times$ end-to-end speedup and a 95.5\% reduction in API usage. An offline evaluation with Gemma~3 12B and GPT-4.1 mini demonstrates that single-request prompting reduces labeling latency and cost across both models. Code is available at: https://github.com/rapid-vlm/rapid-vlm-rl.

</details>


### [81] [TRACE: Coverage Path Planning for Unknown Environments Using Hierarchical Coverage Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21777)
*Zongyuan Shen,Haodong Liu,Gao Wang,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou,Yaming Ou,Zhongqiang Ren,Yikui Zhai,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在线覆盖路径规划算法TRACE，基于分层覆盖树表示未覆盖空间连通性，结合增量全局巡回与局部规划，实现未知环境实时覆盖，仿真与实机实验优于六种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知环境下实时覆盖时，新发现的障碍与已覆盖单元会将剩余未覆盖空间分割为多个不连通区域，导致频繁全局重规划、计算负担大且覆盖进展不连贯，需要一种能高效维护空间结构与覆盖顺序的在线CPP方法。

Method: TRACE构建分层覆盖树，全局表示不断演化的未覆盖空间连通性；当空间被分割时递归扩展对应树节点以显式表示各不连通区域并组织后续规划。基于更新后的树维护增量全局巡回（global tour）引导覆盖。局部仅细化受影响部分，保持未变区域的访问顺序，降低全局重规划开销。局部规划器在全局巡回引导下生成往复式（back-and-forth）覆盖路径，并切换为巡回感知规划以高效完成目标区域。

Result: 理论分析给出TRACE的计算复杂度、完全覆盖性质，以及增量全局巡回细化的近似界。通过大量高保真仿真与移动机器人实机实验验证性能，与六种现有CPP方法对比，在覆盖时间、路径长度、重叠率和转弯次数上均有显著改善。

Conclusion: TRACE通过分层覆盖树与增量全局巡回相结合，实现了计算高效且覆盖一致的未知环境在线覆盖；理论保证完全覆盖并给出近似界，实验证明其在多项关键指标上优于现有方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel online coverage path planning (CPP) algorithm, called TRACE, for real-time coverage of unknown environments. TRACE is built upon a hierarchical coverage tree that provides a global representation of the evolving connectivity of the uncovered space. As the environment is incrementally revealed and covered, newly discovered obstacles and covered cells may fragment the remaining uncovered space into disconnected regions. TRACE recursively expands the corresponding tree nodes to explicitly represent these regions and organize them for subsequent coverage planning. Based on the updated tree, an incremental global tour is maintained to guide the coverage process. TRACE locally refines only the affected portions while preserving the visiting order of unchanged regions, thereby reducing the computational burden of global replanning and maintaining a consistent coverage progression. Guided by the global tour, a local planner generates back-and-forth coverage paths and switches to global-tour-aware planning to efficiently complete the target regions. Theoretical analysis establishes the computational complexity and complete coverage property of TRACE, and derives an approximation bound for the incremental global tour refinement. The performance of TRACE is evaluated through extensive high-fidelity simulations and real-robot experiments using a mobile robot. Comparative evaluations against six existing CPP methods demonstrate significant improvements in coverage time, path length, overlap ratio, and number of turns.

</details>


### [82] [Compact but Moving: Intervention-Relevant Geometry in Recurrent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21787)
*Yuming Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在受控循环世界模型中，紧凑干预校正进入模型后会迅速离开固定入口子空间，但可通过事实循环雅可比链传播入口方向得到低秩图像，仍能捕捉大部分非线性校正并保留反事实未来功能；该模式在多种模型与初始化下复现，并可通过有限时域未来响应算子刻画其局部几何重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作发现学习到的世界模型可能存在紧凑干预，即使循环状态是高维的，但对一次反事实速度干预，其校正进入模型后如何演化尚不清楚；作者希望检验该干预结构是否固定在入口子空间、是否对应动态闭合的低维状态，还是以其他形式持续存在。

Method: 在受控循环世界模型中，利用先前识别出的检查点特定 rank-4 一次性反事实速度干预接口；跟踪校正离开固定入口子空间的过程；将入口方向通过事实循环雅可比链传播以获得低秩图像，并用于切线预测校正与重启；在独立训练的结构化 GRU 和参数匹配的 LSTM（以特权紧凑校正初始化）中复现；进一步定义全循环载体上的有限时域未来响应算子，并通过 patching、方向面板排序和全幅度测定分析未来敏感方向与切线残差。

Result: 校正在自主 rollout 中迅速离开固定入口子空间；但经事实循环雅可比链传播入口方向得到的低秩图像仍能捕捉大部分非线性校正；使用切线预测校正重启可保留大量反事实未来功能；该传播/功能模式在多个独立训练的结构化 GRU 模型和参数匹配的 LSTM 中复现；未来响应算子在 patching 后其主导未来敏感方向向匹配的原生反事实组织移动，并能准确排序已登记方向面板上的有限扰动效应；单独的全幅度测定发现显著的事实端点切线残差，并支持三个检查点中两个的响应重构。

Conclusion: 紧凑干预结构可以作为嵌入高维循环动力学中的、随状态变化的移动局部几何而持续存在，并不意味着存在固定或动态闭合的低维状态。

Abstract: Learned world models may have compact interventions even when their recurrent state is high-dimensional, but it is unclear what happens to such a correction after it enters the model. We study this question in a controlled recurrent world model where prior work identified a checkpoint-specific rank-4 interface for one-shot counterfactual velocity interventions. The correction rapidly leaves this fixed entry subspace during autonomous rollout. Nevertheless, a low-rank image obtained by transporting the entry directions through the factual recurrent Jacobian chain continues to capture most of the nonlinear correction. Restarts using the tangent-predicted correction preserve substantial counterfactual future function. This transport/function pattern recurs across independently trained structured-GRU models and a parameter-matched LSTM initialized with a privileged compact correction. We further characterize a finite-horizon future-response operator over the full recurrent carrier. Patching shifts its leading future-sensitive directions toward the matched native-counterfactual organization, and the local operator accurately ranks finite perturbation effects over the registered direction panels at the patched and native-counterfactual basepoints. A separate full-amplitude assay finds substantial factual-endpoint tangent residuals and supports response reconfiguration in two of three checkpoints. Together, these results show that compact intervention structure can persist as a moving, state-dependent local geometry embedded in high-dimensional recurrent dynamics, without implying a fixed or dynamically closed low-dimensional state.

</details>


### [83] [From Pretraining to Proficiency: Real-World Subtask RL for Long-Horizon Manipulation with Minimal Human Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21788)
*Sichang Su,Benjamin Yang,Zhiyun Deng,Boyuan Liang,Yip Fun Yeung,Zelin Wang,Lingfeng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PARTS是一个真实世界子任务RL微调框架，冻结预训练策略提供标称动作，由选择器和成功验证器激活残差修正并提供局部奖励，从而在稀疏奖励下高效提升长时任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人基础策略能完成长时任务的大部分步骤，却常在少数关键子任务上反复失败。补齐这些短板需要采集完整任务演示进行SFT，但操作者必须重复策略已掌握的熟练行为，代价高昂。现有RL微调方法又难以仅凭稀疏奖励求解长时任务。

Method: PARTS将训练聚焦于瓶颈子任务。执行时冻结的预训练策略持续输出标称动作，由智能体生成的选择器（selectors）与成功验证器（success verifiers）决定何时激活残差修正并提供局部结果奖励，使训练能从成功子任务中学习，即便完整任务成功稀少。训练结合在线RL与成功重加权再训练（success-reweighted retraining），每次再训练后的残差策略重新部署以采集新经验。人工仅在设置阶段识别瓶颈并在需要时进行物理复位。

Result: 在双臂YAM与单臂Franka任务上，PARTS将完整任务成功率分别从32%提升至61%、从50%提升至95%，平均每任务仅需数十分钟真实世界RL rollout。在相同机器人rollout预算下，相比现有真实世界RL微调方法，全任务成功率提升超过25%，且所需人工介入更少。

Conclusion: 通过在瓶颈子任务上集中RL练习、结合冻结策略的标称动作与残差修正及局部奖励，PARTS在稀疏奖励、真实世界条件下高效微调长时任务策略，显著优于现有RL微调方法并减少人工负担。

Abstract: A pretrained robot foundation policy may execute most of a long-horizon task yet repeatedly fail at a few critical subtasks. Collecting additional full-task demonstrations for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) requires operators to repeat behaviors the policy already performs well. Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning offers a promising path to bridge this gap, but existing approaches struggle to solve long-horizon tasks using only sparse rewards. We present PARTS (Policy Adaptation with RL on Targeted Subtasks), a real-world subtask RL framework that concentrates practice at these bottlenecks while allowing training rollouts to proceed with minimal human intervention. The frozen pretrained policy supplies nominal actions throughout execution, while agent-generated selectors and success verifiers activate residual corrections and provide local outcome rewards. These rewards support learning from successful subtasks even when complete-task successes are scarce. Training combines online RL with success-reweighted retraining, and each retrained residual policy is redeployed to collect further experience. Humans identify bottlenecks during setup and perform physical resets when needed. On bimanual YAM and single-arm Franka tasks, PARTS improves complete-task success from 32% to 61% and from 50% to 95%, respectively, using tens of minutes of real-world RL rollouts per task on average. Compared with existing real-world RL fine-tuning methods, PARTS raises full-task success by more than 25% under the same robot-rollout budget while requiring less human involvement.

</details>


### [84] [AcousticDiffusion: Semantically Conditioned Audio-Guided Diffusion Policy for Search-and-Rescue Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21792)
*Iana Zhura,Didar Seyidov,Dmitrii Plotnikov,Hajira Amjad,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AcousticDiffusion：语义条件化的音频引导扩散策略，用于救援机器人朝人声源导航。将冻结音频识别器输出、麦克风阵列DOA与贝叶斯BEV信念场、自我运动补偿结合，条件化扩散模型生成航点轨迹。仿真终点方位误差11.20°，91.78%轨迹在30°内；真机ZSL-1四足未再训练部署，平均方位误差64.9°（A*为98.2°，RRT为90.4°），规划耗时6.07 ms，最终源距由3.96 m降至2.48 m（改善37.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 救援机器人在视觉接触退化或被遮挡的环境中运行时，朝人类呼救者导航是一项重要能力。现有方法依赖视觉或经典规划器，难以在声学观测不确定、定位不精确的条件下有效逼近人声源，因此需要一种能将不确定声学观测转化为可靠导航行为的策略。

Method: 提出AcousticDiffusion：语义条件化、音频引导的扩散策略。(1) 冻结的预训练音频识别器处理10.24 s窗口，通过语音门控与困境感知优先级将识别输出转为源级导航角色；(2) 麦克风阵列DOA测量递归集成到机器人中心贝叶斯鸟瞰图（BEV）信念场，并经自我运动补偿对齐连续观测，逐步约束源位置、保留由方位引起的距离不确定性；(3) 语义信念、近期声学观测、音频特征与机器人状态共同条件化扩散模型，生成航点轨迹。

Result: 在使用录制音频的合成导航验证集上：平均终点方位误差11.20°，91.78%轨迹与呼救者方位偏差在30°内；干扰源抑制率89.20%–98.99%；在91.07%的窗口中策略优先选择标记为HELP的呼救者而非竞争说话人。在ZSL-1四足机器人上在线部署且无需额外再训练：平均方位误差64.9°，优于A*的98.2°与RRT的90.4°；平均规划计算时间6.07 ms。尽管声学定位不完美，平均最终源距从经典规划器（使用ODAS引导）的3.96 m降至2.48 m，改善37.4%。

Conclusion: 结果表明该框架能够将不确定的声学观测转化为朝人类呼救者更接近的接近行为，在仿真与真实四足平台上均验证了音频引导扩散策略用于人导向导航的有效性与实时性。

Abstract: Navigating toward human callers is an important capability for rescue robots operating where visual contact is degraded or occluded. We present AcousticDiffusion, a semantically conditioned, audio-guided diffusion policy for human-directed navigation. A frozen pretrained audio recognizer processes 10.24 s windows, with speech gating and distress-aware prioritization converting recognition outputs into source-level navigation roles. Microphone-array direction-of-arrival measurements are recursively integrated into a robot-centric Bayesian bird's-eye-view belief field. Ego-motion compensation aligns successive observations, progressively constraining source position while preserving bearing-induced range uncertainty. The semantic belief, recent acoustic observations, audio features, and robot state condition a diffusion model that generates waypoint trajectories. On a synthetic-navigation validation set using recorded audio, AcousticDiffusion achieves a mean end-point bearing error of 11.20 degrees, with 91.78% of trajectories aligned within 30 degrees of the caller. Distractor rejection ranges from 89.20% to 98.99%, and the policy favors a HELP-designated caller over a competing speaker in 91.07% of windows. Deployed online on a ZSL-1 quadruped without additional retraining, it achieves a mean bearing error of 64.9 degrees, compared with 98.2 degrees for A* and 90.4 degrees for RRT, with a mean planner compute time of 6.07 ms. Despite imperfect acoustic localization, the reported mean final source distance is reduced from 3.96 m for the classical planners using ODAS-derived (Open embedded Audition System) guidance to 2.48 m, a 37.4% improvement. These results demonstrate the framework's ability to translate uncertain acoustic observations into closer approaches to human callers.

</details>


### [85] [Contact-Rich Motion Planning via GPU-Parallel Mode Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21803)
*Jiayun Li,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoMET，将GPU并行轨迹评估与贪心接触模式扩展相结合，重新审视接触丰富运动规划中的宽泛接触模式评估，在平面推动与双手非抓取操作任务上取得与基线竞争甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富运动规划因组合式的接触决策而计算困难。现有方法通常通过搜索启发式或优化重构来避免对接触模式序列的宽泛评估，作者希望借助现代GPU硬件重新审视这种宽泛评估的可行性。

Method: 提出Contact-Mode Expansion with parallel Trajectory optimization (CoMET)，将GPU并行轨迹评估与贪心接触模式扩展相结合；并进行了消融实验以分析性能来源。

Result: 在平面推动基准上，CoMET在解质量与规划时间上与基于优化、采样和树搜索的基线相当，在几乎所有实例上匹配完全枚举参考，且评估次数更少、规划时间更短。消融表明性能提升很大程度来自高吞吐轨迹评估器。在双手非抓取操作中，随模式空间增大，GPU友好的局部模式扩展比所测试的自适应树搜索具有更高的规划成功率。

Conclusion: 宽泛的显式模式评估为接触丰富运动规划提供了一种简单而有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich motion planning (CRMP) is essential for robotic manipulation and locomotion, yet remains computationally challenging due to combinatorial contact decisions. Existing methods typically avoid broad evaluation of contact-mode sequences through search heuristics or optimization reformulations. We revisit broad evaluation in light of modern GPU hardware and introduce Contact-Mode Expansion with parallel Trajectory optimization (CoMET), which combines GPU-parallel trajectory evaluation with greedy contact-mode expansion. On planar pushing benchmarks, CoMET is competitive with optimization-based, sampling, and tree-search baselines in solution quality and planning time, matching the full-enumeration reference on nearly all instances with fewer evaluations and shorter planning times. Ablations suggest that much of the performance gain comes from the high-throughput trajectory evaluator. In bimanual nonprehensile manipulation, GPU-friendly local mode expansion achieves higher planning success than the tested adaptive tree search as the mode space grows. These results demonstrate that broad explicit mode evaluation provides a simple yet effective alternative for CRMP.

</details>


### [86] [A Sim-to-Real Integration Pipeline for Training and Deployment of Chunk-Based VLA Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21817)
*Mathilde Kappel,Clémence Grislain,Mohamed Chetouani,Olivier Sigaud,Louis Annabi,Faïz Ben Amar,Stéphane Doncieux,Mahdi Khoramshahi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开源sim-to-real实验协议，通过在真实Franka FR3上开环回放仿真专家轨迹来采集真实视觉和本体感觉观测，并将其转换为兼容VLA训练的格式，同时复用同一部署栈进行闭环策略评估，从而降低数据采集成本并直接量化sim-to-real差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型通常在末端执行器位姿空间预测动作块序列，其训练与评估依赖大规模真实世界演示数据。真实硬件上的数据采集多依赖人类遥操作，成本高、耗时长且难以规模化，构成主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种开源sim-to-real实验协议：在仿真中生成专家轨迹，随后在真实Franka FR3装置上开环回放，记录对应的真实视觉与本体感觉观测，并转换为与VLA训练兼容的格式；同一部署栈在闭环模式下复用于评估已训练策略，使数据采集与评估共享一致的硬件配置。

Result: 由于每条真实记录都与其对应的仿真轨迹配对，该协议可直接测量sim-to-real差距；采集的数据集发布于Hugging Face，管线源代码公开于指定GitLab链接。

Conclusion: 该协议通过复用仿真专家轨迹与统一硬件部署栈，缓解了真实数据采集的规模化瓶颈，并为VLA训练、评估及sim-to-real差距分析提供了标准化、可复现的开源方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a prominent paradigm for mapping multimodal inputs, including semantic instructions, visual observations of the scene, and proprioceptive observations, to robot actions. Most state-of-the-art models predict actions in the end-effector pose space as sequences of action chunks. Training and evaluating these models requires large-scale collections of real-world demonstrations, pairing robot actions with the corresponding visual and proprioceptive observations. Collecting such data on real hardware typically relies on human teleoperation, making the process costly, time-consuming, and difficult to scale. We present an open-source sim-to-real experimental protocol that addresses this bottleneck: expert trajectories generated in simulation are replayed open-loop on a real Franka FR3 setup, where the corresponding real visual and proprioceptive observations are recorded and converted into a format compatible with VLA training. The same deployment stack is then reused, in closed-loop, to evaluate a trained policy on that setup, so that data collection and evaluation share an identical hardware configuration. Because each real recording is paired with the simulated trajectory that produced it, the protocol also yields a direct measurement of the sim-to-real gap. We release the collected datasets on Hugging Face together with the pipeline source code https://gitlab.isir.upmc.fr/kappel/sim2real_public_chunk_control.

</details>


### [87] [LunaDrive: A Delay-Compensated High-Voltage GaN FET-Based Motor Driver for Dynamic Robots with Flat BLDC Motors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21818)
*Sota Yuzaki,Temma Suzuki,Hiromi Tada,Masanori Konishi,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种面向高功率扁平BLDC电机的GaN FET驱动器，可在96 V下实现30 A连续电流、80 A峰值电流及3110 Hz最高电频率，并验证了超额定电压高速区延迟补偿效果及动态机器人应用可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态机器人需要快速能量生成，而商用伺服电机工作电压多为48 V以下，限制了最高转速，因此期望通过超额定电压驱动提升瞬时最大速度。但电机驱动器的半导体器件存在耐压与电流容量的权衡：传统Si MOSFET驱动器难以同时实现高压大电流，GaN FET虽具潜力，但可安装于扁平BLDC电机后侧的紧凑型驱动器仍有限。

Method: 采用GaN FET设计适用于高功率扁平BLDC电机的紧凑型电机驱动器，并研究超出额定工作范围的高速区延迟补偿方法。实验在96 V工作条件下进行，加装散热片测量连续电流，测试峰值电流与最高电频率，并使用24S LiPo电池（100 V）开展高速举升负载实验。

Result: 在96 V工作、加装散热片条件下实现了30 A连续电流；确认峰值电流达80 A，最高电频率为3110 Hz；24S LiPo电池（100 V）驱动的高速负载举升实验成功，展示了在动态机器人操作中的适用性。

Conclusion: 基于GaN FET的紧凑型驱动器可突破商用伺服电机的电压与转速限制，同时兼顾高压与大电流，并通过延迟补偿改善超额定高速区性能，满足动态机器人对高瞬时速度与快速能量输出的需求。

Abstract: The performance improvement of high-power flat BLDC motors has accelerated the development of dynamic robots. However, many commercially available servo motors assume operating voltages of 48 V or lower, which limits the maximum rotational speed. Dynamic robots require rapid energy generation, so this voltage constraint restricts motion performance. Therefore, driving motors beyond the rated voltage is desirable to increase the instantaneous maximum speed. On the other hand, semiconductor devices used in motor drivers have a trade-off between voltage rating and current capacity. Conventional drivers using Si MOSFETs have difficulty achieving both high-voltage and high-current operation. Although GaN FETs are promising, compact drivers that can be mounted on the rear side of flat BLDC motors remain limited. In this study, a motor driver for high-power flat BLDC motors using GaN FETs is developed. The effect of delay compensation in the high-speed region beyond the rated operating range is also investigated. In the experiments, under 96 V operation, a continuous current of 30 A was achieved with a heat sink attached. A peak current of 80 A and a maximum electrical frequency of 3110 Hz were confirmed. A high-speed load lifting experiment driven by a LiPo battery 24S (100 V) was also conducted, demonstrating applicability to dynamic robot operation.

</details>


### [88] [PopNavShift: Stress-Testing Social Navigation under Behavioral Population Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21838)
*Kaizhen Tan,Diyu Zheng,Tim Guangyu Wu,ChengHe Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PopNavShift 匹配仿真框架，用 600 条合成人格记录生成人群条件下的行人运动参数，在 8 种人群条件、7,488 次匹配机器人运行中对比三类社交导航策略，发现仅改变时间压力即可显著改变策略排名，且对行人负担的影响敏感度高于机器人行程时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交导航算法通常在固定的行人行为分布下评估，但行人对机器人的反应在个体和社会情境上存在显著差异；固定分布评估可能掩盖策略在人群变化下的性能不稳定与排序反转问题。

Method: PopNavShift 为匹配仿真框架：以 MatrAIx Persona 1M 中 600 条合成人格记录为提示，构造人群条件下的行人运动轮廓，并确定性地映射为有界运动参数；随后在 8 种人群条件下对反应式避让、提前让行、互惠碰撞避免三类代表性策略进行 7,488 次匹配机器人运行比较。此外对同一 202 个虚拟人格做匹配干预，仅改变时间压力，并在第二种行人动力学模型下复现验证。

Result: 在匹配干预中，仅改变时间压力即导致：以机器人行程时间为准则时 8.6% 的控制器排名发生反转，以平均行人延误为准则时反转 22.4%，以最差十分位延误为准则时反转 23.9%。跨人群条件，这种敏感性对行人负担高于对机器人行程时间，且在空间受限场景中增强；同一质性模式在第二套行人动力学模型下依然成立。

Conclusion: 社交导航策略的评估应覆盖不同行为人群，并同时采用机器人性能与行人负担两类指标，以避免因人群分布变化带来的策略排序不稳定与隐性代价。

Abstract: Social-navigation algorithms are often evaluated under a fixed pedestrian-behavior distribution, despite substantial variation in pedestrian responses to robots across individuals and social contexts. We introduce PopNavShift, a matched simulation framework for stress-testing social-navigation strategies under pedestrian population shifts. PopNavShift constructs population-conditioned pedestrian motion profiles by prompting Gemini 3.7 Flash with 600 synthetic persona records from MatrAIx Persona 1M and deterministically mapping the responses into bounded motion parameters. It then compares three representative navigation strategies, reactive avoidance, early yielding, and reciprocal collision avoidance, across eight population conditions and 7,488 matched robot runs. In a matched intervention on the same 202 personas, changing only time pressure reverses 8.6% of controller rankings based on robot travel time, but 22.4% based on mean pedestrian delay and 23.9% based on worst-decile delay. Across population conditions, this sensitivity is greater for pedestrian burden than for robot travel time and increases in spatially constrained settings; the same qualitative pattern persists under a second pedestrian dynamics model. These findings support evaluating navigation strategies across behavioral populations using both robot performance and pedestrian burden.

</details>


### [89] [VIRGA: Virtual-Agent-Intermediated Riemannian Geometry for Active-Sensing Air-Ground Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21883)
*Fenghe Guo,Runjie Shen,Chenyang Sun,Junrui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VIRGA框架，通过将双LiDAR观测转化为有界黎曼场并用虚拟智能体进行弹性耦合，实现无人机-无人地面车在保持云台LiDAR持续可观测约束下的神经几何协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地自主系统在UAV需持续被UGV云台LiDAR观测时难度显著增加：平台须在闭环中同时应对动态障碍规避、异构运动协调、有限感知以及任务主导权切换。

Method: VIRGA将双LiDAR观测转为有界、来源特定的黎曼场，通过带相互弹性反馈的虚拟智能体耦合这些场；再由平台感知执行映射，将共享协同参考转化为可行的UAV、UGV与云台指令，并施加主动观测安全保障。

Result: 与三种基线对比：Ray-RMP响应最快但空地耦合任务中净空不足；稠密解析黎曼场几何规避更好但评估成本高，无法稳定维持视场；ColAG延迟最低但仍存在安全与可观测性违规。VIRGA安全完成全部成对仓库条件，并在未重训练的长距离洞穴压力测试中于不规则受限几何下保持持续协同。消融实验验证了在线几何评估、虚拟智能体中介与相互反馈的贡献。

Conclusion: VIRGA在保持主动观测约束下实现了安全且可泛化的空地协同，其关键组件对性能均有实质贡献。

Abstract: Air-ground autonomy becomes harder when the unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) must remain observable by a gimbal light detection and ranging (LiDAR) mounted on the unmanned ground vehicle (UGV). The platforms must avoid dynamic obstacles while coordinating heterogeneous motion, limited sensing, and changing task initiative within one closed loop. This paper presents VIRGA, a neural geometric coordination framework that turns dual-LiDAR observations into bounded source-specific Riemannian fields and couples them through a virtual agent with reciprocal elastic feedback. Platform-aware execution maps convert the shared coordination reference into feasible UAV, UGV, and gimbal commands while enforcing active-observation safeguards. Evaluation against three complementary baselines reveals distinct limitations. An adapted Ray-RMP controller provides the fastest Riemannian response but produces insufficient clearance in the coupled air-ground task. A dense analytical Riemannian field improves geometric avoidance, yet its high evaluation cost prevents stable field-of-view maintenance. An adapted ColAG controller achieves the lowest latency but still incurs safety and observability violations. VIRGA completes all paired warehouse conditions safely, while a long-range cave stress test without retraining demonstrates sustained coordination in irregular and confined geometry. Ablations confirm contributions from online geometric evaluation, virtual-agent mediation, and reciprocal feedback.

</details>


### [90] [CommitFlow: Semantic Commitment Verification and Local Correction for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21908)
*Zixiang Zhao,Yansong Feng,Yang Yang,Chaoyu Wang,Haoran Xiao,Hui Zhang,Chuang Cheng,Jianjun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CommitFlow 闭环执行框架，通过语义承诺监控与局部修正，在冻结基座策略前提下解决长程 VLA 执行中语义承诺与实际物理状态不匹配的问题，在 RoboTwin 2.0 十项任务上平均成功率 75.9%，较 pi0.5 提升 22.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽发展迅速，但长程执行中可能在所需物理效果尚未建立时就推进到下一任务阶段。作者将这一现象定义为"语义承诺（阶段必须建立或维持的物理条件）与实际物理状态之间的不匹配"。由于仅凭动作指令无法确认该条件是否满足，局部偏差会逐步传播并导致任务失败，因此需要在执行层引入闭环验证与纠偏。

Method: CommitFlow 是一个闭环执行框架，将承诺监控与局部修正结合，且保持基座策略冻结。框架包含三个组件：(1) Semantic Commitment Monitor（SCM）：将阶段需求与当前状态证据进行比对，当所需条件未满足或被破坏时，抑制依赖该条件的后续动作；(2) BoundaryFlow：以当前状态和基座动作为条件生成局部修正；(3) Relation and Gain Calibration（RGC）：选择满足相关约束的最小修正强度，从而在纠偏的同时避免过度干预。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 的十个常见基准任务上，CommitFlow 取得 75.9% 的平均成功率，相较基座策略 pi0.5 提升 22.7%。跨策略实验显示性能提升具有一致性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在执行层引入语义承诺监控与轻量局部修正，可在不重新训练基座策略的情况下显著提升长程执行的可靠性，为可靠的机器人长程任务执行提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Although vision-language-action (VLA) policies have advanced rapidly, long-horizon execution may still progress to the next task stage before the required physical effect has been established. We call this a mismatch between semantic commitments, physical conditions that a stage must establish or maintain, and the actual physical state. Because an action command alone cannot confirm such a condition, local deviations can propagate and cause task failure. To address this problem, we present CommitFlow, a closed-loop execution framework that combines commitment monitoring with local correction while keeping the base policy frozen. CommitFlow integrates three components. A Semantic Commitment Monitor (SCM) compares stage requirements against current state evidence and holds back dependent actions when a required condition is unmet or violated. BoundaryFlow then generates a local correction conditioned on the current state and base action, and Relation and Gain Calibration (RGC) selects the smallest correction strength that satisfies the relevant constraints. Across the ten common RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark tasks, CommitFlow achieves a mean success rate of 75.9 percent, improving on the base policy pi0.5 by 22.7 percent. Cross-policy experiments show consistent gains, pointing toward reliable long-horizon robot execution.

</details>


### [91] [MAAP: Multi-Agent Active Perception for Collaborative Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21929)
*Bruno N. Y. Chen,Li Kang,Heng Zhou,Xiufeng Song,Zhemeng Zhang,Jiahua Ma,Yiran Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MAAP与RAIL，将每个机械臂的腕部相机视为团队移动视角，实现"协作操作即主动感知"，多任务成功率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体操作中自然会因每条机械臂携带腕部相机且在执行动作时移动，而产生多个任务驱动的视角，但这些观测通常未被充分利用；主动感知在操作领域仍常被视作需要专门配置一个感知智能体，而非由操作本身承担。

Method: MAAP（Multi-Agent Active Perception）：每条机械臂双重用途，既执行操作动作，又通过其腕部相机充当团队的移动视角。RAIL（Role-Aware Imitation Learning）：控制器在预测每条机械臂动作块的同时预测其当前角色，并以该角色为条件生成动作，在单一网络中表示依赖角色的动作。

Result: 四个仿真任务中，扩大感知范围使平均成功率从固定相机的56.5%提升至单一主动腕部视角的62.5%、全部腕部视角的70.0%，MAAP+RAIL达79.2%。RAIL的额外增益集中在三臂Microwave任务上，在相同多腕视角输入下成功率从47%升至82%。在双臂平台上，MAAP+RAIL在20次放置试验中成功14次，而固定视角ACT为0/20。

Conclusion: 协作操作本身即可作为一种主动感知机制，无需专门的感知智能体。

Abstract: Multi-agent manipulation naturally produces multiple task-driven viewpoints: every arm carries a wrist camera and moves through the scene while acting. Yet these observations are typically underutilized, and active perception in manipulation is still often treated as requiring a dedicated sensing agent. We introduce MAAP (Multi-Agent Active Perception), in which every arm is dual-purpose: it executes manipulation actions and, through the wrist camera it carries, simultaneously serves as a moving viewpoint for the team. We pair this with RAIL (Role-Aware Imitation Learning), a controller that predicts each arm's current role alongside its action chunk and conditions action generation on it, representing role-dependent actions within one network. Across four simulated tasks, widening the perception regime lifts average success from 56.5% with a fixed camera to 62.5% with one active wrist view and 70.0% with all of them, while MAAP+RAIL reaches 79.2%. RAIL's additional gain is concentrated on the three-arm Microwave task, where success rises from 47% to 82% on identical multi-wrist inputs. On a dual-arm platform, MAAP+RAIL succeeds in 14 of 20 placement trials compared with 0 of 20 for fixed-view ACT. Collaborative manipulation can thus serve as an active perception mechanism in its own right.

</details>


### [92] [When Should a Failing Robot Ask? Initiating Corrective Human-Robot Dialogue from Audited Sensor Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21942)
*Eshika Pathak,Leela Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文构建了故障原因已知的仿真基准，量化各传感器的诊断信息量，并测试六个开源视觉-语言模型在"自救/查传感器/询问人类"三动作决策中的表现，发现模型行为由提示表层形式驱动而非证据，但当提供力数据文本后部分模型诊断可超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在任务失败后需在"自行诊断、调用其他机载传感器、打断人类询问"之间抉择，而正确选择取决于传感器对故障原因的可辨识度及机器人自身诊断可靠性。现有VLM行为是否依赖真实证据尚不明确，需在真因已知的受控环境下测量。

Method: 构建仿真基准，通过注入故障使每个失败的真因已知，并带显式数据泄漏检查地测量每种传感器揭示的信息量；随后在多种提示变体（含/不含示例、选项顺序变动）下测试六个开源视觉-语言模型；将同一批模型给予十行文本形式的力数据；并把抉择形式化为三动作决策问题，其最优策略由实测准确率推导。

Result: 部分故障可由相机图像诊断，另一些仅能由力数据诊断（力数据达0.99，无任何图像方法超过0.55）。模型行为跟随提示表层而非证据：把拒绝选项从末位移至首位，使六对模型-家族中的三对拒绝率从78–100%骤降至0–6%。各提示变体下基于帧的准确率均不超过多数类基线，陈述置信度与正确性无关。以十行文本提供力数据后，六模型中的四个首次出现超基线诊断，说明失败多源于缺失传感器数据而非能力不足。模型不遵循最优策略，询问率对提问成本四倍变化不敏感；但单次向人类提问可将其从基线提升至接近回答者自身可靠度（提问时为0.70–0.81）。

Conclusion: 是否求助应绑定于实测准确率与明示成本，而非模型自身的置信度；模型当前的求助决策与证据、成本脱节，其大部分失败可归因于传感器数据缺失。

Abstract: A robot that fails at a task faces the first decision in corrective dialogue: act on its own diagnosis, consult another onboard sensor, or interrupt a person. Choosing well requires knowing how much the robot's sensors reveal about the cause and how reliable the robot's own diagnosis is. We build a simulated benchmark in which every failure's true cause is known, because we injected it, and measure what each sensor reveals, with explicit checks against data leakage. Some failures are diagnosable from camera images; others only from the robot's force data (0.99 from force data, no image method above 0.55). We then test six open vision-language models. Their behavior tracks the surface of the prompt, not the evidence: moving the refusal option from last to first in the answer list collapses refusal rates from 78-100% to 0-6% in three of the six swept model-and-family pairs. Accuracy from frames stays at or below a majority-class baseline under every prompt variant, with or without worked examples, and stated confidence carries no information about correctness. Handing the same models the force data as ten lines of text produces the first above-baseline diagnoses, in four of the six models: much of the failure reflects missing sensor data, not missing ability. We pose the choice as a three-action decision problem, act, consult your own sensors, or ask a human, whose optimal policy follows from measured accuracy. The models do not follow it, and their ask rates ignore a fourfold change in question cost. One question to a human still lifts them from that baseline to roughly the answerer's own reliability (0.70-0.81 when they ask). The decision to ask should be tied to measured accuracy and stated costs, not to the model's confidence.

</details>


### [93] [GALA: Geometry-Aware Latent Action Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Model Pretraining across Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21948)
*Yichen Liu,Puzhen Yuan,Xiang Zhu,Yanjiang Guo,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GALA：几何感知潜在动作建模框架，通过融合图像级潜在动作与基于UEMR的细粒度末端执行器几何动作，实现跨本体VLA预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多本体数据预训练VLA面临末端执行器动作空间异构问题。现有基于图像的潜在动作模型（LAM）虽能学习本体无关的动作表示，但难以捕捉细粒度末端执行器关节运动，尤其是人手与灵巧手中的手指级几何变化。

Method: 提出GALA框架，在图像潜在动作基础上引入3D末端执行器几何运动；设计统一末端执行器运动表示（UEMR），在保留细粒度运动信息的同时提升跨本体泛化性。GALA联合使用捕捉场景级动态的视觉潜在动作与捕捉共享细粒度末端关节运动的几何潜在动作，为包含无动作自中心人类视频的多本体数据提供有效VLA预训练监督。

Result: 在细粒度运动探测、跨本体检索与下游VLA评测中验证有效性，取得68.3%的RoboCasa-GR1成功率与75.5%的真实世界成功率。

Conclusion: GALA能跨本体建模可泛化的细粒度运动，为多本体VLA预训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Learning large-scale vision-language-action (VLA) models from multi-embodiment datasets remains challenging due to heterogeneous action spaces across end effectors. Although latent action models (LAMs) can learn embodiment-agnostic action representations from diverse video data, existing image-based LAMs often fail to capture fine-grained end-effector articulation, particularly finger-level geometric changes in human and dexterous robot hands. To address this limitation, we propose GALA, a Geometry-Aware Latent-Action modeling framework that augments image-based latent actions with 3D end-effector geometric motion. However, naively incorporating point clouds yields fine-grained action representations with limited shared semantics, hindering cross-embodiment pretraining. To address this issue, we introduce the Unified End-effector Motion Representation (UEMR), which preserves fine-grained motion information while improving the cross-embodiment generalizability of latent actions. Building upon UEMR, GALA combines visual latent actions that capture scene-level dynamics with geometric latent actions that capture shared fine-grained end-effector articulation, providing effective supervision for VLA pretraining from multi-embodiment data, including action-free ego-centric human videos. Experiments on fine-grained motion probing, cross-embodiment retrieval, and downstream VLA evaluation demonstrate GALA's effectiveness in modeling generalizable fine-grained motions across embodiments, achieving 68.3% RoboCasa-GR1 success rate and 75.5% real-world success rate. Code, appendix, and demos are available at https://puzhenyuan.github.io/GALA-website/.

</details>


### [94] [CARF: Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21982)
*Shuqi Zhao,Bang Du,Cheng-En Wu,Yichen Xie,Yixiao Wang,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CARF框架，通过对比式吸引-排斥机制利用机器人不完美数据：模仿推进性片段，规避导致失败的关键片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示采集常包含失败轨迹，现有方法仅利用其中仍推进任务完成的片段，忽视了直接导致失败的关键行为，未能全面利用不完美数据。

Method: CARF引入基于进展的重要性评分器，仅用成功专家演示及其扰动结果训练，估计每步对任务完成的贡献并识别失败轨迹中的信息区域；评分指导统一的流匹配目标，吸引策略趋向推进行为、排斥失败关键行为，同时排除模糊片段。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验在多种失败场景下一致优于对比基线，消融实验验证了评分与吸引-排斥机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 推进性与失败关键片段提供非对称监督，CARF通过吸引-排斥统一目标更全面利用不完美数据，避免模糊失败片段带来的不可靠监督。

Abstract: Robot demonstration collection often produces imperfect or failed trajectories in addition to successful demonstrations. Existing methods typically exploit failed trajectories by identifying segments that still make progress toward task completion, but largely overlook \textit{failure-critical behaviors} that directly lead to task failure. Here we argue that these two types of segments provide fundamentally asymmetric supervision: progressive segments should be imitated, whereas failure-critical segments should be explicitly avoided. Based on this observation, we propose CARF, a Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-guided framework for learning from imperfect robot data. CARF introduces a progress-based importance scorer, trained solely on successful expert demonstrations and its perturbation results, to estimate step-wise contributions toward task completion and identify informative regions in failed trajectories. These scores guide a unified flow-matching objective that attracts the policy toward progressive behaviors and repels it from failure-critical ones, while excluding ambiguous segments. This enables more comprehensive utilization of imperfect data and avoids unreliable supervision from ambiguous failure segments. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate consistent improvements over competing baselines across diverse failure scenarios, with ablations further validating the effectiveness of the proposed scoring and attraction-repulsion mechanisms. Our website is https://zhao-sq.github.io/carf/#.

</details>


### [95] [SkelWAM: A Skeleton-Guided World-Action Model for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21983)
*Pengjun Niu,Yujia Xie,Rui Peng,Hang Zhao,Ke Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SkelWAM，用统一的骨骼几何表示连接感知与控制，实现单源跨形态操控迁移，无需目标域演示或策略更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨机器人形态迁移操控经验对扩展机器人学习、减少重复的任务专用数据采集至关重要，但形态差异会改变视觉外观、动作维度与语义，以及可实现同一工具位姿的全身构型。

Method: 构建由手臂中心线几何、工具中心点位姿与平行夹爪指令组成的共享25维状态，作为第三人称与腕部观测及未来全身动作目标的统一定义；用预测性视觉监督训练视频-动作混合Transformer预测规范骨骼动作块，再由形态特定的约束解码器转为关节式或连续体机器人控制，无需关节一一对应。

Result: 在LIBERO-Cross10（10任务、10目标形态、4类形态组）源域专属迁移基准上，Franka训练的策略在1000条episode上达43.3%成功率，超最佳基线36.2个百分点；并将JAKA mini2训练的策略部署到Feagine A03连续体机器人完成3项桌面任务。

Conclusion: 显式骨骼几何表示可有效桥接跨形态的感知与控制，实现零目标域演示、零目标策略更新的跨形态迁移，并在仿真基准与真实连续体机器人上验证了潜力。

Abstract: Reusing manipulation experience across robot embodiments is important for scaling robot learning and reducing repeated task-specific data collection. However, changes in embodiment alter visual appearance, action dimensionality and semantics, and the whole-body configurations that can realize the same tool pose. We present SkelWAM, a skeleton-guided world-action model that couples perception and control through one explicit geometric representation for single-source cross-embodiment manipulation. Arm centerline geometry, tool-center-point (TCP) pose, and parallel-jaw commands form a shared 25-D state. The same definition underlies canonical third-person and wrist observations and future whole-body action targets. Trained with predictive visual supervision, a video-action mixture of transformers predicts canonical skeleton action chunks, which embodiment-specific constrained decoders convert into joint or continuum-robot controls. This formulation requires no one-to-one joint correspondence and uses no target-task demonstrations or target policy updates. We introduce LIBERO-Cross10, a source-only cross-embodiment transfer benchmark covering ten tasks and ten target embodiments across four morphological groups. On this benchmark, Franka-trained SkelWAM achieves 43.3% success over 1,000 episodes, exceeding the best-performing evaluated baseline by 36.2 percentage points. We further deploy a JAKA mini2-trained policy on the Feagine A03 continuum robot for three tabletop manipulation tasks, illustrating the approach's potential for real-world cross-embodiment manipulation. Project page: http://www.liukepku.com/skelwam/index.html

</details>


### [96] [Gripper-Aware Automatic Dense Packing of Irregular Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22062)
*Tianhao Qin,Connor McCann,Berk Calli,Jing Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出感知-抓取器感知规划-力控执行的闭环密集堆叠流水线，在真实机械臂上实现不规则物体自动装箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓储作业亟需自动密集堆叠，但现有不规则物体堆叠研究多限于理想化接触的仿真，将物体视为孤立刚体，抓取器仅作离散的事后可行性检查，且未处理执行中累积的感知与接触漂移。

Method: 将物体与抓取器统一表示为分层球树复合体；在GPU上以CMA-ES在五自由度空间中搜索放置姿态，竖直坐标基于当前高度图解析确定；执行阶段采用力监控竖直下降，首次接触即停止；释放后施加整合推压以消除抓取器感知规划遗留的横向间隙；每次放置后重新感知容器以避免漂移累积。

Result: 在Franka Emika Panda机器人上完成对3D打印的平面、曲面、凹面物体集及YCB物体子集的堆叠验证；消融实验分离了抓取器感知优化、整合推压与网格派生几何对端到端成功率、堆叠密度和计算成本的影响；并与高度图最小化方法进行对比基准。

Conclusion: 该闭环系统在真实机械臂上实现了抓取器感知的不规则物体密集堆叠，并验证了各关键组件的贡献。

Abstract: Automatic dense packing is widely desired in warehouse operations but remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation. Existing work on irregular-object packing largely targets simulation with idealized contact, treating the object as an isolated rigid body. The gripper often enters as a discrete, post-hoc feasibility check, if considered at all, and the perception and contact drift accumulated during execution are not addressed. We present a closed-loop pipeline that integrates perception, gripper-aware placement optimization, and force-guided execution on a real manipulator. The optimizer represents the object together with the gripper as a single composite body of hierarchical sphere trees. It searches over five degrees of freedom on a GPU within a CMA-ES framework, with the vertical coordinate grounded analytically against the current heightmap. During execution, a force-monitored vertical descent stops on first contact. A post-release consolidation push then closes the residual lateral clearance that gripper-aware planning leaves behind. The container is re-perceived between placements so that drift does not accumulate. We validate the system on a Franka Emika Panda robot packing a 3D-printed set of flat, curved, and concave objects, and a YCB object subset. An ablation study isolates the contribution of gripper-aware optimization, the consolidation push, and mesh-derived geometry to end-to-end success, achieved density, and computational cost. We further benchmark against the heightmap-minimization method as a baseline representative of prior irregular-object packing work.

</details>


### [97] [Duty Factor Predicts Robust Constrained Quadrupedal Locomotion Across Gait Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22073)
*James Zhu,David Ologan,George Ortiz,Thomas Chun Fai Lee,Selvin Garcia Gonzalez,Ardalan Tajbakhsh,Pinhas Ben-Tzvi,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过三类四足运动控制方法（全身轨迹优化+LQR、学习型控制器、质心模型预测控制）研究步态参数关系，发现占空比（duty factor）比名义步态类型更能预测局部误差收敛，并可作为调节窄地形鲁棒性的低维参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人需在扰动和受限地形下保持运动鲁棒性。传统上用"步态类型"（walking/trotting）刻画运动，但同一步态内占空比、速度、步宽等参数仍可变，步态类型无法唯一确定运动特性，因此需要找到更具预测性和可调节性的参数。

Method: 采用三种不同的四足运动控制方法进行对比研究：(1) 全身轨迹优化配合LQR反馈；(2) 学习型运动控制器，研究窄地形下的占空比选择；(3) 质心模型预测控制框架，并在实体四足机器人上进行窄地形实验验证。

Result: 占空比是比名义步态类型更强的局部误差收敛预测指标；占空比可作为学习控制器中适应窄地形鲁棒性的低维参数；上述趋势在质心MPC框架下依然成立，并获实体机器人窄地形实验验证。

Conclusion: 占空比为理解和选择跨步态类型、跨控制架构的鲁棒四足运动提供了简单而有效的基础。

Abstract: Quadrupedal robots are increasingly deployed in environments where locomotion must remain robust to disturbances and constrained terrain. Gait type, such as walking or trotting, is commonly used to characterize quadrupedal locomotion. However, gait type does not uniquely define locomotion, as parameters such as duty factor, speed, and stance width can vary within a single gait type. In this work, we investigate the relationship between these gait parameters using three distinct quadrupedal locomotion control approaches. First, using whole body trajectory optimization with LQR feedback, we show that duty factor is a stronger predictor of local error convergence than nominal gait type. Second, we investigate duty factor selection with a learned locomotion controller, suggesting how duty factor may serve as a low-dimensional parameter for adapting locomotion robustness in narrow-terrain environments. Finally, we show that these trends persist under a centroidal model predictive control framework and validate them through narrow-terrain experiments on a physical quadruped. These results show that duty factor provides a simple and effective basis for understanding and selecting robust quadrupedal locomotion across gait types and control architectures.

</details>


### [98] [LIMBO: Learning and Internalizing Model-Free Barrier Objectives for Agile and Safe Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22075)
*Jake Gonzales,Arturo Flores Alvarez,Yu-Ming Chen,Aaron D. Ames,Lillian J. Ratliff,Manikantan Nambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIMBO 提出一种"先合成状态-动作控制屏障函数(Q-CBF)、再将其安全结构蒸馏进任务策略"的框架，在29自由度人形机器人上实现无在线安全滤波器的全身避障与平衡控制，并可迁移到真实硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身控制需在高维非线性动力学下同时兼顾碰撞避免与平衡，安全证书难以设计且难以在不同行为间复用；同时传统安全滤波在部署时需要在线求解，代价高且不便于复用。

Method: 1) 在冻结的基控制器周围的残差动作空间上，从黑盒转移与基于状态的失败规范中学习安全证书，使 Q-CBF 的合成在完整控制维度上可行，并将证书置于任务策略的控制空间中；2) 合成阶段用学到的安全值驱动"风险引导采样"，聚焦于可恢复性边界附近；3) 任务学习阶段将该安全值作为教师，提供动作级安全反馈以训练鲁棒的任务策略。

Result: 在29自由度人形机器人上完成躲避球避让与低障碍下行走两项任务；相同安全规范下仅改变采样集中度即可得到从下蹲到新颖的"后仰-limbo"式动作等不同策略；所学策略无需在线安全滤波器即可迁移到真实硬件。

Conclusion: 将学习型 Q-CBF 扩展到全身控制是可行的；风险引导的边界采样为探索"可恢复性边缘"提供了有理论依据的方式；安全合成可与任务策略结合并直接部署，表明学习式安全合成能够扩展到敏捷全身控制。

Abstract: Safe whole-body control requires coordinating collision avoidance and balance under high-dimensional, nonlinear dynamics--making safety certificates difficult to design and reuse across behaviors. We present LIMBO, a framework for synthesizing a state-action control barrier function and distilling its safety structure into a task policy. LIMBO learns the safety certificate from black-box transitions and a state-based failure specification over residual actions around a frozen base controller, making Q-CBF synthesis tractable in the full control dimension while placing the certificate in the task policy's control space. During synthesis, the learned safety value drives risk-guided sampling near the estimated boundary of recoverability; during task learning, it serves as a teacher that provides action-level safety feedback, yielding a robust task policy and alleviating the need for an online safety filter at deployment. We demonstrate LIMBO on a 29-degree-of-freedom humanoid performing dodgeball avoidance and locomotion beneath low obstacles. Beyond scaling learned Q-CBFs to whole-body control, we show that risk-guided boundary sampling provides a theoretically grounded way to explore the edge of recoverability. Under the same safety specification, ceteris paribus, varying the sampling concentration produces strategies ranging from crouching to a novel backward-leaning limbo maneuver. In both settings, the learned policies transfer to hardware without online safety filtering, showing that learned safety synthesis scales to agile whole-body control.

</details>


### [99] [SeeQ: Training Generalist Value Functions for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22085)
*Saksham Singh,Zheyuan Hu,Max Sobol Mark,Jeffrey Yu,Zackory Erickson,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SeeQ提出对"当前活跃子任务"学习Q函数，利用离线数据中的子任务标注缩短价值预测时域，并以自回归方式在测试时预测子任务，从而在真实双臂机器人操作任务中显著提升best-of-N策略引导效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在复杂长时程任务上表现脆弱，这类任务包含多个阶段或需在同一阶段反复尝试与推理。Q值函数可通过排序候选动作或引导策略改进来提升策略，但从稀疏任务级奖励学习面临信用分配时域长、Bellman备份困难、数据覆盖要求高等问题。因此需要一种能缩短信用分配时域、降低学习难度的价值学习范式。

Method: 提出SeeQ（Subtask-elicited Q-functions），即针对当前活跃子任务学习Q值，以缩短价值预测时域，使其可用时序差分（TD）目标有效学习。训练时利用离线机器人数据中已有的子任务级标注作为分解依据，从而可从广泛且可能次优的机器人数据集中学习。为免除测试时的人工标注或模块化子任务预测系统，Q函数架构被训练为在估计子任务价值前，先以自然语言自回归地预测当前活跃子任务。实现上基于视觉-语言骨干网络，先在多样化开源机器人操作数据上预训练，再在下游任务上微调。

Result: 在两个双臂机器人平台上的四个真实世界操作任务中，SeeQ价值函数大幅提升了best-of-N策略引导的性能。

Conclusion: 以子任务为单位的Q函数学习能有效缓解长时程任务的信用分配与Bellman备份难题，并借助离线数据标注与自回归子任务预测，实现无需人工标注的测试时部署，为提升通用机器人策略在复杂长时程任务上的鲁棒性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, generalist robot policies remain brittle on complex, long-horizon tasks that comprise multiple stages or require repeated attempts and deliberation on the same underlying stage before success. Q-value functions can improve these policies by ranking candidate actions or guiding policy improvement, but learning from sparse task-level rewards entails long credit-assignment horizons, difficult Bellman backups, and broad data-coverage requirements. We introduce SeeQ (Subtask-elicited Q-functions), which instead learns Q-values for the currently active subtask. This shortens the value-prediction horizon and enables effective learning with temporal-difference (TD) objectives. During training, subtask-level annotations present in offline robot data provide the decomposition and enable learning from broad, potentially suboptimal robot datasets. To eliminate the need for human annotations or modular subtask prediction systems at test time, our Q-function architecture is trained to autoregressively predict the active subtask in natural language before estimating its value. We instantiate SeeQ using a base vision-language backbone, pretrain it on diverse open-source robot manipulation data, and finetune it on downstream tasks. Across four real-world manipulation tasks on two bimanual robot platforms, the SeeQ value function substantially improves best-of-N policy steering.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [On permutation-invariant construction of glued lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20952)
*Maria Fernanda Zordan Bonini,Lenny Fukshansky*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究由置换作用于单个向量轨道张成的格，给出秩上界可达的充分条件，刻画其结构（正交循环块经向量粘合）、行列式公式，并在整数向量情形下联系根格 A_k 与代数整数格，最后强化了关于代数共轭集生成的良舍入格的已有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环格是格理论中的重要类，其自然推广是由置换 τ 作用于单向量轨道张成的置换不变格。此前文献已给出该类格秩的上界，但何时能达到该上界、其内部结构（如何由更小的块组成）以及行列式如何计算尚不清楚。作者希望填补这些空白，并将其与根格、代数整数格及良舍入格等经典对象建立联系。

Method: 设 τ 为 n 个字母上的置换，考察向量 x∈R^n 在坐标置换作用下的轨道所张成的格。通过分析各循环分量对应的正交循环块，应用正交分解与粘合（gluing）思想，推导秩上界可达的充分条件；用循环块的行列式与置换向量的范数写出整体行列式公式。对整数向量 x，将各块识别为根格 A_k 的子格，并研究其粘合直和的可实现性。进一步以多个循环数域的组合构造置换不变的代数整数格；并结合代数共轭集构造，强化已有关于良舍入格的结论。

Result: 给出了使先前秩上界被取到的 x 的充分条件。证明此类置换不变格由置换向量粘合各正交循环块而成，并得到用循环块行列式与置换向量范数表示的行列式公式。当 x 为整数向量时，这些块是相应维数根格 A_k 的子格，根格本身及其粘合直和均可由该构造实现。还构造了由循环数域集合得到的置换不变代数整数格。最后证明了关于代数共轭集生成良舍入格的一个已有结果的加强版本。

Conclusion: 置换不变格构成循环格的自然推广，其秩、结构、行列式可由正交循环块及其粘合完全描述；整数情形下与根格 A_k 紧密相关，并可通过循环数域构造代数整数格。该框架统一并强化了若干已有结果，为研究良舍入格与代数格提供了新工具。

Abstract: Given a permutation $τ$ on $n$ letters, we consider lattices spanned by an orbit of one vector $\boldsymbol x$ in $\mathbb R^n$ under the action of $τ$ by permutation of the coordinates. Such lattices generalize the important class of cyclic lattices and have previously been studied in~\cite{perm}, where a bound on their rank was established. We prove a sufficient condition on $\boldsymbol x$ for this bound to be achieved. We further investigate the structure of such permutation-invariant lattices, proving that they are glued by the permuted vector from the orthogonal cyclic blocks and giving a determinant formula for the lattice in terms of determinants of these blocks and the norm of the permuted vector. In the case $\boldsymbol x$ is an integer vector, these blocks are sublattices of the root lattices $A_k$ in respective dimensions with root lattices themselves and their glued direct sums also realizable by this construction. We also exhibit a glued construction of permutation-invariant algebraic integral lattices from collections of cyclic number fields. Finally, we prove a strengthened version of a previous result of~\cite{lf_ek} on a related construction of well-rounded lattices spanned by sets of algebraic conjugates.

</details>


### [101] [Extreme value laws for intrinsic Diophantine approximation on spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21036)
*Alexander Gorodnik,Zouhair Ouaggag*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究球面 $S^d$ 上的内蕴 Diophantine 逼近，证明当 $d\geq 3$ 且沿足够稀疏的高度序列时，最佳有理逼近质量的计数函数渐近服从 Poisson 分布，极限分布为 Weibull 分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Diophantine 逼近的度量理论（如 Poisson 极限律）已较成熟，但球面等流形上的内蕴（intrinsic）Diophantine 逼近的极限分布问题尚待刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以给定高度处可用的最佳有理逼近的优劣程度作为度量，考察其随高度变化的统计行为；沿足够稀疏的高度序列分析计数函数的渐近性质，利用极值理论与 Poisson 逼近技术推导极限分布。

Result: 当 $d\geq 3$ 时，沿足够稀疏的高度序列，最佳有理逼近的度量具有 Weibull 极限分布，且相应的计数函数渐近 Poisson 分布。

Conclusion: 球面内蕴 Diophantine 逼近的极限统计行为表现出与经典情形相似的 Poisson 型规律，且极限分布为 Weibull 型，推广了相关度量理论结果。

Abstract: We study intrinsic Diophantine approximation on the sphere $S^d$, measured by the quality of the best rational approximation available at a given height. We show that, for $d\geq 3$ and along sufficiently sparse sequences of heights, these quantities have a Weibull limit distribution and the associated counting functions are asymptotically Poissonian.

</details>


### [102] [Eigenvectors of $p$-Curvature for Geometric Difference Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21090)
*Vitaly Tarasov,Alexander Varchenko*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将qKZ型加性离散平坦联络的p-曲率算子本征截面构造归结为有限格Z^r/pZ^r上的Bethe-ansatz型离散超几何和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qKZ型加性离散平坦联络在特征p下的p-曲率算子是一族相互交换的自同构，其本征截面与本征值的显式构造此前缺乏系统方法，作者希望借助Bethe-ansatz思想给出构造性描述。

Method: 提出Bethe-ansatz型构造方法，将p-曲率算子的本征截面表示为有限格Z^r/pZ^r上的离散超几何和，用以类比连续情形下的超几何积分。

Result: 本征截面被构造为有限格Z^r/pZ^r上的离散超几何和，从而给出了p-曲率算子本征值与对应的本征截面。

Conclusion: p-曲率本征截面可由超几何积分的有限离散类比得到，说明Bethe-ansatz型方法适用于特征p下qKZ型离散平坦联络的p-曲率谱问题。

Abstract: A qKZ-type additive discrete flat connection in characteristic $p$ has $p$-curvature operators. They are commuting automorphisms of the connection. We present a Bethe-ansatz-type construction of eigensections and eigenvalues of the $p$-curvature operators. An eigensection of $p$-curvature operators is a discrete hypergeometric sum over the finite lattice $\mathbb{Z}^r/p\mathbb{Z}^r$. Thus, the $p$-curvature eigensections are constructed by a finite, discrete analogue of a hypergeometric integral.

</details>


### [103] [Galois deformation rings and modularity in the residually reducible case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21156)
*Geoffrey Akers*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明某些剩余可约的p进伽罗瓦表示来自模形式，通过伪特征与Selmer群界，证明在Fontaine-Laffaille条件下万有变形环的约化是离散赋值环，并应用于Q(i)上的4维表示模性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究剩余可约的p进伽罗瓦表示何时来自模形式，是模性提升问题的核心。作者希望在不需要自对偶假设的情况下，处理数域上的高维变形，并推广已有变形环结果。

Method: 利用伪特征（pseudocharacters）研究满足在p上方素处Fontaine-Laffaille条件的变形，并计算相关Selmer群的界；证明Bellaïche-Chenevier定义的可约性理想是极大且主的，从而给出约化万有变形环的结构。论证不依赖自对偶假设，主要变形结果适用于n维表示。

Result: 在某些条件下，约化的万有变形环是离散赋值环；可约性理想被证明是极大且主的。应用于Klosin给出的Hermitian模形式同余，证明了虚二次域Q(i)上某些4维p进表示的模性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了一类剩余可约p进伽罗瓦表示可由模形式产生，给出了n维变形环的刚性结构，并通过Hermitian模形式同余得到Q(i)上4维表示的模性结果。

Abstract: We prove some residually reducible $p$-adic Galois representations of number fields arise from modular forms. We study the universal deformation ring arising from deformations satisfying the Fontaine-Laffaille condition at primes over $p$. Under certain conditions, we establish the reduced universal deformation ring is a discrete valuation ring. The method uses pseudocharacters and certain bounds on Selmer groups, where the ideal of reducibility as defined by Bellaïche and Chenevier is shown to be maximal and principal. A self-dual assumption is not needed in our argument. The main result on deformations applies to $n$-dimensional representations. In applications, a congruence between Hermitian modular forms due to Klosin is used with our results to prove modularity of some 4-dimensional $p$-adic representations of the imaginary quadratic field $\mathbf{Q}(i)$.

</details>


### [104] [On The Cyclicity of Algebraic Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21174)
*Maria Fernanda Zordan Bonini,Robson Ricardo de Araujo,Antonio Aparecido de Andrade,Jéfferson Luiz Rocha Bastos*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在循环与准循环格的理论研究上取得进展：给出基本事实、讨论若干著名格的循环性，并在代数框架下通过Minkowski嵌入研究Galois数域中Z-模所诱导的循环格，建立了代数格循环性的充要条件以及理想格循环性的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环格与准循环格在密码学与编码理论中具有重要应用价值，但其代数结构（尤其是在数域与理想格框架下）的循环性判定条件尚不完善，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 方法上，首先整理循环格与准循环格的基本性质并讨论若干典型格的循环性；核心部分在代数设定中展开，利用Minkowski嵌入研究Galois数域中Z-模生成的循环格，通过与之自然关联的群刻画循环性；对理想格则依据该理想在底数域中的分解性质进行分析。

Result: 得到了代数格在循环Galois数域及一般Galois数域上循环的充分必要条件，该条件以自然关联群的形式表述；同时给出了理想格为循环格的充分必要条件，该条件依赖于理想在底数域中的分解。

Conclusion: 本文为循环格与准循环格的代数理论提供了系统的判定工具，将格的循环性问题转化为数域中群结构与理想分解的问题，为后续在密码与编码中的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: This work presents theoretical advances in the study of cyclic and quasi-cyclic lattices. First, we provide elementary facts regarding cyclic and quasi-cyclic lattices, and discuss about the cyclicity of some notable lattices. The main contributions of the paper is in the algebraic setting: we investigate cyclic lattices arising from $\mathbb{Z}$-modules in Galois number fields via the Minkowski embedding. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for an algebraic lattice to be cyclic over both cyclic and general Galois number fields, expressed in terms of naturally associated groups. Moreover, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for ideal lattices to be cyclic, depending on the factorization of the ideal in the underlying number field.

</details>


### [105] [The large sieve for square moduli under Hooley's hypothesis $R^*$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21195)
*Stephan Baier*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在 Hooley 假设 $R^*$ 下, 平方模大筛和在临界点 $N=Q^3$ 取得 $S\ll Q^{1/2-1/134+\varepsilon}N\sum|a_n|^2$, 首次将 Baier–Zhao (2008) 的指数 $1/2$ 降下来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhao 平方模大筛和在临界点 $N=Q^3$ 的最佳无条件界自 Baier–Zhao (2008) 以来一直是 $Q^{1/2+\varepsilon}N\sum|a_n|^2$, 与猜想界 $Q^{\varepsilon}N\sum|a_n|^2$ 相差 $Q^{1/2}$; 该指数半个世纪未被改进。作者旨在借助 Hooley 的短 Salié 和假设 $R^*$ 突破此障碍。

Method: 核心是估计落在 $b/r$ 附近 $Q^{-3}$ 内形如 $a/q^2$ ($q\le Q$) 的分数个数 $P(\alpha)$。作者证明对任意模 $Q^{1/2+\varepsilon}\le r\le Q^{3/2}$ 有 $P(b/r+z)\ll(Q^{2/3}r^{-1/3}+Q^{1/4})Q^\varepsilon$, 改进并推广了 Baier (2026) 仅对 $r=p,p^2$ 的 $Q^{9/16}r^{-1/8}$。关键观察: 区间 $J$ 上的模平方根和 $\sum_{n\in J}e_r(a\sqrt{jn})$ 经完备化及对各模数二次 Gauss 和的精确求值后, 等于 $r^{-1/2}$ 乘以长度 $r/|J|$ 的 Salié 和, 故假设 $R^*$ 直接给出平方根相消, 无需 Weyl 差分。文中完整证明了含偶模数及系数与模数不互素情形的 Gauss 和求值。

Result: 在假设 $R^*$ 下, 于 $N=Q^3$ 得到 $S\ll Q^{1/2-1/134+\varepsilon}N\sum|a_n|^2$, 首次将指数降至 $1/2$ 以下; 同时把 $P(\alpha)$ 的界从部分模数推广到所有模数并显著改进。

Conclusion: 借助 Hooley 假设 $R^*$ 与对各模数 Gauss 和的精确求值, 无需 Weyl 差分即可获得平方根相消, 从而突破平方模大筛和的 $1/2$ 指数壁垒, 其节约恰为 Weyl 差分途径所得之平方。

Abstract: Let $S(Q,M,N,(a_n)):=\sum_{q\le Q}\sum_{(a,q)=1}|\sum_{M<n\le M+N}a_ne(an/q^2)|^2$ be Zhao's large sieve sum with square moduli. At the critical point $N=Q^3$ the best known unconditional bound, due to Baier and Zhao (2008), is $S\ll Q^{1/2+\varepsilon}N\sum |a_n|^2$, against the conjectured $Q^{\varepsilon}N\sum|a_n|^2$, and the exponent $\tfrac12$ has not been lowered since. We prove that, under Hooley's Hypothesis $R^*$ for short Salié sums -- square-root cancellation for $\sum_{x_1<n\le x_2}\big(\tfrac nc\big)e_c(a\bar n+bn)$ over arbitrary subintervals of a period -- one has $S\ll Q^{1/2-1/134+\varepsilon}N\sum|a_n|^2$ at $N=Q^3$. The key estimate is a bound for the number $P(α)$ of fractions $a/q^2$, $q\le Q$, within $Q^{-3}$ of a point $α$ near $b/r$: we show $P(b/r+z)\ll(Q^{2/3}r^{-1/3}+Q^{1/4})Q^\varepsilon$ for every modulus $Q^{1/2+\varepsilon}\le r\le Q^{3/2}$, improving the bound $Q^{9/16}r^{-1/8}$ obtained by Baier (2026) for $r=p,p^2$ only, and reaching every modulus. The proof rests on a single observation: a sum of modular square roots $\sum_{n\in J}e_r(a\sqrt{jn})$ over an interval $J$ is, after completion and an exact evaluation of quadratic Gauss sums at every modulus, $r^{-1/2}$ times a Salié sum of length $r/|J|$. Hypothesis $R^*$ therefore yields square-root cancellation for these sums directly, at every modulus, without Weyl differencing; the saving over the trivial bound is the square of what the Weyl-differencing route gives. The Gauss-sum evaluations, including even moduli and coefficients sharing a factor with the modulus, are proved in full. The paper was prepared in collaboration with Claude (Anthropic); Section 1.9 sets out what each of us contributed.

</details>


### [106] [Annular Frobenius Classification of p-adic Stieltjes--Schwarzian Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21213)
*Mohammadreza Mohajer,Abdellah Sebbar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出p进Schwarzian方程的显式环形正规形式并分类Frobenius结构，核心是四类微分模、平方类判据与环形幂的正规形式及其在可约模方程上的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由developing differentials定义的p进Schwarzian方程所对应的微分模结构，寻求其环形正规形式并判定Frobenius存在性，并将其应用于可约模方程以确定Frobenius存在的最小迭代指数。

Method: 利用Robba环上单位元的降阶（reduction of order）将关联模表示为由剩余决定的unipotent扩张的二次秩一扭；以平方类条件刻画Frobenius存在性；在奇素数处给出四类微分模；在均匀主导单项式假设下给出零剩余轨迹上解析族中的Frobenius公式；对有理非整环形幂得到对角正规形式与奇素数处的同余判据，当单位因子为显式平方时对所有素数成立；最后应用于θ²y−ℓ²E₄y/144=0并与Heine–Stieltjes剩余消没、非退化Jacobi构型联系。
