Result: 奇素数处恰有四种微分模类型；有理非整环形幂的对角正规形式与奇素数同余判据（显式平方单位时对所有素数成立）；对gcd(ℓ,6)=1的可约模方程在每个素数处恰有两种环形类型，并确定使q↦q^{p^s}下Frobenius存在的最小s≥1；子族ℓ=12n+1只有单一环形类型；给出Heine–Stieltjes剩余消没与非退化Jacobi构型的显式例子；并证明Robba环developing映射的常投影Frobenius等变性的一个障碍。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了此类p进Schwarzian方程的环形正规形式与Frobenius结构：奇数素数下类型有限（四类）且有平方类存在判据，环形幂情形有对角正规形式与同余判据，并在可约模方程上给出精确的环形类型计数与最小Frobenius迭代指数，同时给出等变性障碍。

Abstract: We give explicit annular normal forms and classify Frobenius structures for $p$-adic Schwarzian equations defined by developing differentials. For a unit of the Robba ring, reduction of order expresses the associated module as a quadratic rank-one twist of a unipotent extension determined by the residue. Frobenius existence is characterized by a square-class condition. At odd primes, there are exactly four differential-module types, and a uniform dominant-monomial hypothesis gives a Frobenius formula in analytic families across the zero-residue locus. For rational nonintegral annular powers, we obtain a diagonal normal form and a congruence criterion at odd primes; both results hold at every prime when the unit factor is an explicit square. Applied to the reducible modular equations $θ^2y-\ell^2E_4y/144=0$, with $\gcd(\ell,6)=1$, this gives exactly two annular types at every prime and determines the least $s\geq1$ for which Frobenius under $q\mapsto q^{p^s}$ exists. The subfamily $\ell=12n+1$ has a single annular type. Heine--Stieltjes residue cancellation and nondegenerate Jacobi configurations provide explicit examples. We also prove an obstruction to constant projective Frobenius equivariance of Robba-ring developing maps.

</details>


### [107] [The integer group determinants for $\mathbb{Z}_p^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21224)
*Michael J. Mossinghoff,Christopher Pinner*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究群 $\mathbb{Z}_p^n$ 的整数群行列式中素数 $p$ 的幂次允许条件，给出新的必要条件并证明在 $n\leq 5$ 时是紧的，同时刻画了 $\mathbb{Z}_3^3$ 的完整特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于 $\mathbb{Z}_p^n$ 的整数群行列式，与 $p$ 互素的值已知，但确定 $p$ 的允许幂次的哪些倍数能够出现要复杂得多，这一空白需要填补。

Method: 给出整除 $\mathbb{Z}_p^n$ 整数群行列式的素数 $p$ 的允许幂次的新条件，并验证其在 $n\leq 5$ 时的紧性；通过分析充分大幂次的倍数出现性以及 $\mathbb{Z}_3^3$ 的算术条件来完成刻画。

Result: 所给条件在 $n\leq 5$ 时为紧；所有充分大的 $p$ 的幂次的倍数都能作为 $\mathbb{Z}_p^n$ 的整数群行列式出现；给出了 $\mathbb{Z}_3^3$ 的完整特征，其中某些 $3$ 的幂次的倍数需要特定算术条件。

Conclusion: 本文推进了对 $\mathbb{Z}_p^n$ 整数群行列式中 $p$ 幂次出现规律的理解，建立了尖锐条件与充分大幂次的普遍出现性，并完整刻画了 $\mathbb{Z}_3^3$ 的情形。

Abstract: We give new conditions on allowable powers of a prime $p$ dividing an integer group determinant for the group $\mathbb{Z}_p^n$, and show these conditions are sharp for $n\leq 5$. The values coprime to $p$ for these groups are already known; determining which multiples of allowable powers of $p$ occur is much more complicated. We show that all multiples of sufficiently large powers of $p$ occur as integer group determinants of $\mathbb{Z}_p^n$. We also provide a complete characterization for the group $\mathbb{Z}_3^3$, where particular arithmetic conditions are required for multiples of certain powers of $3$.

</details>


### [108] [Difference of the Sum of All Odd and Even Overlined Parts of Overpartitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21361)
*Nayandeep Deka Baruah,Pankaj Gogoi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究与overpartition中带上划线部分相关的统计量OSOME(n)，给出其生成函数的闭形式，并用以证明若干同余式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Garvan与Sarma近期研究了overpartition及无重复奇数部分分拆中非上划线部分之和。本文针对上划线部分对应的统计量展开相应研究，填补该方向的空白。

Method: 定义OSOME(n)为n的overpartition中所有奇数上划线部分之和减去所有偶数上划线部分之和；推导其生成函数的闭形式，并综合运用初等q级数方法、半整权模形式理论以及overpartition函数的已知同余式。

Result: 得到了OSOME(n)生成函数的闭形式，并借助该生成函数证明了若干同余式。

Conclusion: 该研究将非上划线部分和的统计量结果自然推广至上划线部分情形，并通过多种方法证明了相关同余式。

Abstract: Recently, Garvan and Sarma studied sums of non-overlined parts in overpartitions and partitions without repeated odd parts. In this paper, we study the corresponding statistic for overlined parts. We define $\mathrm{OSOME}(n)$ as the sum of all odd overlined parts minus the sum of all even overlined parts in the overpartitions of $n$. We obtain a closed form for its generating function and use it to prove congruences by elementary $q$-series methods, the theory of half-integral weight modular forms, and known congruences for the overpartition function.

</details>


### [109] [Frobenius Numbers Associated with Primitive Pythagorean Quadruples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21397)
*WonTae Hwang,Kyunghwan Song*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究由本原勾股四元组 $(2mn, 2mp, m^2-n^2-p^2, m^2+n^2+p^2)$ 生成的数值半群 $S$，在 $m$ 关于 $Q=n^2+p^2$ 与 $\delta=\gcd(n,p)$ 满足一定下界条件时，确定了其 Frobenius 数并给出用 $m,n,p$ 表示的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值半群的 Frobenius 数是经典研究对象，而已知结果多集中于由勾股三元组或特定线性型生成的半群。本文旨在将研究推广到由本原勾股四元组生成的半群，填补该类半群 Frobenius 数显式刻画方面的空白。

Method: 将生成元写为 $(a,b,c,d)=(2mn,2mp,m^2-n^2-p^2,m^2+n^2+p^2)$，令 $Q=n^2+p^2$、$\delta=\gcd(n,p)$、$n=\delta n_0$。证明依赖三种技术：模 $2mn$ 的显式完全剩余系、任意半群元素的归一化处理，以及对边界代表元的提升-轨道描述。

Result: 在假设 $m\geq 2Q$（当 $\delta=1$）以及 $m\geq \frac{2Q}{\delta}-1$（其余情形）下，确定了半群 $S=\langle a,b,c,d\rangle$ 的 Frobenius 数，并给出其仅依赖于 $m$、$n$、$p$ 的显式公式。

Conclusion: 本文给出了本原勾股四元组所生成数值半群 Frobenius 数的显式表达式，将 Frobenius 数的研究从勾股三元组情形推广至四元组情形，所用剩余系、归一化与提升-轨道方法对同类问题具有参考价值。

Abstract: Let $(a,b,c,d) = \left(2mn, 2mp, m^2 - n^2 - p^2, m^2 + n^2 + p^2\right)$ be a primitive Pythagorean quadruple and let $S=\langle a, b, c, d\rangle$ be the numerical semigroup generated by $a,b,c,$ and $d.$ For convenience, we also let $Q = n^2 + p^2, δ= \gcd(n,p),$ and $n = δn_0$ for some $n_0 \in \mathbb{Z}$. In this paper, we determine the Frobenius number of $S$ and derive an explicit formula in terms of $m$, $n$, and $p$ with the assumption that $m\geq 2Q$ for $δ= 1$ and $m \geq \frac{2Q}δ - 1$ for the remaining cases. The proof is based on an explicit complete residue system modulo $2mn$, a normalization procedure for arbitrary semigroup elements, and a lift-orbit description of boundary representatives.

</details>


### [110] [Arithmetic structure of $L_2$-norms of ${\mathrm{SL}}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21460)
*Igor E. Shparlinski,Yixiu Xiao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究 SL₂(ℤ) 矩阵元素平方和 R(γ) 的无平方因子性，给出了渐近公式 S_sq(X) = 𝔖 N(X) + O(X^{19/10+o(1)})，并证明存在正比例矩阵使其 R(γ) 无平方因子且至多有 9 个素因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Friedlander 与 Iwaniec (2009) 曾研究 R(γ) 取素值，但其结果依赖于极强形式的 Elliott–Halberstam 猜想。本文希望给出一个无条件下可证明的算术性质（无平方因子性）结果，以补充该工作。

Method: 结合小模数的 δ-method 与处理大平方除子的两平方和估计，计算局部 p²-密度的显式 Euler 乘积，从而导出主项与误差项。

Result: 证明了 S_sq(X) = 𝔖_R^sq N(X) + O(X^{19/10+o(1)})，其中 𝔖_R^sq 为显式正 Euler 乘积；并证明对至少 cN(X)/log X 个矩阵，R(γ) 无平方因子且素因子数不超过 9，c>0 为绝对常数。

Conclusion: 在无条件下得到了 SL₂(ℤ) 矩阵平方和无平方因子的渐近分布，给出了显式主项与误差项，并通过素因子个数上界结果进一步刻画了这类矩阵的分布密度。

Abstract: For a matrix $γ\in\mathrm{SL}_2({\mathbb Z})$, we define \[ {\mathcal R}(γ)=a_1^2+a_2^2+a_3^2+a_4^2, \qquad \text{where} \ γ=\begin{pmatrix}a_1&a_2\\ a_3&a_4\end{pmatrix}, \] and let $S_{\mathrm{sq}}(X)$ count the number of matrices $γ$ with $\|γ\|_\infty = \max\{|a_1|, |a_2|,|a_3|,|a_4|\} \leq X$ and such that ${\mathcal R}(γ)$ is squarefree. We prove that \[ S_{\mathrm{sq}}(X) =
{\mathfrak S}_{\mathcal R}^{\mathrm{sq}}N(X) +O(X^{19/10+o(1)}), \quad \text{as}\ X\to \infty, \] where $N(X)=\#\{γ\in{\mathrm{SL}}_2({\mathbb{Z}}):\|γ\|_\infty\leq X\}$ and ${\mathfrak{S}}_{\mathcal{R}}^{\mathrm{sq}}$ is an explicit positive Euler product of local $p^2$-densities. The proof combines the $δ$-method for small moduli with a sum-of-two-squares estimate for large square divisors. This complements a result of J. B. Friedlander and H. Iwaniec (2009) on prime values of ${\mathcal R}(γ)$, which, however, is conditional on a very strong form of the Elliott--Halberstam conjecture. We also show that ${\mathcal R}(γ)$ is squarefree and has at most $9$ prime divisors for at least $cN(X)/\log X$ matrices $γ\in\mathrm{SL}_2({\mathbb Z})$ with $\|γ\|_\infty\le X$, where $c>0$ is an absolute constant.

</details>


### [111] [A Note On Certain Minimal Excludants Over Overpartitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21649)
*Dipika Sarkar,M. P. Thejitha,S. N. Fathima*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文从算术角度研究overpartition的最小排除子之和σMex(n)与偶最小排除子之和σ_eMex(n)，证明二者模4同余关系及其渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小排除子（minimal excludant）是整数分拆理论中的重要统计量。作者希望从算术视角研究overpartition上的最小排除子之和σMex(n)与偶最小排除子之和σ_eMex(n)，建立二者的同余关系并刻画其渐近性质。

Method: 利用生成函数与q级数方法，对σMex(n)、σ_eMex(n)以及parts均不小于2的overpartition数\bar{p}_{\geq 2}(n)进行组合与算术分析，推导模2^2同余式，并采用解析方法（如鞍点法思想）研究其渐近展开。

Result: 证明了当n≥0时，σ_eMex(n)≡σMex(n)−\bar{p}_{\geq 2}(n) (mod 2^2)；建立了σMex(n)、σ_eMex(n)和\bar{p}_{\geq 2}(n)在n→∞时的渐近行为；特别地证明了σMex(n)∼2σ_eMex(n)。

Conclusion: σMex(n)与σ_eMex(n)之间存在模4同余关系及渐近比例关系σMex(n)∼2σ_eMex(n)，揭示了overpartition最小排除子统计量的算术结构。

Abstract: Let $σ\mathrm{Mex}(n)$ and $σ_e\mathrm{Mex}(n)$ denote the sum of minimal excludants and sum of even minimal excludants over all overpartitions respectively. In this work, we study these two combinatorial objects from an arithmetic perspective. We prove that, for $n\geq 0$, $σ_e\mathrm{Mex}(n)\equivσ\mathrm{Mex}(n)-\bar{p}_{\geq 2}(n)\pmod{2^2},$ where $\bar{p}_{\geq 2}(n)$ denotes the number of overpartitions of $n$ with all parts at least $2$. Furthermore, we prove the asymptotic behavior of $σ\mathrm{Mex}(n)$, $σ_e\mathrm{Mex}(n)$, and $\bar{p}_{\geq 2}(n)$ as $n\to\infty$. In particular, we prove that $σ\mathrm{Mex}(n)\sim 2\,σ_e\mathrm{Mex}(n).$

</details>


### [112] [On low-discrepancy sequences and Poissonian pair correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21689)
*Hannah Porath*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文构造了一个既具有低差异又满足泊松配对相关的序列，从而证明这两个伪随机性性质并非不可兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模1均匀分布（以差异量化）和泊松配对相关是两种伪随机性概念。已知泊松配对相关可推出均匀分布，反之则不成立；且经典低差异序列往往不具备泊松配对相关。由于低差异行为需要高度结构刚性，学界曾猜测这两种性质可能无法兼得，本文旨在检验并否定这一猜测。

Method: 作者通过显式构造的方法，给出了一个具体的序列示例，并证明该序列同时满足低差异性与泊松配对相关。

Result: 成功构造出一个具有泊松配对相关的低差异序列。

Conclusion: 低差异性与泊松配对相关这两种伪随机性性质并非不可调和，之前关于二者可能因结构刚性而无法兼得的猜测不成立。

Abstract: Uniform distribution modulo 1 is a classical notion of pseudo-randomness for sequences in the unit interval, which is quantified in terms of the discrepancy. Sequences whose discrepancy is of the smallest possible asymptotic order are called low-discrepancy sequences. The Poissonian pair correlation is another notion of pseudo-randomness, which studies the distribution of the gaps between pairs of elements of the sequence on a local scale. It is known that Poissonian pair correlation implies uniform distribution mod 1, and that the opposite implication is not true in general. It has also been observed that classical examples of low-discrepancy sequences fail to have Poissonian pair correlation, and it has been speculated that the two properties might be irreconcilable due to the high degree of structural rigidity that is required for low-discrepancy behavior. As we prove in this paper, this is not the case: we construct an example of a low-discrepancy sequence with Poissonian pair correlation.

</details>


### [113] [Higher Labute-Serre duality and Lyndon words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21695)
*Ido Efrat,Levav Ferber Tas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文研究了自由prof蕞群的下p-中心滤链中，滤链因子的对偶配对在Lyndon字基下的性质，证明了配对的支撑集受限于互为排列的Lyndon字，且配对值本质上不依赖于n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的对偶配对仅有单位三角性（unitriangular）这一较弱结论，作者希望揭示该配对在Lyndon字基下更精细的结构，即非零配对的条件及其随滤链层级n的变化规律。

Method: 设S为有限有序基X上的自由prof蕞群，S^{(n,p)}为其下p-中心滤链。利用S^{(n,p)}/S^{(n+1,p)}与H^2(S/S^{(n,p)}, F_p)之间的自然对偶，并考察以长度≤n的Lyndon字为指标的两组基，分析其配对矩阵的支撑与取值。

Result: 证明了两个基元素的配对为零，除非对应的Lyndon字互为排列；进一步证明了配对值本质上独立于n。

Conclusion: 该结果显著加强了已知的单位三角对偶性，表明该配对在Lyndon字基下接近完全对偶，且配对值具有随n的稳定性。

Abstract: Let $S$ be a free profinite group on a finite ordered basis $X$, and let $S^{(n,p)}$, $n=1,2,\ldots,$ denote its lower $p$-central filtration. There is a natural duality between $S^{(n,p)}/S^{(n+1,p)}$ and $H^2(S/S^{(n,p)},\mathbb{F}_p)$. These $\mathbb{F}_p$-linear spaces admit natural bases indexed by Lyndon words of length $\leq n$ in the alphabet $X$. These bases are known to be unitriangularly dual. We prove that they are much closer to being fully dual, by showing that the pairing between two basis elements vanishes unless the corresponding Lyndon words are permutations of one another. We further show that the value of the pairing is essentially independent of $n$.

</details>


### [114] [Non-existence of sets with few special directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21779)
*Luca Ghidelli,Gergely Kiss,Gábor Somlai*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域 $\mathbb{F}_p^2$ 上子集与多重集的"特殊方向"数目问题：证明充分大的素数 $p$ 下集合不可能恰有 4 个特殊方向，并对更多特殊方向给出条件性推广；用二阶矩方法证明一般情形下集合的特殊方向数不能恰为固定的 $k\ge4$，且该结果在 $k$ 上可达 $\sqrt p/\log p$ 量级；同时说明多重集情形截然不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特殊方向（special directions）刻画了有限域平面上点集在某些投影方向上的退化行为，是加法组合与有限几何中的核心研究对象。此前已知在多重集情形下特殊方向的分布非常自由，而子集情形是否存在类似的自由度尚不清楚。本文旨在确定：对于一个基数被 $p$ 整除的集合，其特殊方向数目是否可能取到某些特定值（尤其是较小的固定值如 4 及一般的 $k\ge4$），从而揭示子集与多重集之间的本质差异。

Method: 作者综合使用组合与代数方法。首先针对恰有 4 个特殊方向的集合，利用投影函数的结构性质进行直接分析并给出否定性证明。对于更大数目的特殊方向，则在相应投影函数满足仿射无关（affine-independence）的假设下将结论推广。另一方面，引入二阶矩（second-moment）方法，对特殊方向的计数进行统一的概率/组合估计，证明对每个固定 $k\ge4$，当 $p$ 充分大时不存在恰好 $k$ 个特殊方向的子集，并给出该结论在 $k$ 上的一致有效范围。作为对照，构造 $\{0,1,2\}$-值多重集，实现任意不超过 $p$ 个方向的预设集合。

Result: 主要结果有三：其一，对充分大的奇素数 $p$，任何基数被 $p$ 整除的集合 $S\subseteq\mathbb{F}_p^2$ 都不可能恰好有 4 个特殊方向，并在投影函数仿射无关的假设下将该结论推广到更多特殊方向；其二，二阶矩论证表明，对每个固定 $k\ge4$，当 $p$ 充分大时 $\mathbb{F}_p^2$ 的任何子集都不可能有恰好 $k$ 个特殊方向，且结论对 $k$ 一致成立，范围可达 $k\le c\sqrt p/\log p$（$c>0$ 为常数）；其三，与子集形成鲜明对比，对任意至多 $p$ 个方向的预设集合，都存在一个 $\{0,1,2\}$-值多重集恰以该集合为其特殊方向集。

Conclusion: 子集与多重集在特殊方向的可实现数目上存在根本性差异：子集在 $p$ 充分大时被严格限制，无法取到任何固定的 $k\ge4$，且该限制在 $\sqrt p/\log p$ 范围内一致有效；而多重集则完全自由，可任意指定不超过 $p$ 个特殊方向。这一对比凸显了集合的刚性结构，并说明除条件性推广外，仿射无关假设可能是必要的。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime and let $S\subseteq\mathbb{F}_p^2$ have cardinality divisible by $p$. We prove that, for all sufficiently large primes $p$, no such set has exactly four special directions, and obtain a conditional extension to larger numbers of special directions under an affine-independence assumption on the corresponding projection functions. A separate second-moment argument shows more generally that, for every fixed $k\ge4$, no subset of $\mathbb{F}_p^2$ has exactly $k$ special directions once $p$ is sufficiently large. In fact, the result holds uniformly for $k$ up to a positive constant times $\sqrt p/\log p$. In contrast, for multisets every prescribed collection of at most $p$ directions can occur as the set of special directions of a $\{0,1,2\}$-valued multiset on $\mathbb{F}_p^2$.

</details>


### [115] [Real quadratic fields and finite quantum dilogarithms I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21892)
*Danylo Radchenko,Campbell Wheeler*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了实二次域对应的Stark-Shintani射线类不变量（Stark单位）是代数数，方法是通过Faddeev模量子双对数特殊值满足的多项式方程组及Ocneanu刚性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Stark-Shintani射线类不变量（Stark单位）的代数性，并探索其与Faddeev模量子双对数特殊值、融合范畴及SIC-POVM相关猜想的联系。

Method: 利用Faddeev模量子双对数的特殊值，发现其满足一个显式的超定多项式方程组，该方程组与Andersen-Kashaev在双循环群乘积上量子双对数定义方程的变体相匹配；通过该方程组的解对Izumi引入的融合环进行范畴化，再借助Ocneanu刚性定理证明特殊值的代数性。

Result: 证明了实二次域的Stark单位是代数数；得到一族显式的无穷非有理近群融合范畴；并证明了Appleby、Flammia和Kopp近期由Zauner关于SIC-POVM（复等角线）猜想所启发而提出的Stark单位二次关系族。

Conclusion: 模量子双对数特殊值的代数结构为Stark单位的代数性提供了新证明途径，并在融合范畴与SIC-POVM相关猜想的二次关系上产生重要应用。

Abstract: We prove that Stark-Shintani ray class invariants (Stark units) associated to real quadratic fields are algebraic numbers. These invariants are given by special values of Faddeev's modular quantum dilogarithm, introduced by Garoufalidis-Kashaev-Zagier. Our main discovery is that special values of modular quantum dilogarithm satisfy an explicit overdetermined system of polynomial equations, matching a variation on the defining equations of Andersen-Kashaev's notion of a quantum dilogarithm on a product of two cyclic groups. Solutions to this system of equations can be used to categorify fusion rings introduced by Izumi, and the algebraicity of the special values then follows by Ocneanu's rigidity theorem. As a byproduct, we obtain an explicit infinite family of irrational near-group fusion categories. As a further application, we prove a family of quadratic relations for Stark units recently conjectured by Appleby, Flammia, and Kopp motivated by Zauner's conjecture about SIC-POVMs (complex equiangular lines).

</details>


### [116] [$\mathbf{Bad}(\mathbf{r};\mathbf{s})$ is Hyperplane Absolute Winning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22016)
*Chengyang Wu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：对任意给定权重 $\mathbf{r},\mathbf{s}$，$(\mathbf{r};\mathbf{s})$-坏逼近的 $m\times n$ 实矩阵集合在 $\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$ 上为超平面绝对获胜集，从而完整回答了 D. Kleinbock 1998 年提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D. Kleinbock 于 1998 年提出关于加权坏逼近矩阵集合的结构性质问题（Question 8.2 (iii)），此前该问题尚未被完全解决。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$ 空间上，针对 $m$ 维权重 $\mathbf{r}$ 与 $n$ 维权重 $\mathbf{s}$，研究 $(\mathbf{r};\mathbf{s})$-坏逼近矩阵集合，并运用超平面绝对获胜（hyperplane absolute winning）这一度量丢番逼近中的博弈论/分形刻画工具进行证明。

Result: 证明了 $(\mathbf{r};\mathbf{s})$-坏逼近的 $m\times n$ 矩阵集合是 $\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$ 上的超平面绝对获胜集。

Conclusion: 该结果完整回答了 Kleinbock 1998 年提出的问题（Question 8.2 (iii)），给出加权坏逼近矩阵集合在强测度意义下的结构性刻画。

Abstract: Given an $m$-dimensional weight $\mathbf{r}$ and an $n$-dimensional weight $\mathbf{s}$, we prove that the set of $(\mathbf{r};\mathbf{s})$-badly approximable $m\times n$ matrices is hyperplane absolute winning on $\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$. This fully answers a question \cite[Question 8.2 (iii)]{Kl} of D. Kleinbock in 1998.

</details>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Binary Minor Certificates for the $\mathcal A(1)$-hit problem of $MO(2)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20859)
*Dang Vo Phuc*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该文计算了 $\mathbb F_2\otimes_{\mathcal A(1)}M$（$M=\widetilde H^*(MO(2);\mathbb F_2)$）在每个内次数 $h\ge0$ 处的结构，并给出严格证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服有限实验外推的局限，严格验证 Shih 2012 报告提出的单项生成族与模分解，并修正相关计算中的遗漏。

Method: 采用严格认证式证明：在每个次数展示二进制 hit 矩阵的行列式为一的非零子式，并构造互补的线性对偶泛函族湮灭所有 hit 列，从而精确保证秩；同时利用长度二对称多项式模型导出闭形式二项式公式。

Result: 确定各内次数 $h\ge0$ 的商 $\mathbb F_2\otimes_{\mathcal A(1)}M$；得到每个 Steenrod 平方的闭二项式公式；修正 $\mathcal A(0)$ 计算的边界遗漏；计算 $\mathcal A(1)$-余hit 的精确 Hilbert 级数；证明 $M$ 分解为两个稳定 $\mathcal A(1)$-子模的直和。

Conclusion: 严格建立了 Shih 提出的单项生成族与模分解，给出闭形式公式、边界修正、精确 Hilbert 级数及内在直和分解。

Abstract: Let $M=\widetilde H^*(MO(2);\mathbb F_2)$ and let $\mathcal A(1)$ be the subalgebra of the mod $2$ Steenrod algebra generated by $\operatorname{Sq}^1$ and $\operatorname{Sq}^2$. In this work, we determine the quotient $\mathbb F_2\otimes_{\mathcal A(1)}M$ in every internal degree $h\geq0$, where internal degree $h$ corresponds to cohomological degree $h+2$. To overcome the limitations of finite experimental extrapolation, our proof is strictly certifying: in each degree, we exhibit a determinant-one minor of the binary hit matrix and a complementary family of linear dual functionals annihilating every hit column, mathematically guaranteeing the exact rank for all degrees. This rigorously establishes the monomial generating families and module decomposition proposed in Shih's 2012 report. Furthermore, we derive a closed binomial formula for every Steenrod square from the length-two symmetric-polynomial model, correct a boundary omission in the $\mathcal A(0)$ calculation, compute the exact Hilbert series of the $\mathcal A(1)$-cohit, and prove an intrinsic direct-sum decomposition of $M$ into two stable $\mathcal A(1)$-submodules.

</details>


### [118] [A Tale of Two Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20860)
*Johnathon Taylor*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文在余球状 $\infty$-群胚范畴中给出一种新的柱对象（cylinder object）表示，并证明其与 Lanari 构造的柱构造同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柱对象是模型范畴与同伦论中的关键工具，用于定义同伦、左/右同伦等概念。在余球状 $\infty$-群胚范畴中，已有的柱构造（Lanari）缺少另一种独立视角的表示，作者希望提供一个新的构造方式并验证其与已有构造的一致性。

Method: 在余球状 $\infty$-群胚范畴内显式构造一个新的柱对象（presentation），随后通过范畴论同构论证，将该构造与 Lanari 给出的柱构造进行比对，证明二者同构。

Result: 所提出的新柱对象构造与 Lanari 的柱构造同构，即两种构造在范畴意义下等价。

Conclusion: 新的表示与 Lanari 的构造一致，为余球状 $\infty$-群胚范畴中的柱对象提供了另一等价刻画，可加深对该范畴同伦结构的理解。

Abstract: We provide a new presentation for a cylinder object inside of the category of co-globular $\infty$-groupoids. We prove that the cylinder construction that Lanari provides is isomorphic to the one we build in this paper.

</details>


### [119] [Products in absolute Hodge cohomology and mixed Hodge formality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21034)
*Pedro Magalhães*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文将绝对Hodge上同调的乘法结构与混合Hodge形式性联系起来，证明混合Hodge形式性蕴含绝对Hodge上同调作为平方零扩张的分裂，并定义研究了一类类似Massey积、阻碍该分裂的乘积，同时将其与乘法Chow-Künneth分解的存在性相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复代数几何中，混合Hodge结构提供了丰富的额外信息，而有理同伦论中的形式性概念可用于刻画上同调代数的简洁性。作者希望将形式性概念推广到考虑混合Hodge结构的情形（即混合Hodge形式性），并揭示其与绝对Hodge上同调乘法结构及代数圈理论中Chow-Künneth分解之间的深层联系。

Method: 针对复代数簇，作者研究其绝对Hodge上同调上的乘积结构；证明了混合Hodge形式性意味着绝对Hodge上同调可分裂为平方零扩张；并定义并研究了一类类比Massey积的高阶乘积，这些乘积取值于绝对Hodge上同调中并构成其分裂的阻碍；此外还探讨了混合Hodge形式性与乘法Chow-Künneth分解存在性之间的关系。

Result: 证明了混合Hodge形式性蕴含绝对Hodge上同调作为平方零扩张的分裂；构造了一类类似Massey积的乘积，它们位于绝对Hodge上同调中并阻碍上述分裂；建立了混合Hodge形式性与乘法Chow-Künneth分解存在性之间的关联。

Conclusion: 绝对Hodge上同调的乘法分裂受到混合Hodge形式性的控制，而所定义的高阶乘积正是其障碍；同时，混合Hodge形式性与乘法Chow-Künneth分解的存在性密切相关，从而在形式性理论、Hodge结构与代数圈理论之间架起了桥梁。

Abstract: Given a complex algebraic variety, we relate the product structure on its absolute Hodge cohomology with mixed Hodge formality, a notion of formality in the sense of rational homotopy, that takes into account the mixed Hodge structures present in complex algebraic geometry. Namely, we prove that mixed Hodge formality implies the splitting of absolute Hodge cohomology as a square-zero extension. We define and study certain products, akin to Massey products, which live in the absolute Hodge cohomology and obstruct its splitting. We also relate mixed Hodge formality to the existence of multiplicative Chow-Künneth decompositions.

</details>


### [120] [Character theory for 2-representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21052)
*Kate Ponto,Travis Wheeler*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该文构建了一个面向2-表示（2-representations）的直观特征标理论，以双范畴迹为核心，并证明其与限制、诱导相容，且在充分有限的情形下具有次级特征标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群表示可自然推广到高阶范畴，2-表示的限制与诱导已有直接类比经典定义的版本，但相应的特征标理论长期不清晰、缺乏直观框架。作者因此整合多方面的动机来填补这一空白。

Method: 以用于描述拓扑不动点理论的双范畴迹（bicategorical trace）为基础构造特征标理论；采用Ben-Zvi与Nadler的次级迹（secondary traces），其与微分分次范畴及Fourier-Mukai变换背景下的Riemann-Roch和Lefschetz型定理相联系；并借鉴Lipman关于Grothendieck对偶的工作，以及Bartlett与Ganter–Kapranov的特征标方法（后者又受Hopkins、Kuhn与Ravenel关于等变Morava E-理论工作的启发）。

Result: 所构造的特征标与限制和诱导运算相容；在充分有限的情形下，该特征标还容许一个次级特征标。

Conclusion: 论文给出了一个直观且具兼容性的2-表示特征标理论，将双范畴迹、次级迹与Grothendieck对偶及多种特征标进路统一起来，为2-表示的特征标计算提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Group representations very comfortably admit a higher categorical generalization. This includes induction and restriction for 2-representations that very directly parallel classical definitions. An appropriate character theory for 2-representations has been more opaque.
  In this paper we draw from a range of motivations to provide an intuitive character theory for 2-representations. It is built from the bicategorical trace developed to describe topological fixed point theory. We use the secondary traces of Ben-Zvi and Nadler which connect to Riemann-Roch and Lefschetz-type theorems in the context of differential graded categories and Fourier-Mukai transforms. Additional sources of motivation include Lipmans' work on Grothendieck duality and, crucially, the Bartlett and Ganter--Kapranov approaches to characters. (The second of these was in turn motivated by Hopkins, Kuhn and Ravenel's work on equivariant Morava $E$-theories.) Our characters are compatible with restriction and induction and, in cases that are sufficiently finite, admit a secondary character.

</details>


### [121] [Continuous Comparison of Free Simplicial Prounipotent and Pro-$p$ Resolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21513)
*Andrey Mikhovich*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文在特征零的幂幺群（prounipotent groups）与pro-p群范畴中，给出了自由单纯分解比较定理的连续版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于作者此前关于 subcontractible 表列的 Bousfield--Kan 完备化的工作，需要处理自由单纯分解的比较定理，并将其推广到连续（拓扑）情形。

Method: 构造带退化相容自由基（秩可无限）的分解：特征零情形利用连续线性分裂与完备自由李代数的泛性质完成提升步骤；pro-p情形利用自由pro-p群的经典投射性。通过相对提升论证与显式单纯柱体建立同伦唯一性。使用单纯范畴中的真实满射表述自由基，使由单纯恒等式关联的退化词从一开始即被等同，并完整写出提升论证所需的匹配对象。

Result: 得到幂幺群与pro-p群范畴中自由单纯分解的连续版本比较定理，并确立所构造分解的同伦唯一性。

Conclusion: 连续比较定理在特征零与pro-p两种情形下均成立，自由基的显式处理与匹配对象的刻画为后续 Bousfield--Kan 完备化研究提供了工具。

Abstract: We give a continuous version of the comparison theorem for free simplicial resolutions in the categories of prounipotent groups over a field of characteristic zero and of pro-$p$ groups. The resolutions carry free bases compatible with degeneracies; their ranks may be infinite. In characteristic zero the lifting step follows from continuous linear splittings and the universal property of completed free Lie algebras. In the pro-$p$ case it follows from the classical projectivity of free pro-$p$ groups. A relative lifting argument and an explicit simplicial cylinder establish homotopy uniqueness. We formulate the free bases using actual surjections in the simplex category, so degeneracy words related by simplicial identities are identified from the outset, and we spell out the full matching objects used in the lifting argument. The question arose naturally in our preceding work on Bousfield--Kan completions of subcontractible presentations.

</details>


### [122] [Bousfield--Kan Completions of Subcontractible Presentations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21534)
*Andrey Mikhovich*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文通过自由单纯消解、其滤过谱序列与非交换算术方的相互作用研究Bousfield–Kan完备化，对有限型自由离散单纯群给出了整原幂零完备化作为有理原单幂零完备化与各pro-p完备化在显式adélic单纯群上的同伦拉回表达，并将其应用于呈现复形得到R∞K的同伦型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望理解Bousfield–Kan完备化（特别是整原幂零完备化）的结构，并寻求将整完备化分解为有理部分与pro-p部分、通过算术方粘合的显式构造。已有方法通常需要分量群的幂零性假设，作者意在去掉这一限制并给出可计算的表达。

Method: 方法结合三要素：自由单纯消解（free simplicial resolutions）、其滤过谱序列（filtration spectral sequences），以及非交换算术方（noncommutative arithmetic square）。adélic项通过在有限幂零阶段取限制积再取逆极限构成；对可缩呈现的有限子呈现给出显式应用；利用relator的独立性使有理与mod-p滤过谱序列的正次项消失，并在商塔上验证收敛；再通过自由单纯消解的连续比较，在特征零情形用该范畴中的态射与同伦实现与常值自由原单幂零群的等价。

Result: 主要结果：对有限型自由离散单纯群，其整原幂零完备化同伦等价于有理原单幂零完备化与各pro-p完备化在显式adélic单纯群上的同伦拉回，且无需分量群幂零假设。对相应呈现复形K，得到R∞K ≃ K(F_R(Z),1)，其中R=Q、F_p、Z分别对应自由原单幂零群的有理点、自由pro-p群、自由原幂零群。在该情形下，幂零阶段上相容的收缩使算术方的四个项全部一致。

Conclusion: Bousfield–Kan整完备化可由有理与pro-p完备化通过非交换算术方显式重构，adélic群提供了统一的粘合对象；relator独立性带来的谱序列退化与连续比较使构造可精确实现。对呈现复形，完备化空间同伦等价于相应自由（原单幂零/pro-p/原幂零）群的分类空间。

Abstract: We study Bousfield--Kan completions through the interaction of free simplicial resolutions, their filtration spectral sequences, and a noncommutative arithmetic square. For every free discrete simplicial group of finite type, we express its integral pronilpotent completion as the homotopy pullback of its rational prounipotent completion and the product of its pro-$p$ completions over an explicit adelic simplicial group. The adelic entry is formed by taking restricted products at finite nilpotent stages and then their inverse limit; no nilpotency assumption on the original group of components is required. Finite subpresentations of contractible presentations provide an explicit application of this construction. Independence of the specified relators makes the positive-degree terms of the rational and mod-$p$ filtration spectral sequences vanish, with convergence verified on the quotient towers. Continuous comparison of free simplicial resolutions then realizes, in characteristic zero, the equivalence with a constant free prounipotent group by morphisms and homotopies in that category. For the corresponding presentation complex $K$ we obtain $R_\infty K\simeq K(F_R(Z),1)$ for $R=\mathbb Q,\mathbb F_p,\mathbb Z$, where $Z$ indexes a complementary basis and $F_R(Z)$ denotes, respectively, the rational points of a free prounipotent group, a free pro-$p$ group, or a free pronilpotent group. Compatible contractions at the nilpotent stages identify all four entries of the arithmetic square in this case.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Square Root Gauss-Newton iLQR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21053)
*Maximilian Haas-Heger,Jur van den Berg*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Gauss-Newton（加权最小二乘）结构的iLQR平方根形式：利用扩展的augmented cost-to-go矩阵的半正定性，使反向传播每步仅需一次QR分解，即可直接提取反馈增益与传播的Cholesky因子，兼具数值稳定性与算法简洁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: iLQR通过动态规划求解局部线性二次近似，传播二次cost-to-go函数。其在增广拉格朗日框架下处理约束时，大惩罚项会严重恶化数值条件。平方根形式（传播Cholesky因子）可显著改善数值性质，但已有平方根iLQR及相关算法或在数值上次优，或在算法上复杂，甚至两者兼有。因此需要一个既数值稳健又算法简洁的平方根iLQR。

Method: 关键在于利用代价函数的Gauss-Newton（加权最小二乘）结构。该结构带来的半正定性不仅适用于Hessian，还扩展到完整的augmented cost-to-go矩阵。据此设计反向传播：每一步仅需一次QR分解，从中直接提取反馈增益与需要传播的Cholesky因子。

Result: 所提出的平方根iLQR反向传播步骤简化为单次QR分解，可直接得到反馈增益与传播的Cholesky因子，且具备由Gauss-Newton结构保证的半正定性，数值性质优于既有平方根形式。

Conclusion: 有效的平方根iLQR形式源于代价函数的Gauss-Newton（加权最小二乘）结构，该结构赋予完整augmented cost-to-go矩阵半正定性，从而实现异常简洁的反向传播（每步一次QR分解）。相比已有方法，本文方法在数值稳健性与算法简洁性上均有改进。

Abstract: The iterative Linear Quadratic Regulator (iLQR) is a widely used algorithm for nonlinear trajectory optimization. At each iteration, it solves a local linear-quadratic approximation of the problem via dynamic programming, propagating a quadratic cost-to-go function. If the Hessian of the cost-to-go approximation is positive-semidefinite, one can derive a square root formulation of iLQR that propagates its Cholesky factor instead. This offers significant numerical advantages - much as square root Kalman filters improve upon their conventional counterparts - particularly when iLQR is used within an augmented Lagrangian framework for handling constraints, where large penalties degrade conditioning. Previous square root formulations of iLQR and related algorithms exist, but they are either numerically suboptimal, algorithmically complex, or both. In this paper, we show that the key to an effective square root formulation lies in the Gauss-Newton (weighted least-squares) structure of the cost function: this yields a positive semidefiniteness property that extends beyond the Hessian to the full augmented cost-to-go matrix, and enables a backward pass of remarkable simplicity in which each step reduces to a single QR-decomposition, from which the feedback gain and propagated Cholesky factor are extracted directly.

</details>


### [124] [Bounds on the Semidefinite Programming Complexity of the Euclidean Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21089)
*Guanxi Li,Kevin Shu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出欧几里得球的线性矩阵不等式表示规模与半定扩展复杂度的紧下界：任何表示欧几里得球的LMI至少需要n×n矩阵，任何谱面体扩展至少需要2√(n−1)大小的矩阵，与已有显式构造吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧几里得球是半定规划中的基本对象，其线性矩阵不等式(LMI)表示与谱面体扩展的最小规模此前仅有显式构造给出的上界，缺乏匹配的紧下界，因此本文旨在确定其精确的下界。

Method: 证明依赖于关于谱面体面集维数的初等事实，并推广到任何其极点构成半代数集的凸集。

Result: 任何表示欧几里得球的线性矩阵不等式所使用的矩阵规模至少为n；任何欧几里得球的谱面体扩展所使用的矩阵规模至少为2√(n−1)。这两个下界与现有显式构造所达到的界相匹配，即为紧界。

Conclusion: 欧几里得球的LMI表示复杂度与半定扩展复杂度被精确确定，且所采用的面维数论证方法具有更广的适用性，可推广至极点构成半代数集的凸集。

Abstract: We present tight lower bounds for the size of any linear matrix inequality representation of the Euclidean ball, and on the semidefinite extension complexity of the ball. Specifically, we show that any linear matrix inequality representing the Euclidean ball must involve matrices of size at least $n$, and any spectrahedral extension of the Euclidean ball must involve matrices of size at least $2\sqrt{n-1}$. These match the bounds given by existing explicit constructions. Our proofs rely on elementary facts about the dimension of the set of faces of spectrahedra, and indeed extend to any convex set whose extreme points form a semialgebraic set.

</details>


### [125] [Spectral Deflation for Factorization-Free Matrix Filtering in Muon and Semidefinite Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21102)
*Haoran Sun,Shucheng Kang,Heng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出"谱去平"（spectral deflation）：先估计并移除主导谱分量，对归一化残差用原滤波器处理后再还原，可在不改变目标矩阵函数的前提下严格降低Newton–Schulz族的有限步误差，并在Muon与无分解ADMM上取得实证收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器与一阶半定规划（SDP）求解器的GPU实现共享同一计算模式：用固定深度多项式滤波替代矩阵分解。当少数主导谱分量携带输入的大部分尺度时，归一化会把其余谱压向零，而零附近正是滤波精度最差的区域，从而引入误差。

Method: 提出谱去平：估计主导谱分量并将其移除，对归一化后的残差沿用原滤波器进行滤波，最后将主导分量还原，从而保证目标矩阵函数不变。实现上，Muon采用批量随机SVD，ADMM采用热启动子空间跟踪。

Result: 理论证明谱去平严格降低经典Newton–Schulz族的有限步误差；实验上，在Newton–Schulz与Polar Express两种映射下均持续改进GPT-2预训练（包括墙钟时间），并在大规模SDP上以相近的投影时间降低无分解ADMM的KKT残差。

Conclusion: 谱去平是一种通用且理论可证的改进手段，可同时提升Muon优化器与一阶SDP求解器在低精度滤波场景下的数值精度与效率。

Abstract: GPU implementations of the Muon optimizer and of first-order semidefinite programming (SDP) solvers share one computational pattern: a matrix factorization is replaced by a fixed-depth polynomial filter applied after normalization. When a few dominant spectral components carry most of the input's scale, normalization pushes the remaining spectrum toward zero, where the filter is least accurate. We propose spectral deflation: estimate the dominant components, remove them, filter the normalized residual with the unchanged filter, and restore them. Deflation preserves the target matrix function, and we prove that it strictly reduces the finite-step error of the classical Newton--Schulz family. Implemented with batched randomized SVD for Muon and warm-started subspace tracking for ADMM, deflation consistently improves GPT-2 pretraining over the corresponding Muon baselines with both the Newton--Schulz and Polar Express mappings, also in wall-clock time, and lowers the KKT residuals of factorization-free ADMM on large-scale SDPs within a similar projection time.

</details>


### [126] [Benign Nonconvex Landscape for Policy Optimization: Infinite-Horizon Discounted MDPs with General State and Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21433)
*Xin Chen,Minda Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一般状态-动作空间上无限时域折扣MDP在结构化平稳策略类下的策略梯度优化景观，提出更弱的条件以保证无次优稳定点并建立PLK条件，且在两类运筹模型中得到验证与非渐近收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有基于加权策略迭代的全局收敛框架要求策略类在"逐策略加权策略改进"下封闭，但该性质在策略类已包含最优策略时仍可能不成立，因此需要更弱、更易验证的条件来保证策略梯度方法收敛到全局最优。

Method: 提出较弱的条件以保证无次优平稳点，并建立策略梯度目标函数的Polyak–Lojasiewicz–Kurdyka（PLK）条件：其一基于有限concentrability系数，其二基于对每个状态成立的策略改进界且无需concentrability假设；进一步在马尔可夫调制需求的库存系统与随机现金余额问题中，利用Bellman方程证明Q值函数关于动作变量的近似凸性，偏差由一阶平稳性度量控制。

Result: 在两类模型中得到指数为1的PLK条件，并在额外曲率假设下得到指数为2的PLK条件；结合策略梯度的Lipschitz连续性，分别推出精确策略梯度投影梯度下降的O(1/ε)迭代复杂度与线性收敛，并给出求解无限时域折扣马尔可夫调制需求库存系统及随机现金余额问题的首个非渐近收敛率。

Conclusion: 所提条件在常见标准假设下可涵盖既有框架的适用场景，为一般策略类下的策略梯度全局收敛提供更灵活的充分条件，并将理论保证拓展至具体运筹管理问题。

Abstract: We study the optimization landscape for infinite-horizon discounted Markov decision processes (MDPs) with general state and action spaces under structured stationary policy classes. A general weighted policy-iteration approach to establishing global convergence guarantees for policy gradient methods requires closure under weighted policy improvement at every policy, a property that may fail even when the policy class contains an optimal policy. To address this issue, we propose weaker conditions that guarantee the absence of suboptimal stationary points and establish the Polyak--Lojasiewicz--Kurdyka (PLK) condition for the policy gradient objective with a finite concentrability coefficient. We also establish the PLK condition from a policy-improvement bound that holds at every state, without a concentrability assumption. Our general results encompass settings covered by the earlier framework when the common standing assumptions hold for the same policy class and parameter domain. We further verify our proposed conditions for two operations models: inventory systems with Markov-modulated demand and stochastic cash-balance problems. For both models, the Bellman equation yields approximate convexity of the Q-value functions in the action variable, with deviations controlled by the first-order stationarity measure. These estimates establish exponent-one and, under additional curvature assumptions, exponent-two PLK conditions, which, together with Lipschitz continuity of the policy gradient, imply an $\mathcal{O}(1/ε)$ iteration complexity and linear convergence, respectively, for projected gradient descent using exact policy gradients. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first non-asymptotic convergence rates for solving infinite-horizon discounted inventory systems with Markov-modulated demand and stochastic cash-balance problems using policy gradient methods.

</details>


### [127] [Moving-Average Iteration of Linear Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21442)
*Jeremias Epperlein,Fabian Wirth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究复矩阵的滑动平均迭代（moving-average iteration），将其纳入Krasnoselskii与Mann迭代的一般框架，给出了依赖固定窗口宽度的渐近稳定性充要谱条件，推导出复平面上稳定区域的显式公式，并证明该区域为开、凸且半代数集，同时揭示稳定区域随窗口长度严格增大并确定了窗口趋于无穷时的极限稳定区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动平均迭代在数值计算与迭代方法中具有应用价值，但其相对于经典Krasnoselskii和Mann迭代的理论定位尚不清晰，且其渐近稳定性的谱条件与稳定区域的几何性质缺乏系统刻画。作者希望将滑动平均迭代统一到已有的迭代理论框架中，并给出完整、精确的稳定性分析。

Method: 将滑动平均迭代置于Krasnoselskii和Mann迭代的一般理论框架下；针对复矩阵，通过谱分析推导渐近稳定性的充要条件，该条件依赖于固定窗口宽度；进而解析求解复平面中对应的稳定区域，并研究其几何与分析性质（开放性、凸性、半代数性）及随窗口长度变化的单调行为。

Result: 给出了依赖窗口宽度的渐近稳定性充要谱条件；推导出复平面中稳定区域的显式公式，并证明该区域为开集、凸集且半代数集；证明稳定区域随窗口长度严格增大，并确定了窗口尺寸趋于无穷时的极限稳定区域。

Conclusion: 滑动平均迭代可被有效纳入Krasnoselskii和Mann迭代框架，其稳定性可由谱条件精确刻画；相应的复平面稳定区域具有良好的几何结构（开、凸、半代数），且随窗口长度单调扩张并存在明确的极限区域。这些结果完善了滑动平均迭代的稳定性理论。

Abstract: We consider moving-average iteration for fixed complex matrices and place the theory in the general context of Krasnoselskii and Mann iteration. Necessary and sufficient spectral conditions for the asymptotic stability of moving-average iteration are presented in dependence on the fixed window width. For the corresponding region of stability in the complex plane explicit formulas are derived and it is shown that the region is open, convex and semi-algebraic. The regions are shown to be strictly increasing in the window length and the limiting stability region is determined as the window size tends to infinity.

</details>


### [128] [A global stochastic maximum principle for forward-backward stochastic control systems with quadratic convex generator and unbounded terminal condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21451)
*Ying Hu,Feng Li,Jiaqiang Wen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究正倒向随机控制系统在二次凸生成元与无界终端条件下的随机最优控制问题，控制域非凸，借助新概率测度与新的伴随方程推导方法，建立了全局随机最大值原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题的难点在于：生成元为二次凸且终端条件无界，导致传统BMO鞅方法失效；同时控制域不必为凸，经典凸变分方法不再适用，因此需要新的分析框架。

Method: 引入一个新的概率测度，将后续分析全部置于该测度下进行，以规避BMO鞅方法缺失带来的困难；并采用一种新的伴随方程推导途径。

Result: 在所设条件下成功推导出伴随方程，并据此建立了全局随机最大值原理。

Conclusion: 通过测度变换与新的伴随方程推导方法，可在非凸控制域、二次凸生成元及无界终端条件下获得全局随机最大值原理，推广了已有结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a stochastic optimal control problem for forward-backward stochastic control systems with quadratic convex generator and unbounded terminal condition, where the control domain is not necessarily convex. Due to the absence of bounded mean oscillation (BMO) martingales approach, we introduce a new probability measure, under which all subsequent analysis is then carried out under this new measure. Finally, by means of a new approach to the derivation of the adjoint equations, a global stochastic maximum principle is established.

</details>


### [129] [A Sparse Corner-Difference MILP for Minimum Square Tiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21453)
*Zhuo Yu,Yuan Wang,Zhuo Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究用边长小于N的整数边轴对齐正方形铺满N×N正方形并最小化块数的问题，提出角差分MILP（CD-MILP），用至多四个带符号角系数表示每个正方形，将覆盖相关非零系数从Θ(N^5)降至Θ(N^3)，并引入角序约束部分消除D_4对称性；实验表明组合公式求解能力显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的基于放置的精确覆盖MILP存在两个缺陷：一是包含Θ(N^5)个放置-单元非零系数，规模庞大；二是受正方形域的D_4二面体对称性影响，导致求解效率低。因此需要一种更紧凑的建模方式以减少非零系数并缓解对称性。

Method: 提出角差分MILP（CD-MILP）：将每个被选正方形用至多四个带符号角系数表示；通过二维前缀重构证明其约束与原单元覆盖方程等价，同时把覆盖相关非零系数降至Θ(N^3)。此外引入轻量级角序约束，使每个D_4轨道至少保留一个代表，尽管并列情形可能残留对称性。在九个素数规模实例上，使用Gurobi和COPT比较基线、CD-MILP、带角序的基线及其组合。

Result: 在所述实验设置下，组合公式用Gurobi求解了8个实例、用COPT求解了7个实例，而基线分别仅求解4个和5个。组合公式预求解后的矩阵平均约含10^5个非零系数，基线则约含10^7个。

Conclusion: CD-MILP通过角差分表示大幅压缩了非零系数规模，结合角序约束可有效提升MILP求解器在大规模正方形铺砌问题上的求解能力，显著优于标准放置型精确覆盖模型。

Abstract: We study the problem of tiling an $N\times N$ square with axis-aligned squares whose side lengths are integers strictly less than $N$, with the objective of minimizing the number of tiles. The standard placement-based exact-cover MILP contains $Θ(N^5)$ placement-to-cell nonzero coefficients and is also affected by the dihedral symmetry of the square domain. We introduce a Corner-Difference MILP (CD-MILP) that represents each selected square by at most four signed corner coefficients. Two-dimensional prefix reconstruction shows that the resulting constraints are equivalent to the original cell-cover equations, while reducing the coverage-related nonzero count to $Θ(N^3)$. We also introduce lightweight corner-ordering constraints that retain at least one representative from every $D_4$ orbit, although ties may leave residual symmetry. Computational experiments on nine prime-size instances compare the baseline, CD-MILP, the baseline with corner ordering, and their combination using Gurobi and COPT. Under the stated experimental settings, the combined formulation solves eight instances with Gurobi and seven with COPT, compared with four and five, respectively, for the baseline. Its average presolved matrixs contain approximately $10^5$ nonzero coefficients on average, compared with approximately $10^7$ for the baseline.

</details>


### [130] [Single-Loop Stochastic Projected Damped Extragradient Methods for Stochastic Nonconvex--(Strongly) Concave Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21747)
*Huiling Zhang,Minhao Zhang,Zi Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出单循环随机投影阻尼外梯度方法（SPDE）及其递归方差缩减变体（VR-SPDE），用于随机非凸-(强)凹极小极大优化，并在博弈稳定性和优化稳定性两种准则下给出随机一阶预言复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机非凸-(强)凹极小极大优化中，多循环方法虽能取得较好复杂度，但实现复杂；单循环方法复杂度较差。本文旨在保留单循环结构的同时，达到与多循环方法相当的复杂度保证。

Method: 提出单循环随机投影阻尼外梯度方法 SPDE，并结合递归方差缩减技术得到 VR-SPDE。方法基于无偏随机梯度预言（方差一致有界），VR-SPDE 额外假设随机梯度满足均方 Lipschitz 条件。

Result: SPDE 达到 ε-博弈稳定点的 SFO 复杂度为非凸-强凹 O(κε^{-4})、非凸-凹 O(ε^{-5})；VR-SPDE 分别改进为 O(κ^{3/2}ε^{-3}) 和 O(ε^{-9/2})。对于 ε-优化稳定点，SPDE 达到 O(κε^{-4}) 和 O(ε^{-6})，VR-SPDE 达到 O(κ^{3/2}ε^{-3}) 和 O(ε^{-6})，其中 κ=L/μ。

Conclusion: VR-SPDE 的优化稳定性复杂度与多循环方法已知最优界匹配，同时保持单循环实现；在各自稳定性准则和问题类别下，本文结果提供了单循环随机一阶方法中已知最优的 SFO 复杂度保证。

Abstract: We develop single-loop stochastic projected damped extragradient methods for stochastic nonconvex--(strongly) concave minimax optimization, with complexity guarantees for both game stationarity (GS) and optimization stationarity (OS). Our approach combines a stochastic projected damped extragradient (SPDE) method with a recursive variance-reduced variant, VR-SPDE, both of which retain a single-loop structure. Under an unbiased stochastic gradient oracle with uniformly bounded variance, SPDE finds an $\varepsilon$-game-stationary point with stochastic first-order oracle (SFO) complexities of $O(κ\varepsilon^{-4})$ and $O(\varepsilon^{-5})$ in the nonconvex--strongly concave and nonconvex--concave settings, respectively, where $κ=L/μ$. Under an additional mean-square Lipschitz condition on the stochastic gradients, VR-SPDE improves these GS complexities to $O(κ^{3/2}\varepsilon^{-3})$ and $O(\varepsilon^{-9/2})$, respectively. For an $\varepsilon$-optimization-stationary point, SPDE achieves SFO complexities of $O(κ\varepsilon^{-4})$ and $O(\varepsilon^{-6})$, while VR-SPDE achieves $O(κ^{3/2}\varepsilon^{-3})$ and $O(\varepsilon^{-6})$, in the two settings, respectively. These OS guarantees match the best-known bounds achieved by multi-loop methods while preserving a single-loop implementation. To the best of our knowledge, our results provide the best-known SFO complexity guarantees among single-loop stochastic first-order methods for the respective stationarity criteria and problem classes.

</details>


### [131] [Distributed Intraday Energy Management for 16.7-Hz Railway Power Systems---Part II: Software Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21802)
*Navid Noroozi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出Part I中两层能量管理系统数学模型的Python可执行实现（BahnstromEMS），详述软件行为与数值契约，性能对比留待Part III。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Part I提出的两层（日前+日内）铁路能量管理系统数学模型落地为可执行、可审计的软件，填补数学建模与工程实现之间的缺口，并明确软件行为与数值契约。

Method: 软件包含：时刻表层（小时级运行数据转15分钟时刻表，计算净牵引功率与可用再生功率）；电价处理层（缓存或经ENTSO-E Transparency Platform获取）；日前规划层（确定性小时级日前计划，支持must-run转换器承诺模式与子小时可行性证书模式）；日内层（按区域二次规划+consensus ADMM的风险中性日内场景MPC）；S1预测层（一周季节朴素点预测+因果残差路径重采样）。并规定日前与日内数据契约、绝对UTC耦合、局部QP组装、热启动、残差检验、求解器状态解释、物理验证恢复路径、状态与KPI更新、铁路自有发电与抽水增量。

Result: 实现了可执行且可审计的Python包：含版本号、依赖锁、冻结计划清单、确定性测试夹具、运行日志与哈希清单，保证软件配置可复现、可审计。

Conclusion: 本文聚焦软件行为与数值契约的描述，不提供比较性能与压力测试结论，相关评估预留于Part III。

Abstract: This paper presents the executable Python implementation~\cite{noroozi2026bahnstromems} of the two-layer energy-management system developed mathematically in Part~I~\cite{norooziP1I}. If needed, the software first converts hourly railway operation data into a 15-minute timetable and calculates the amount of gross motoring and available regenerative powers in a 15-minute resolution using the timetable handling layer. Given the energy prices are available either cached or by sending request to ENTSO-E Transparency Platform through the price handling layer, the software constructs a deterministic hourly day-ahead plan with either in the so-called must-run converter commitment mode or in a sub-hourly feasibility certificate mode, where the latter demands more computational complexity. Given the inputs from the timetable, day-ahead planning layers and initial measurements of the intraday-resolutions network's physical quantities, the intraday handling layer executes a risk-neutral intraday scenario model predictive controller by area-wise quadratic programming and consensus ADMM. The retained S1 forecast layer combines a one-week seasonal-naive point forecast with causal residual-path resampling, while the current measured stage is identical in all scenarios. We specify the day-ahead and intraday data contracts, absolute-UTC coupling, local-QP assembly, warm starts, residual tests, solver-status interpretation, physically validated recovery paths, state and key-performance-index updates, and the railway-owned generation and pumping increment. The Python package version, dependency lock, frozen-plan manifests, deterministic fixtures, run logs, and hash manifests make the software configuration executable and auditable. The paper describes software behavior and numerical contracts; comparative performance and stress-test claims are reserved for Part~III~\cite{norooziP1III}.

</details>


### [132] [Distributed Intraday Energy Management for 16.7-Hz Railway Power Systems---Part I: Theoretical Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21808)
*Navid Noroozi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文（三篇系列论文的第一部分）为16.7 Hz单相铁路电力系统的能量管理提出分布式日内情景规划器，建立了双层能量管理系统的数学基础，将日内能量规划建模为风险中性凸随机优化问题（稀疏凸二次规划），采用分布式情景模型预测控制递归求解，并提出一种在精确保持小时能量目标的前提下，由时刻表与列车运行信息估计微观牵引/再生功率曲线的数学严谨方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 16.7 Hz单相铁路电网由多个控制区互联而成，其日内能量管理需要在日前计划、储能测量与多源预测基础上进行决策；同时，实际电网的详细列车运行与微观牵引、再生功率曲线因数据隐私与安全原因通常无法公开获取，导致精确仿真与数值验证缺乏数据。因此需要一套可分布式实施、且不依赖敏感微观数据的日内规划方法与数据估计手段。

Method: 1) 面向由多个控制区互联的16.7 Hz单相铁路电网，建立双层能量管理系统数学基础；2) 在给定日前计划、路侧电池储能最近测量值以及牵引、可用再生与可再生能源预测的前提下，对日内时间尺度的铁路电网进行数学建模；3) 将日内能量规划表述为风险中性凸随机优化问题，并证明其整体为稀疏凸二次规划；4) 采用分布式情景MPC框架递推求解，推导各区子问题、协调更新、收敛条件以及闭环关键性能指标的累积方式；5) 提出将因果时刻表与列车运行信息转换为15分钟牵引/再生功率曲线的数学估计方法，同时精确保持各小时能量目标。

Result: 日内能量规划被证明等价于稀疏凸二次规划问题；该问题可通过分布式情景MPC递归求解，并给出了区域子问题、协调更新、收敛条件及闭环KPI累积的理论推导；所提微观功率曲线估计方法可在精确保持每小时能量目标的同时，由时刻表和列车运行信息生成15分钟粒度的牵引与再生功率曲线；相关方法实现并测试为开源Python包 bahnstrom_ems v0.9.1。

Conclusion: 该系列第一部分为16.7 Hz单相铁路电力系统的分布式日内情景规划奠定了数学基础：以稀疏凸二次规划刻画日内能量规划，并通过分布式情景MPC实现递归求解，同时提供了在数据受限条件下生成微观牵引/再生功率曲线的可行途径；成果以开源Python包形式实现，为后续两部分工作提供支撑。

Abstract: In a series of three papers, we propose a distributed intraday scenario planner for energy management in 16.7-Hz single-phase railway power systems, which is realized and tested as open-source Python package \texttt{bahnstrom\_ems v0.9.1}~\cite{noroozi2026bahnstromems}. The first part of our work develops the mathematical foundation of a two-layer energy management system for a 16.7-Hz single-phase railway power network, where the network is an interconnection of several control areas. Given a day-ahead plan, last measurement of stored energy in wayside batteries, and forecasts of the tractive, available regenerative and renewable energy, we provide a mathematical modeling of the 16.7-Hz single-phase railway power network in intraday time-scale. Then the intraday energy planning is formulated as a risk-neutral convex stochastic optimization problem. The overall intraday planning is shown to be a sparse convex quadratic program. Hence, this problem is solved recursively by adopting a distributed scenario model predictive control (MPC) setting. The corresponding area problems, coordination updates, convergence conditions, and closed-loop key-performance-index accumulation are derived. In practice, detailed train-motion and microscopic motoring and regenerative-power profiles are usually needed for accurate simulations and numerical validation purposes. Due to data privacy and security concerns, detailed train-motion and microscopic motoring and regenerative-power profiles in a real power network may not be publicly available. Therefore, we also propose a mathematically-solid approach to estimate such microscopic information by converting causally timetable and train-motion information into quarter-hour motoring and regenerative-power profiles while exactly preserving each hourly energy target.

</details>


### [133] [A Tight Surrogate-Based Adaptive Step Size for the Multiobjective Conditional Gradient Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21831)
*Wang Chen,Yong Zhao,Liping Tang,Xinmin Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对多目标条件梯度（MCondG）方法中自适应步长偏保守的问题，提出利用各目标函数梯度Lipschitz常数构造更紧的二次代理函数，并在有限候选集上评估得到新步长策略，保持下降性的同时改进最坏情况复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCondG通过最小化一维凸二次代理函数导出自适应步长，保证每次迭代所有目标函数充分下降。但该代理函数由所有目标函数梯度中最大的Lipschitz常数主导，导致步长过小、算法收敛变慢，因此需要设计更紧的代理函数以加快收敛。

Method: 观察到原代理函数的保守性源于使用统一的最大Lipschitz常数，作者构造了一个利用各目标函数各自梯度Lipschitz常数的更紧二次代理函数；基于该代理函数，在有限候选集上评估并选取步长，提出新的自适应步长策略。

Result: 理论上证明了新步长策略保持MCondG方法的下降性质，并具有比原始自适应步长策略更优的最坏情况复杂度界；在基准和实际问题上的数值实验表明，该策略在计算效率和求解质量方面均有效。

Conclusion: 通过利用个体梯度Lipschitz常数构造更紧代理函数并配合有限候选集搜索步长，可在不牺牲下降性质的前提下缓解原步长过小的问题，提升MCondG的收敛速度与实用性。

Abstract: The multiobjective conditional gradient (MCondG) method is an effective descent-type algorithm for solving constrained multiobjective optimization problems. In this algorithm, the adaptive step size is derived by minimizing a one-dimensional convex quadratic surrogate function, which guarantees a sufficient decrease in all objective functions at each iteration. However, we observe that this surrogate function is relatively conservative, producing overly small step sizes and consequently slowing the convergence of the algorithm. This conservativeness stems from the fact that the surrogate function is dominated by the largest Lipschitz constant among the gradients of all objective functions. Motivated by this observation, we develop a tighter quadratic surrogate function that exploits the individual gradient Lipschitz constants. Based on this surrogate, we propose a new adaptive step size strategy obtained by evaluating this surrogate over a finite candidate set. We prove that the proposed step size preserves the descent property of the MCondG method, and has an improved worst-case complexity bound compared with the original adaptive step size strategy. Numerical experiments on benchmark and real-world problems demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed strategy in terms of computational efficiency and solution quality.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond Shadow Weights: Quantization-Aware Training as Quantized-Endpoint Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21834)
*Sheng-An Xu,Hanyang Li,Jianhao Ma,Ying Cui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将QAT重新解释为有限网格端点动力学，提出残余相位理想化模型与跨越定律，并由此设计直接作用于量化码的QAR框架，在无需全精度影子权重的情况下取得与QAT相当或更优的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对QAT的解释多从影子权重x出发（平坦盆地、量化振荡鲁棒性、影子损失与量化误差的权衡），无法直接解释"部署端点损失f(Q(x))改善而影子损失f(x)不降甚至显著上升"这一经验现象，因此需要新的理论视角。

Method: 将QAT视为有限网格上的端点动力学；基于预训练权重重标度后近似正态的观察，为残余相位（影子权重在量化单元内的相对位置）建立理想化模型，导出决定影子更新后哪些坐标跨越量化边界的跨越定律；结合理想化模型与QAT中的信号失衡现象，提出直接作用于量化码的QAR框架，并为幂放大函数族给出可行梯度界（受限于不可避免的有限网格下界），无需保留全精度影子权重副本。

Result: 在大型语言模型后训练实验中，结果与端点视角一致；QAR在更小内存开销下可达到与QAT相当或更好的性能，同时具备理论保证与内存高效性。

Conclusion: QAT的成功可由部署端点而非影子权重的动力学来解释；直接操作量化码的QAR为量化感知训练提供了理论可证且内存高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) updates a full-precision shadow weight $\mathbf{x}$ but deploys the quantized endpoint $Q(\mathbf{x})$. Existing explanations for QAT largely view its success through the lens of shadow weights: QAT can move $\mathbf{x}$ toward flatter basins, gain robustness from quantization-induced oscillations, or balance the shadow loss $f(\mathbf{x})$ against the quantization error $\|\mathbf{x}-Q(\mathbf{x})\|_2$. These perspectives do not directly explain the empirical observation that the deployed endpoint loss $f(Q(\mathbf{x}))$ improves while the shadow loss $f(\mathbf{x})$ does not, and can even increase substantially. In this paper, we offer a different explanation by treating QAT as finite-grid endpoint dynamics. Motivated by the approximate normality of rescaled pretrained weights, we propose an idealized model for the residual phase, which records where each shadow weight sits inside its quantization cell as a fraction of the cell width. This model leads to a crossing law that determines which coordinates cross quantization boundaries after a shadow update. Inspired by the idealized model and signal-imbalance phenomenon in QAT, we further propose QAR (Quantization with Amplified Routing), an algorithmic framework that directly operates on the quantization code. In contrast to QAT, QAR is both theoretically grounded and memory-efficient: it admits feasible-gradient bounds for a family of power amplifiers up to unavoidable finite-grid floors without retaining a full-precision shadow weight copy. Experiments on post-training of large language models provide evidence consistent with the endpoint view and show that QAR can be comparable to or better than QAT with smaller memory cost.

</details>


### [135] [Application of Optimal Inexact Second-Order Acceleration to Distributed Stochastic Optimization under Statistical Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21878)
*Yury A. Sokolov,Maxim K. Mashtaler,Alexander V. Gasnikov,Martin Takáč,Dmitry I. Kamzolov,Artem D. Agafonov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究固定样本预算下分布式随机凸优化，利用局部Hessian统计相似性，提出分布式重启加速非精确Newton外推梯度法，达到统计精度N^{-1/2}，通信轮数为Õ(max{N^{1/7},m^{1/4}})，较先前工作将样本量依赖从N^{1/6}改进为N^{1/7}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式随机凸优化中，通信轮数是主要瓶颈。已有加速三次Newton方法虽降低通信复杂度，但对总样本量的依赖仍为Õ(N^{1/6})；作者希望在保持统计最优精度N^{-1/2}的前提下，进一步减少通信轮数对样本量的依赖，并避免Hessian通信开销。

Method: 采用样本平均近似将问题化为正则化有限和问题，利用局部Hessian的统计相似性，用服务器端局部目标的Hessian作为全局目标的非精确Hessian，worker仅通信梯度。基于Chen et al. (2026)的最优加速非精确Newton外推梯度法，针对强凸经验问题提出其分布式重启变体，每轮迭代使用两次梯度聚合。

Result: 该方法达到统计精度N^{-1/2}，通信轮数为Õ(max{N^{1/7},m^{1/4}})。当m=N^{4/7}时仅需Õ(N^{1/7})轮，优于Agafonov et al. (2021)加速三次Newton构造的Õ(N^{1/6})；每次迭代仅需两次梯度聚合，无需Hessian通信。

Conclusion: 该分布式重启加速非精确Newton外推梯度法在固定样本预算下实现了更优的通信-统计权衡，将样本量依赖从N^{1/6}改进至N^{1/7}，同时保持统计最优精度并避免Hessian通信，提升了分布式随机凸优化的通信效率。

Abstract: We consider distributed stochastic convex optimization with a fixed budget of $N$ independent samples split among $m$ workers. Sample average approximation reduces the problem to a regularized finite-sum problem whose local Hessians are statistically similar. This allows the Hessian of the local objective at the server to be used as an inexact Hessian of the global objective, while the workers communicate only gradients. We apply the optimal accelerated inexact Newton extragradient method of (Chen et al., 2026) and propose its distributed restarted variant for the strongly convex empirical problem. The method reaches the statistical accuracy of order $N^{-1/2}$ in $\widetilde O\left(\max\{N^{1/7},m^{1/4}\}\right)$ communication rounds. Hence, with $m=N^{4/7}$ workers, it requires $\widetilde O\left(N^{1/7}\right)$ rounds, improving the dependence on the total sample size from $\widetilde O\left(N^{1/6}\right)$ for the previous accelerated cubic Newton construction of (Agafonov et al., 2021). Each iteration uses two gradient aggregation rounds and does not require Hessian communication.

</details>


### [136] [Near-Optimal Acceleration for Smooth $\ell_p$ / $\ell_q$ Nondual Convex First-Order Oracle Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21880)
*David Martínez-Rubio,Brian Bullins,Cristóbal Guzmán,Mathieu Molina*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在 $\ell_q$ 球约束下具有 $(L,\kappa-1)$-Hölder 连续梯度的凸目标优化，耦合选择子的移动界与 Hölder 下降，给出多项式时间一阶方法，误差为 $\widetilde O_{\kappa,p,q}(LR^\kappa/T^{\kappa(1+1/p-(1/q-1/2)_+)-1})$，解决了 COLT 2015 开放问题（忽略对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在解决 Guz15 在 COLT 2015 提出的开放问题，即在高维情形下为 Hölder 连续梯度凸优化获得最优一阶方法收敛率，突破既有 Lipschitz 凸优化方法的局限。

Method: 方法基于 (MG26) 提供的高维凸嵌套集追逐选择子及其移动界，将选择子的移动界与 Hölder 下降相结合，构造多项式时间一阶方法；适用于 $1<\kappa\le 2$、$p<q$、高维 $T\le d$ 且 $p<\min\{q,2\}$ 的情形。

Result: 在 $T\le d$ 的高维情形、$p<\min\{q,2\}$ 时，$T$ 次一阶查询后可行输出误差为 $\widetilde O_{\kappa,p,q}(LR^\kappa/T^{\kappa(1+1/p-(1/q-1/2)_+)-1})$；在 $(p,q)=(1,2)$ 处速率为 $\widetilde{O}(LR^\kappa/T^{2\kappa-1})$，光滑情形（$\kappa=2$）达到 $\widetilde{O}(LR^2/T^3)$ 三次衰减。

Conclusion: 该结果以对数因子意义下解决了 COLT 2015 开放问题，给出了高维 Hölder 连续梯度凸优化的近最优一阶收敛率，并涵盖光滑情形下已有的三次衰减率。

Abstract: We study the optimization of convex objectives with $(L,κ-1)$-Hölder-continuous gradients in $\ell_q$ over $R B_p^d$, $1<κ\le 2$. (MG26) provides selectors with a movement bound for the problem of chasing high-dimensional convex nested sets for every $p<q$ and generally reduces Lipschitz convex optimization to bounds on the movement of selectors. We couple that movement with Hölder descent yielding a polynomial-runtime first-order method whose feasible output, in the high-dimensional regime $T\le d$ and for $p<\min\{q,2\}$, has error $$
  \widetilde O_{κ,p,q}\!\left(
  \frac{LR^κ}{T^{κ(1+1/p-(1/q-1/2)_+)-1}}
  \right), $$ after $T$ queries to a first-order oracle, solving the COLT 2015 open problem of (Guz15), up to logarithmic factors. At $(p,q)=(1,2)$, the rate is $\widetilde{O}(LR^κ/T^{2κ-1})$, including $\widetilde{O}(LR^2/T^{3})$ cubic decay in the smooth case.

</details>


### [137] [An $Ω(κ_y^8ε^{-6})$ Lower Bound for Stochastic NC-SC Bilevel Optimization with First-order Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21905)
*Zhihao Gu,Qilong Wu,Junchi Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了非凸-强凸随机双层优化问题达到ε-稳定点的oracle复杂度下界为Ω(Δκ_y²ε⁻²max{1,σ²κ_y⁶ε⁻⁴})，在噪声主导区间为Ω(Δσ²κ_y⁸ε⁻⁶)，从而证实了已有最优一阶随机方法ε⁻⁶依赖的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化中上层非凸、下层强凸的问题在机器学习（如超参数优化、元学习）中广泛存在。已有的最优一阶随机方法达到ε⁻⁶的复杂度，但该依赖是否为最优、是否存在更优算法尚不清楚。本文旨在建立该问题的一阶oracle复杂度下界，判定ε⁻⁶依赖的最优性。

Method: 考虑返回上下层目标无偏随机梯度的随机一阶oracle，其方差上界为σ²。针对任意初始最优性间隙Δ>0和足够小的ε>0，构造困难实例并利用信息论/算法下界分析技术（如随机oracle框架下的极小极大下界论证），证明任意自适应随机一阶算法所需oracle查询数的下界，下界以Δ、κ_y、ε和σ表达。

Result: 证明了任意自适应随机一阶算法找到超目标函数ε-稳定点需 Ω(Δκ_y²ε⁻²max{1, σ²κ_y⁶ε⁻⁴}) 次oracle查询；在噪声主导区间为Ω(Δσ²κ_y⁸ε⁻⁶)。该下界与最优已知一阶随机方法的ε⁻⁶复杂度相匹配。

Conclusion: 本文建立的非凸-强凸随机双层优化的oracle复杂度下界，证明了现有最优一阶随机方法在ε依赖上（特别是噪声主导下的ε⁻⁶）已达到最优，无法通过改进算法进一步降低ε阶复杂度。

Abstract: We study the oracle complexity of finding $ε$-stationary points of smooth bilevel optimization problems with a nonconvex upper-level objective and a strongly convex lower-level problem. We consider a stochastic first-order oracle that returns unbiased stochastic gradients of both the upper- and lower-level objectives, with variance bounded by $σ^2$. We prove that, for any initial optimality gap $Δ>0$ and all sufficiently small $ε>0$, every adaptive randomized first-order algorithm requires $Ω\!\left(Δκ_y^2 ε^{-2}\max\{1,σ^2κ_y^6ε^{-4}\}\right)$ oracle queries to find an $ε$-stationary point of its hyper-objective function, where $κ_y$ denotes the condition number of the lower-level problem. In particular, in the noise-dominated regime, the lower bound is $Ω\!\left(Δσ^2κ_y^8ε^{-6}\right)$. This establishes the optimality of the $ε^{-6}$ dependence achieved by the best-known first-order stochastic methods.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Little Red Dots As Super-Eddington Fountain Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20920)
*Nicholas Kaaz,Eliot Quataert,Taya Govreen-Segal,Hanpu Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出"小红点"(LRDs)是偏离轴向观测的超爱丁顿吸积超大质量黑洞，被缓慢的准球对称"光子疲惫"风包裹；通过辐射转移模拟成功再现了从"小蓝点"到V形、红色LRDs的观测多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRDs光谱常显示出蓝移约100-300 km/s的吸收槽，暗示存在缓慢外流风；然而超爱丁顿吸积盘本应驱动快达10^3-10^4 km/s的高速风，这一速度矛盾构成核心谜题，需要解释LRDs低速风的物理起源及其光谱特征。

Method: 作者将LRDs解释为离轴观测、被缓慢风包裹的超爱丁顿SMBH：被困光子将内盘吹胀为准球对称包层并驱动慢风，而快风沿极向逃逸。采用理想化的球对称"边缘束缚"(v~v_esc)与"光子疲惫"风模型，输入辐射转移代码Sirocco，研究10^6 M_☉ SMBH吸积光在风中如何被再处理。

Result: 模型将大部分风描述为"巴尔末茧"——一个康普顿厚区域，其中由巴尔末连续谱光子(hν>3.4 eV)而非莱曼连续谱光子(hν>13.6 eV)的耗尽维持气体电离。光谱覆盖LRD类源范围：低外流率(~2.5 M_☉/yr)对应"小蓝点"；中等率(~5-10 M_☉/yr)对应带巴尔末断点的V形LRDs；高率(~15 M_☉/yr)对应带完整断点的红色LRDs。巴尔末线剖面呈现宽指数翼上的P Cygni特征，与观测一致。

Conclusion: 相比于LTE模型，该机制可在更低风密度下产生断点，因为莱曼α俘获维持了n=2氢原子布局数，电子散射增强了光学厚度。相关论证同样适用于超大质量恒星或准星的风。

Abstract: Little red dots (LRDs) may be powered by supermassive black holes (SMBHs) accreting above the Eddington limit. Spectroscopy of LRDs often shows absorption troughs blueshifted by $\sim100-300\,{\rm km\,s^{-1}}$, implying a slow wind. This is puzzling: super-Eddington disks drive much faster winds, $\gtrsim10^3-10^4\,{\rm km\,s^{-1}}$. We argue that LRDs are super-Eddington SMBHs viewed off-axis and engulfed in a slow wind that covers most sight-lines; the fast wind escapes near the poles. Trapped light puffs the inner disk into a quasi-spherical envelope that launches the slow wind. The wind may be ``photon-tired'', meaning the light only barely unbinds it. Such marginally unbound winds lead to fountain flows, where some gas escapes and the rest falls back. We model the envelope with idealized, spherically symmetric ``marginally unbound'' ($v\sim v_{\rm esc}$) and ``photon-tired'' winds. We feed these profiles into the radiative transfer code Sirocco to study how the wind reprocesses the light from an accreting $10^6\,M_\odot$ SMBH. We describe most of the wind as a ``Balmer cocoon'' -- a Compton-thick region in which depletion of Balmer continuum photons ($hν>3.4\,{\rm eV}$) rather than Lyman continuum photons ($hν> 13.6\,{\rm eV}$) keeps the gas ionized. The spectra span the range of LRD-like sources: ``little blue dots'' at lower outflow rates ($\sim2.5\,M_\odot\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$); V-shaped LRDs with a Balmer break ($\sim5-10\,M_\odot\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$); and red LRDs with full breaks ($\sim15\,M_\odot\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$). Our Balmer line profiles show P~Cygni features atop broad, exponential wings, as is observed. The break is possible at lower wind densities than in LTE models because Lyman~$α$ trapping sustains our $n=2$ hydrogen population and electron scattering enhances the optical depth. Our arguments are also applicable to winds from supermassive stars or quasi-stars.

</details>


### [139] [Systematic Uncertainties and Their Impact on Gamma-Ray Searches for Dark Matter in Dwarf Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20944)
*Toni Bertólez-Martínez,Dan Hooper,Arifa Khatee Zathul*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 矮星系伽马射线观测是暗物质湮灭最强约束之一，但J因子系统不确定性常被忽略；模拟表明Δlog₁₀J~0.5–1.0的不确定性会使叠加分析中位上限削弱1.4–3.2倍，并导致过度排除真值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矮星系伽马射线观测已接近可检验银河系中心伽马射线超出的暗物质解释所需的灵敏度，但结果高度依赖推断的暗物质分布（J因子与空间轮廓）。文献中多数J因子测定忽略了多种系统不确定性，且测量离散度大于所报误差，暗示实际不确定性可能达Δlog₁₀J~0.5或更大，因此需量化这些未建模不确定性对暗物质湮灭截面约束的影响。

Method: 综述矮星系J因子测定中的系统不确定性来源，包括恒星成员资格、未分辨双星、偏离平衡与球对称假设，以及恒星和暗物质密度轮廓的假设。并使用模拟的Fermi-LAT观测集合，对27个矮星系组成的叠加样本进行观测模拟，量化这些未建模不确定性对暗物质湮灭截面约束的影响。

Result: 将Δlog₁₀J~0.5或1.0的不确定性以平方和方式加入后，叠加分析的中位上限制分别削弱约1.4倍或3.2倍。低估这些不确定性会使分析以高于名义置信水平的频率排除真实湮灭截面，即出现欠覆盖（undercoverage），在叠加分析中尤为明显，且即使所采用与真实的J因子不确定性一致时仍存在。将空间延展晕误建模为点源还会进一步强化限制并提高真实信号被错误排除的概率。

Conclusion: 矮星系J因子的系统不确定性不可忽略，普遍应给予Δlog₁₀J~0.5或更大的误差；忽略或低估这些误差会显著削弱叠加分析的约束力并造成统计欠覆盖，从而导致真实暗物质信号被错误排除，因此相关暗物质约束的解释需审慎对待这些系统效应。

Abstract: Gamma-ray observations of Milky Way dwarf galaxies provide some of the strongest constraints on dark matter annihilation and are approaching the sensitivity required to test dark matter interpretations of the Galactic Center Gamma-Ray Excess. The results, however, depend critically on the inferred dark matter distributions in these systems, conventionally described by their $J$-factors and spatial profiles. We review several sources of systematic uncertainty in dwarf galaxy $J$-factor determinations, including stellar membership, unresolved binaries, departures from equilibrium and spherical symmetry, and assumptions regarding the stellar and dark matter density profiles. Most $J$-factor determinations reported in the literature neglect several of these uncertainties, and the resulting measurements are often more dispersed than their quoted uncertainties would suggest. This indicates uncertainties of order $Δ\log_{10}J\sim0.5$ or larger may be warranted for many of these systems. Using an ensemble of simulated Fermi-LAT observations of a stacked sample of 27 dwarf galaxies, we quantify how these unmodeled uncertainties affect the resulting constraints on the dark matter annihilation cross section. We find that adding an uncertainty of $Δ\log_{10}J\sim 0.5$ or 1.0 in quadrature weakens the median upper limits of a stacked analysis by factors of 1.4 or 3.2, respectively. Furthermore, underestimating these uncertainties can cause such analyses to exclude the true annihilation cross section more frequently than the nominal confidence level would imply. Such undercoverage is especially pronounced in stacked analyses and persists even when the adopted and true $J$-factor uncertainties agree. Mismodeling spatially extended halos as point sources, as is sometimes done, further strengthens the resulting limits and increases the probability that true signals will be incorrectly excluded.

</details>


### [140] [Self-Similar Mass Spectra of Hierarchical Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21033)
*Liam Blum,Stefano Profumo,Ryan Schantz,Quoc Ha Tran*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 将重复合并黑洞的质量分布视为凝聚（coagulation）问题，用单一核函数统一描述合并率对质量与宇宙时间的依赖，导出晚期谱的标度律与分类，并应用于原初黑洞的四种标准双星形成通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复合并的黑洞种群在其质量分布中留下了合并历史记录，但此前缺乏一个统一框架来刻画不同合并环境下的晚期质量谱演化，也缺乏对合并环境随质量偏好程度的系统分类。

Method: 把层级合并建模为凝聚问题：单一核函数编码合并率对参与质量和宇宙时间的依赖；推导晚期谱渐进形式，给出闭式标度律、一个可精确求解的基准情形，并用单一指数对合并环境分类（该指数衡量合并是偏好还是抑制大质量参与者）。将文献中的合并率（尤其是原初黑洞情形）翻译成核函数形式，对四种标准双星形成通道进行分类；并对十三个抑制型、非凝聚（nongelling）核进行数值求解，同时计入每次合并辐射的能量。

Result: 三个标准双星形成通道对应超线性核，属于失控增长候选情形——最重天体占主导且不存在稳定的质量守恒谱；要确证物理凝聚（gelation）仍需完整的种群依赖核函数及其有限凝聚历史，本文未予定论。仅早期三体通道映射到非凝聚区，允许缓慢自相似增长。十三个抑制型非凝聚核的数值解表明：谱形并不由标度指数单独决定；计入每次合并辐射的能量会明显改变高质端截断，并减少种群的总黑洞质量。

Conclusion: 凝聚框架为层级合并黑洞种群提供了统一的标度律、可解基准和环境分类，并可用于原初黑洞各形成通道的定性判别；但物理凝聚的确认依赖于完整种群依赖核与有限合并历史，尚待解决。此外，谱形不由标度指数唯一确定，能量辐射效应在高质截断与总质量预算上不可忽略。

Abstract: Black holes that merge repeatedly carry a record of that history in their mass distribution. We treat hierarchical merging as a coagulation problem, in which a single kernel encodes how the merger rate depends on the masses involved and on cosmic time, and we derive the late-time spectra that such populations approach. The framework yields closed scaling laws, an exactly solvable benchmark, and a classification of merger environments by a single exponent measuring how strongly mergers favor or suppress massive participants. Applying it to primordial black holes requires care in translating published merger rates into kernel form; done correctly, three of the four standard binary-formation channels map onto superlinear kernels and are therefore candidates for runaway growth, in which the heaviest objects would dominate and no steady mass-conserving spectrum exists; establishing physical gelation, however, requires the full population-dependent kernel and its finite coagulation history, neither of which we settle here. Only the early three-body channel maps into the nongelling regime and admits slow self-similar growth. Numerical solutions across thirteen suppressive, nongelling kernels show that the shape of the spectrum is not fixed by the scaling exponent alone, and that accounting for the energy radiated at each merger measurably changes the high-mass cutoff and drains the population's total black-hole mass.

</details>


### [141] [A mechanism for fast radio bursts and their narrow spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21076)
*Andrei M. Beloborodov,Ivan Demidov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可在磁星爆发中运作的快速射电暴（FRB）辐射机制，能产生宽谱与窄谱。脉冲内等离子体以 Γ∝r 加速，环境kHz波受激散射被提升 Γ² 倍并转为径向GHz辐射，指数放大微弱种子光至观测亮度，并预测存在超强微秒成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB 的辐射机制尚不明确，尤其是如何自然解释其宽广与狭窄的频谱特征，以及如何在磁星磁层环境中高效产生并逃逸出可观测的 GHz 辐射。

Method: 考虑磁星爆发从内磁层 r₀~10⁷ cm 发射电磁脉冲，脉冲内等离子体向外加速，洛伦兹因子 Γ(r)≈r/r₀；脉冲在 r~10¹⁰ cm 离开磁层前穿过活跃磁星磁层中的环境 kHz 波。作者分析这些波在脉冲内发生受激散射，借助 Γ² 增强被转换为径向 GHz 辐射，并研究该过程的指数放大与饱和辐射区。

Result: 受激散射将微小 GHz 种子指数放大至极高光度，与观测到的 FRB 相符；产生的 GHz 爆发位于爆炸脉冲内部，有助于逃逸至远处观测者；FRB 持续时间和能量由脉冲持续时间和能量控制；在饱和辐射区，预测爆发包含一个超强微秒成分。

Conclusion: 该机制能够统一解释 FRB 的宽/窄谱、GHz 辐射产生与逃逸，并给出可由脉冲参数调控的持续时间与能量，同时预测饱和区存在微秒级超强成分，可作为观测检验。

Abstract: We find an emission mechanism for fast radio bursts (FRBs) capable of producing broad and narrow spectra. It operates in magnetar explosions, which launch an electromagnetic pulse from the inner magnetosphere at radii $r_0\sim 10^{7}$ cm. Plasma inside the pulse accelerates outward with Lorentz factor $Γ(r)\approx r/r_0$. Before exiting the magnetosphere at radius $r\sim 10^{10}$ cm, the pulse passes through ambient kHz waves that populate the magnetospheres of active magnetars. We show that the waves experience stimulated scattering inside the pulse, with the boost factor $Γ^2$ converting them into radial GHz radiation. This process exponentially amplifies tiny GHz seeds to extreme luminosities consistent with observed FRBs. The produced GHz burst rides inside the explosion pulse, which helps it escape to distant observers. The FRB duration and energy are controlled by the pulse duration and energy. In a saturated emission regime, the predicted burst contains an ultrastrong microsecond component.

</details>


### [142] [The Auger Radio Infill SKALA Extension (ARISE): First Air-Shower Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21105)
*Carmen Merx for the Pierre Auger Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: ARISE阵列于2025年部署于Pierre Auger天文台，利用18个SKALA v2天线在50–350 MHz探测约100 PeV以上的近垂直宇宙线空气簇射，首次观测到与SD-433事件符合的射电脉冲，验证了射电与表面探测器重建方向的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在100 PeV及以上能区实现近垂直宇宙线空气簇射的射电探测，需要评估并验证新型射电阵列的探测能力。

Method: 在Pierre Auger天文台的433 m间距表面探测器阵列（SD-433）的一个站点周围部署18个SKALA v2天线组成ARISE阵列，由SD-433提供触发；在50–350 MHz频段探测射电发射，并将射电信号与SD-433事件进行符合分析，比较二者重建的到达方向。

Result: 首次测量中探测到与广延大气簇射相关的射电脉冲，射电信号与SD-433事件符合，射电与表面探测器重建的到达方向一致，证实了ARISE对空气簇射的测量。

Conclusion: 该观测完成对探测器设置的初步评估，展示了ARISE在近垂直空气簇射射电探测方面的潜力。

Abstract: The Auger Radio Infill SKALA Extension (ARISE) was installed at the Pierre Auger Observatory in 2025 to detect the radio emission in the band of $50-350\,$MHz of near-vertical cosmic-ray air showers of energies around and above $100\,$PeV. The array comprises 18 SKALA~v2 antennas deployed around one of the surface-detector stations of the $433\,$m array (SD-433), which provides the trigger for ARISE. We present first measurements with ARISE and show the detection of radio pulses associated with extensive air showers. Radio signals are found in coincidence with SD-433 events, and a comparison of the arrival directions reconstructed from the radio and surface detector signals confirms the measurement of air showers with ARISE. These observations provide a first assessment of the detector setup and demonstrate the potential of ARISE for radio detection of near-vertical air showers.

</details>


### [143] [A method for enhanced gamma-proton discrimination with imaging atmospheric Cherenkov telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21635)
*Jianling Liu,Hu Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文提出基于切伦科夫光子发射位置横向分布的新观测量，结合似然比方法提升成像大气切伦科夫望远镜的伽马-质子鉴别能力，在1 TeV附近较传统Hillas宽度提升约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甚高能伽马天文的关键挑战是有效区分伽马光子与本底（主要是质子）。空间 calorimeter 在1 TeV附近可达10^4–10^5的抑制能力，而地面成像切伦科夫望远镜在同能段仅约10，优化空间大。

Method: 使用CORSIKA与sim_telarray模拟H.E.S.S.探测器响应，从切伦科夫簇射图像重建光子发射位置；借鉴空间calorimeter分析思路，构建基于切伦科夫光子发射位置横向分布的新观测量；采用似然比方法跨多望远镜组合单镜变量，并与传统Hillas分析对比。

Result: 基于发射位置横向分布的观测量对切伦科夫图像尺寸依赖更弱，单镜伽马-质子鉴别优于Hillas宽度参数；似然比结合宽度显著优于传统mean reduced scaled width：1 TeV附近提升约一个数量级，10 TeV以上提升仍超50%。

Conclusion: 利用光子发射位置横向分布并结合似然比方法可显著改进地面成像切伦科夫望远镜的伽马-质子鉴别性能，缩小与空间量能器之间的差距。

Abstract: Purpose: Very-high-energy gamma-ray astronomy is an important window to study the extreme astrophysical processes in the universe, and how to effectively distinguish between gamma photons and background signals (mainly protons) is a key technical challenge to realize very-high-energy gamma-ray detection. Spaceborne calorimeters record secondary particle spatial distributions, achieving a background rejection power of 10000-100000 near 1 TeV. Ground-based imaging Cherenkov telescopes measure Cherenkov photon angular distributions, yet they only reach a rejection factor of about 10 in this energy range, leaving substantial room for optimization. Methods: We use CORSIKA and sim_telarray to simulate H.E.S.S. detector responses. Photon emission positions are reconstructed from Cherenkov shower images. Inspired by analysis techniques for space-borne calorimeter, we propose new observables derived from the transverse distribution of Cherenkov photon emission positions to separate gamma-ray signals from proton backgrounds. We employ a likelihood-ratio method to combine single-telescope variables across multiple telescopes and compare its performance with conventional Hillas-based analysis. Results: The results show that observables built from the transverse distribution of Cherenkov photon emission positions are less sensitive to Cherenkov image size and outperform the Hillas width parameter for single-telescope gamma-proton discrimination. Furthermore, the likelihood ratio combining width yields substantially better performance than the conventional mean reduced scaled width: the improvement reaches roughly one order of magnitude near 1 TeV, and this enhancement remains above 50% for energies above 10 TeV.

</details>


### [144] [Energy Partition in Relativistic Electron-Positron-Ion Reconnection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21118)
*Nilay Mancini,Luca Comisso,Lorenzo Sironi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文将单粒子轨道动力学与集体等离子体响应耦合，建立解析理论并用PIC模拟验证，揭示了电子-正电子-离子等离子体中磁重联的能量分配规律：轻子平均能量标度为σ*，离子在非相对论逃逸时标度为μ^{1/3}(σ*)^{2/3}，超相对论逃逸时趋近轻子能量；离子能量份额可远低于等分假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论磁重联作为粒子加速的主要机制已在电子-离子和电子-正电子对等离子体中广泛研究，但在电子-正电子-离子等离子体中耗散能量如何在各粒子组分间分配，尤其是离子如何在以对等离子体为主的层中被加速，仍缺乏理解。

Method: 发展了一个将单粒子轨道动力学与集体等离子体响应耦合的解析理论，并用跨越对主导到电子-离子组分范围的粒子-细胞（PIC）模拟进行验证。

Result: 粒子在电场主导区域被直接加速，直到重联磁场将其偏转至外流；特征逃逸长度由含离子贡献的重联加热混合物的相对论趋肤深度设定。以σ*表示每粒子可用磁能（单位m_ec^2）、μ=m_i/m_e，轻子平均能量标度为σ*；离子非相对论逃逸时平均能量标度为μ^{1/3}(σ*)^{2/3}，超相对论逃逸时趋近轻子能量。预测的能量份额在不同组分、质量比和磁化参数下与模拟吻合。

Conclusion: 离子在耗散能量中的份额可远低于通常假设的离子-轻子等分，从而减少了重联驱动的天体物理源中可用于强子和中微子辐射的能量。

Abstract: Relativistic magnetic reconnection, a prime mechanism for particle acceleration, has been extensively studied in electron--ion and pair plasmas, but how the dissipated energy is shared among species in electron--positron--ion plasmas, and in particular how ions are energized within a pair-dominated layer, remains poorly understood. We develop an analytic theory that couples single-particle orbit dynamics to the collective plasma response, and we validate it with particle-in-cell simulations spanning pair-dominated to electron--ion compositions. Particles are directly energized in electric-dominated regions until the reconnected magnetic field deflects them into the outflow; the characteristic escape length is in turn set by the relativistic skin depth of the reconnection-heated mixture, including the ion contribution. Writing $\sigmastar$ for the magnetic energy available per particle in units of $m_ec^2$ and $μ=m_i/m_e$, the mean lepton energy scales as $\sigmastar$, whereas the mean ion energy scales as $μ^{1/3}(\sigmastar)^{2/3}$ when ions escape non-relativistically from the electric-dominated regions and approaches the lepton energy when their escape is ultrarelativistic. The predicted energy fractions agree with simulations across composition, mass ratio, and magnetization. The ion share of the dissipated energy can fall far below the commonly assumed ion--lepton equipartition, reducing the energy available for hadronic and neutrino emission in reconnection-powered astrophysical sources.

</details>


### [145] [Effects of Physical Cooling on the Structure of Circumbinary Disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21136)
*Minjae V. Kim,Julian H. Krolik,Scott C. Noble*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对等质量超大质量双黑洞周围的环双星盘进行辐射MHD模拟，采用基于局域热力学平衡与扩散冷却的近似热力学模型，发现盘在r=5a内达到准稳态，其质量分布与无物理热力学模拟显著不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射压与辐射冷却在天体结构及动力学中常起重要作用，但以往环双星盘模拟多缺乏物理热力学处理，需考察辐射与磁场耦合对内区盘性质的影响。

Method: 对等质量超大质量双黑洞周围的环双星盘开展辐射MHD模拟，采用基于局域热力学平衡和扩散冷却的近似热力学模型，以刻画光学厚区内能演化。

Result: 盘在r=5a内达到准稳态；与无物理热力学模拟相比，方位平均面密度的局部极大值与团块被完全抹除，时均内边缘更偏心，垂直密度分布强烈依赖半径。这些变化归因于冷却驱动的垂直压缩增强了磁场。

Conclusion: 内区环双星盘的基本性质对辐射与磁物理均敏感。

Abstract: Radiation pressure and radiative cooling often play an important role in deciding the structure and dynamics of astrophysical objects. In this paper, we present a radiative MHD simulation of a circumbinary disk (CBD) around an equal-mass SMBBH. It employs an approximate thermodynamic model based on local thermodynamic equilibrium and diffusion cooling which captures how the internal energy evolves in an optically thick region. The disk reaches a quasi-steady state within $r=5a$ whose mass distribution differs substantially from the one obtained from simulations without physical thermodynamics: the local maximum in the azimuthally-averaged surface density and the lump are completely erased; the time-averaged inner edge becomes more eccentric; and the vertical density distribution depends strongly on the radius. These changes are attributable to the enhancement of the magnetic fields caused by the cooling-driven vertical compression of the disk. Our result emphasizes that the basic properties of the inner CBD are sensitive to both radiation and magnetic physics.

</details>


### [146] [CRAFT HTR2: Polarimetry of 64 non-repeating fast radio bursts from the updated CRAFT catalogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21177)
*T. Dial,A. T. Deller,Marcin Glowacki,A. Bera,R. M. Shannon,Clancy W. James,Joscha N. Jahns-Schindler,Ziteng Wang,Akhil Jaini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: CRAFT在ASKAP上用CRACO系统新探测34例快速射电暴，使高时间分辨样本翻倍，比较了RM与偏振分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展CRAFT高时间分辨FRB目录，并借助CRACO更高灵敏度探测更暗弱的FRB群体，以检验不同望远镜频段下RM与偏振特性的异同。

Method: 利用ASKAP上CRAFT巡天（2024年5月至2026年6月）获取34个新FRB的高时间分辨谱偏振数据，多数由新调试的CRACO相干探测系统发现；将河外RM与线偏振度分布与CHIME、DSA目录进行对比。

Result: CRAFT与DSA的河外RM分布无显著差异，而低频探测的CHIME样本RM明显偏低；三个目录的线偏振度分布无显著差异。初步证据显示更宽、更暗的暴具有更低的偏振度，主要由几乎全部未偏振、且宽度≫10 ms、流量≪10^34 erg s^-1 Hz^-1的暴所驱动。

Conclusion: FRB的线偏振度分布似乎不依赖于观测频率；宽而暗的暴倾向于低偏振度，其中未偏振暴多为宽且暗的典型ASKAP探测之外的群体。

Abstract: We present high-time resolution spectro-polarimetric data for 34 new fast radio bursts (FRBs) discovered by the Commensal Real-time Fast Transients (CRAFT) survey on the Australian Square Kilometer Array Pathfinder (ASKAP) during the period May 2024 to June 2026. Most of these were detected by the higher-sensitivity CRAFT COherent (CRACO) detection system that was commissioned on the telescope during this period. This new sample doubles the size of the CRAFT HTR catalogue and probes a fainter population of FRBs thanks to the improved sensitivity of CRACO.
