We compare the distribution of extragalactic rotation measure (RM) and polarisation fraction to the CHIME and DSA catalogues. While no significant differences were seen between CRAFT and DSA, the extragalactic RM distribution seen in CHIME FRBs (which are detected at lower frequency) was substantially lower. Surprisingly, we find no significant differences in the linear polarisation fraction distribution between the three FRB catalogues, suggesting an indifference to the different telescope observing frequencies. We show tentative evidence for wider and fainter bursts possessing lower polarisation fractions; this is predominantly driven by the growing sample of unpolarised bursts that are, in almost all cases, wider ($\gg$10 ms) and fainter ($\ll$10$^{34}$ ergs s$^{-1}$ Hz$^{-1}$) than the median ASKAP detection.

</details>


### [147] [Neutrino Emission from Proton Photon Jet Interactions in Microquasars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21278)
*Theodoros Smponias*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作通过相对论磁流体力学模拟微类星体喷流，采用光线追踪生成合成中微子图像与光谱，并与现有及未来中微子探测器的灵敏度进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微类星体是银河系内高能粒子产生的候选场所，但其距离远、辐射区致密，直接观测约束受限，因此需要数值模拟来连接喷流动力学与可能的多信使信号。

Method: 对相对论磁流体力学微类星体喷流进行建模，聚焦加速质子与环境光子场相互作用产生的质子-光子通道中微子；未对质子-质子过程建模；对流体模拟输出施加光线追踪，从静止观测者视角生成合成中微子图像。

Result: 给出了合成中微子光谱和强度分布图，并与当前及未来中微子探测器的代表性灵敏度进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该框架可将喷流动力学与多信使中微子信号联系起来，为评估微类星体作为高能中微子源的潜力及探测器可观测性提供依据。

Abstract: Microquasars are candidate sites for high energy particle production within our galaxy. Because their distances and compact emission regions limit direct observational constraints, numerical simulations are useful for connecting jet dynamics with possible multi messenger signatures. This work models neutrino production from relativistic magnetohydrodynamic microquasar jets, focusing on the proton photon channel associated with interactions between accelerated protons and ambient photon fields. Proton proton interactions are acknowledged as a possible process in dense environments, but they are not modeled in the present calculation. A ray tracing procedure is applied to the hydrodynamic simulation output to produce synthetic neutrino images from the perspective of a stationary observer. Synthetic spectra and intensity maps are presented and compared with representative sensitivities of current and future neutrino detectors.

</details>


### [148] [Why most neutron star low-mass X-ray binaries accrete transiently: an evolutionary study of transient and persistent phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21324)
*Divyansh Tripathi,Sudip Bhattacharyya,Pulkit Ojha*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过计算中子星低质量X射线双星（NS LMXB）的长期演化，揭示其参数与相位如何决定暂现/持续吸积，自然产生更多暂现源，并与观测部分相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NS LMXB是研究强引力、超密物质和吸积-喷流过程的理想天体，但要可靠利用它们，必须理解为何部分源持续吸积、部分源暂现吸积。现有基于吸积盘热-黏性不稳定性的聚焦模型只解释暂现机制，缺乏对"哪些LMXB参数和相位导致暂现"以及"为何暂现源多于持续源"的全局认识。

Method: 对NS LMXB进行长期演化计算，从更宏观的视角考察LMXB参数与演化相位对吸积模式的调控。

Result: 计算结果自然产生比持续源更多的暂现源，并对LMXB参数与相位给出解释；与已知观测源性质部分一致，例如在较低轨道周期处通常得到更大的持续相比例，这与观测大体相符。

Conclusion: 现有聚焦模型（如热-黏性不稳定模型）不足以完全解释观测，需进一步改进；该长期演化计算为区分不同聚焦模型提供了新途径。

Abstract: A neutron star (NS) low-mass X-ray binary (LMXB), in which an NS accretes matter from a low-mass donor star, is an ideal source for probing some fundamental aspects of physics and astronomy, such as strong gravity, superdense matter, and the accretion-ejection processes. However, to reliably achieve these goals, one must adequately understand NS LMXBs, including why some accrete persistently and others transiently. Focused models, such as those based on a thermal-viscous instability in the accretion disk, are considered to explain transient accretion. However, broader perspectives, including which LMXB parameter values and phases cause transients and why there are more transients than persistents, remain poorly understood. Here, our computation of the long-term evolution of NS LMXBs addresses these questions, providing insight into LMXB parameters and phases, naturally producing more transients than persistents, and being partially consistent with the known properties of observed sources. For example, we typically find a greater fraction of persistent phase at lower orbital periods from the LMXB evolution computation, which is somewhat consistent with observations. However, a lack of full consistency calls for improving the aforementioned focused models, and our computations provide a new way to discriminate among these models.

</details>


### [149] [A Gamma-ray Emitting Head-Tail Radio Galaxy Swimming Through the Hot Medium of the Merging Cluster Abell 3627](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21343)
*Vaidehi S. Paliya,M. Böttcher,Swayamtrupta Panda,Kiran Wani,D. J. Saikia,Mahadev Pandge,C. S. Stalin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 首次将位于Abell 3627团外围的头尾射电星系ESO 0137-G007（z=0.016）与伽马射线源FL16Y J1615.5−6034证认关联，并给出其多波段辐射机制探讨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头尾射电星系是穿行于星系团致密介质的射电喷流活动星系核，因喷流通常以大视角观测而在伽马射线天区极为罕见；每一次新的伽马探测都为探究这类天体的高能辐射起源提供独特机会。

Method: 将头尾射电星系ESO 0137-G007与伽马源FL16Y J1615.5−6034进行位置证认；利用4.1 m SOAR望远镜获取光学光谱；由视向恒星速度弥散估算中心超大质量黑洞质量；并构建若干辐射模型拟合其宽波段能谱分布（SED）。

Result: 该星系穿行致密团环境产生的冲压剥离和/或团内介质湍流，形成了长度>500 kpc、呈扭曲摇摆状的射电喷流；光学光谱无发射线，表明吸积活动辐射效率低；测得中心黑洞质量为(3.36±1.32)×10^9 M☉。

Conclusion: 对ESO 0137-G007这一独特伽马发射头尾射电星系开展更深度的多波段观测，将有助于理解喷流与热团环境的相互作用，并为未来平方公里阵列（SKA）研究此类AGN奠定基础。

Abstract: Head-tail radio sources are jetted active galactic nuclei (AGN) moving through the dense intracluster medium of galaxy clusters. They are extremely rare in the gamma-ray sky, likely because they are usually observed at large viewing angles. Therefore, every new gamma-ray detection of head-tail radio galaxies allows us to probe the origin of the high-energy emission in this unique class of AGN. Here we report, for the first time, the association of ESO~0137-G007 (z=0.016), a head-tail radio galaxy located at the outskirts of the merging cluster Abell 3627, with the gamma-ray source FL16Y~J1615.5$-$6034. The motion of the galaxy through the dense cluster environment and subsequent ram pressure and/or intracluster medium turbulence have led to the formation of a $>$500 kpc-long jet exhibiting a twisted, wiggly radio morphology. Its optical spectrum, obtained with the 4.1 m SOAR telescope, is devoid of emission lines, indicating that the underlying accretion activity is radiatively inefficient. From the measured line-of-sight stellar velocity dispersion, we estimate the mass of the central supermassive black hole to be $(3.36\pm1.32)\times10^9$ \Msun. We briefly discuss several radiative models to explain the observed broadband spectral energy distribution of ESO~0137$-$G007. We conclude that deeper multi-wavelength observations of this unique gamma-ray emitting head-tail radio galaxy will enable us to better understand the interaction of the jet with the hot cluster environment, thus setting the stage for the research of this class of AGN with the upcoming Square Kilometer Array.

</details>


### [150] [Gamma-ray Quasi-periodic Oscillations in the Changing Look Blazar B2 1420+326 (OQ 334)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21411)
*Sunanda,Shubham Kishore,Alok C. Gupta*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文报告在变脸耀变体 B2 1420+326 的 Fermi-LAT 伽马射线光变曲线中探测到周期约 10–13 天的暂现准周期振荡（QPO），发生于 2026 年 5 月 7 日至 8 月中旬的耀发期间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变脸耀变体的伽马射线辐射机制尚不清楚，暂现 QPO 可为探测辐射区结构、喷流进动或吸积过程等物理机制提供关键线索。

Method: 使用 2026 年 1 月 1 日至 8 月 25 日的 Fermi-LAT 伽马射线光变数据，采用加权小波 Z 变换（WWZ）、广义 Lomb-Scargle 周期图（GLSP）和相位色散最小化（PDM）三种标准周期分析技术进行 QPO 搜索与确认。

Result: 在 2026 年 5 月 7 日至 8 月中旬的耀发期间探测到显著 QPO 信号，周期约 10–13 天；耀发前源呈现类似周期的弱阻尼振荡特征，阻尼时标约 20 天。

Conclusion: 基于所测周期时标与 QPO 特征，讨论了可能的物理机制以解释观测到的显著特征。

Abstract: In the Fermi-LAT gamma-ray light curve from January 1 to August 25, 2026, of the changing look blazar B2 1420+326, we report here the detection of transient quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) of period ranging around 10-13 d. The source entered the flare state around May 7, 2026, and lasted till around mid-August, 2026 during which it depicted strong QPO signatures. We utilized standard techniques, including weighted wavelet Z-transform analysis, generalized Lomb-Scargle periodogram, and phase dispersion minimization, to search for and confirm these findings. Prior to the flare, the source displayed a mild damped oscillatory feature of similar periodicity with a damping timescale of around 20 d. Based on the period timescale and nature of the QPOs detected, we discuss possible physical mechanisms for the observed salient features.

</details>


### [151] [Wave-particle acceleration during supernova explosions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21652)
*Zaza N. Osmanov,S. M. Mahajan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究超新星爆发早期电磁波与量子物质波共振相互作用驱动的粒子加速机制，发现其可将质子加速至最高约3×10^14 eV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超新星爆发极早期阶段的粒子加速过程，检验电磁波与粒子量子波共振相互作用这一机制能否有效加速质子。

Method: 针对典型前身星质量(8、12、20)倍太阳质量，建立电磁波与粒子量子波之间的共振相互作用模型，计算质子在此机制下的加速能量。

Result: 该机制可将质子加速至最高约3×10^14 eV；尽管光度持续下降，粒子能量先随上升、达到峰值后转而下降。

Conclusion: 共振加速机制能有效解释超新星早期质子加速，其能量随时间的先增后减特征是该机制固有行为的体现。

Abstract: We investigate particle acceleration during the early stages of a supernova explosion via a resonant interaction between electromagnetic waves and quantum waves associated with particles. For typical progenitor masses of $(8; 12; 20)\times M_s$, we find that this mechanism can accelerate protons to energies of up to $3\times 10^{14}$ eV. Interestingly, despite the continuously declining luminosity, the particle energy initially increases, reaches a maximum, and subsequently decreases, reflecting the characteristic behavior of the resonant acceleration mechanism.

</details>


### [152] [COBALT-BLUE: a fast and accurate model for gravitational self-lensing binary systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21708)
*Danny J. Dixon,Adam Ingram,Matthew J. Middleton,Adam McMaster,Grzegorz Wiktorowicz,Hugh Dickinson,Allison Crossland,Eric C. Bellm*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: COBALT-BLUE 是一个快速公开的光变曲线模型，用于描述非吸积双星中亮星受致密伴星引力自透镜等效应引起的光变，可多波段自洽计算；将其拟合到 14 个 ZTF 候选源自透镜事件后发现最佳参数不合理，判定这些耀发更可能是恒星耀发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一个能同时刻画非吸积双星中亮星多种光变物理效应（引力自透镜、多普勒增亮、椭球变形、偏心轨道、波长相关临边昏暗）的快速公开模型，以便仅凭测光数据约束双星参数（如致密星质量），并系统搜索巡天数据中的双星运动信号。

Method: 构建 COBALT-BLUE 模型：纳入引力自透镜、多普勒增亮、椭球变化、偏心轨道几何与波长相关临边昏暗；采用基于恒星大气模拟的非线性临边昏暗律计算自透镜耀发轮廓，实现由同一组物理参数在多波段自洽生成光变曲线，并提供简化临边昏暗选项以降低计算开销；随后将模型拟合到 14 个候选自透镜源的 ZTF 数据。

Result: 在足够侧向（edge-on）的系统中，模型预测出现尖锐的周期性自透镜耀发，以及耀发之外相位锁定的轨道调制。对 14 个 ZTF 候选源的拟合显示，模型能较好描述多数候选源的耀发形态，但最佳拟合参数不具备物理合理性。

Conclusion: 观测到的耀发最可能是恒星耀发而非引力自透镜事件。未来计划用该模型系统搜索光学巡天数据中的双星运动特征。

Abstract: We present COBALT-BLUE, a fast public model for the optical light curve of a luminous star in a non-accreting binary with a compact object. The model includes gravitational self-lensing, Doppler beaming, ellipsoidal variations, eccentric orbital geometry, and wavelength-dependent limb darkening. In sufficiently edge-on systems, it predicts sharp periodic self-lensing flares together with phase-locked orbital modulation outside the flare. We improve upon earlier models by including non-linear limb darkening laws based on stellar atmosphere simulations to calculate the self-lensing flare profile. This enables light curves to be self-consistently calculated in multiple wavebands from one set of physical parameters, thus offering the prospect of constraining binary parameters such as compact object mass from photometry alone if multiple self-lensing flares are observed. We also include the option to utilise simpler limb darkening prescriptions to save computational expense. As a proof of principle, we fit our model to ZTF data of 14 candidate self-lensing sources. The model describes the flare morphology well for most candidates, but we find that the best fitting parameters are not physically plausible. We conclude that the observed flares are most likely stellar flares and not self-lensing events. In future, we plan to use the model to systematically search optical survey data for signatures of binary motion.

</details>


### [153] [X-ray polarization of the Compton-thin Seyfert galaxy NGC 5506 with IXPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21711)
*Daniele Tagliacozzo,Alessandro Leonardo Lai,Andrea Gnarini,Giorgio Matt,Stefano Bianchi,Francesco Ursini,Andrea Marinucci,Alessia Tortosa,Sudip Chakraborty,Federico Ferretti,Vittoria Elvezia Gianolli,Elias Kammoun,Frédéric Marin,Herman Marshall,Riccardo Middei,Romana Mikusincova,Dawoon Kim*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用IXPE对Compton-thin塞弗特星系NGC 5506进行观测，2–8 keV偏振度上限为3.1%（99%置信度），结合多台X射线望远镜的谱偏振分析及MONK模拟表明，若冕为径向扩展结构，则要求倾角小于40°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活动星系核（AGN）X射线冕的几何结构尚不明确。X射线偏振测量可对冕的形状与观测倾角提供独立约束。NGC 5506作为典型的Compton-thin塞弗特星系，是检验冕几何与反射成分的理想目标，因此作者利用IXPE开展偏振观测并结合多波段数据与理论模拟加以限制。

Method: 使用IXPE对NGC 5506进行净曝光975 ks的观测，其中部分与NuSTAR（52 ks）和XMM-Newton（37 ks）同步。首先仅用IXPE数据建立简单基线模型，给出2–8 keV偏振度上限。随后联合三台X射线望远镜数据进行谱偏振分析，区分相对论反射、冷远距离反射与主连续谱幂律的贡献。最后使用辐射转移程序MONK进行Monte Carlo模拟，探索不同冕几何结构，并将结果与IXPE数据比较。

Result: 仅用IXPE数据的基线模型给出2–8 keV偏振度上限Π<3.1%（99%置信度）。联合谱偏振分析确认存在相对论反射与冷远距离反射以及主连续谱幂律，但对冕偏振度的约束依赖于具体假设而有所差异。MONK模拟与IXPE数据对比表明，在假设冕为径向扩展（与此前同类源观测一致）时，偏振上限要求倾角小于40°。

Conclusion: NGC 5506的X射线偏振度很低，表明其冕辐射的偏振信号被削弱或观测倾角较小。结合径向扩展冕的模拟，IXPE偏振上限对倾角给出约束（<40°），说明X射线偏振测量可有效限制AGN冕的几何与视角，未来更高灵敏度观测将进一步区分不同冕模型。

Abstract: We report on the observation of the Compton-thin Seyfert galaxy NGC~5506 performed with the Imaging X-ray Polarimetry Explorer (IXPE). The observation, with a net observing time of 975~ks, was partly simultaneous with \textit{NuSTAR} (52~ks) and \textit{XMM-Newton} (37~ks). Using a simple baseline model with IXPE data only, we find an upper limit on the 2--8~keV polarization fraction at the $99\%$ confidence level: $Π<3.1\%$. We then performed a spectropolarimetric analysis combining data from the three X-ray observatories. While pure spectral modeling confirms the presence of both relativistic and cold distant reflection, in addition to the primary continuum power law, their contributions lead to somewhat different constraints on the coronal $Π$, depending on the specific assumptions. We also performed Monte Carlo simulations with the radiative transfer code \texttt{MONK}, exploring various coronal geometries. Comparing these results with the IXPE data, and assuming a radially extended corona, as suggested by previous observations of similar sources, the corresponding polarization upper limits require inclination angles below 40°.

</details>


### [154] [Beyond Disk Truncation: X-ray Reverberation Signatures of an Outflowing Corona](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21732)
*Ke Qin,Bei You,Adam Ingram,Barbara De Marco*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用蒙特卡洛辐射转移模拟研究黑洞X射线双星中日冕外流的谱与时序效应，可用于区分外流日冕与截断盘两种情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬态下观测存在硬谱、弱盘反射及高于预期的偏振度，mildly relativistic outflowing corona（轻度相对论性外流日冕）被提出以解释这些现象，需系统考察其谱与时序特征。

Method: 构建由几何薄、光学厚的截断盘与具有指定体外流速度的椭球形日冕组成的模型，采用蒙特卡洛辐射转移模拟，计算不同外流速度与盘截断半径下的康pton化连续谱、盘反射成分及滞后-频率谱。

Result: 外流速度增大通过相对论集束效应减少反射比例，因更少康pton化光子照射盘面；当β≲0.5时高频软滞后对外流速度不敏感，而增大盘截断半径使零交叉频率ν0显著向低频移动。将该模型应用于MAXI J1820+070，其平台期异常的R-Γ反相关与日冕收缩伴随体速度增大定性一致；仅盘退行在模型假设下会给出相反的ν0演化。

Conclusion: 时序性质可帮助区分日冕外流情景与盘截断情景，未来的偏振计算将为扩展的外流日冕模型提供额外检验。

Abstract: The corona in black hole X-ray binaries (BHXRBs) is likely dynamic during outbursts. A mildly relativistic outflowing corona has been proposed to explain hard spectra, weak disk reflection, and the higher than expected polarization degree observed in the hard state of X-ray binaries. In this work, we investigate the spectral and timing effects of coronal outflow using Monte Carlo radiative-transfer simulations. The model consists of a geometrically thin, optically thick truncated disk and an ellipsoidal corona with a prescribed bulk outflow velocity. We calculate the Comptonized continuum, the disk reflection component, and the lag-frequency spectra for different outflow velocities and disk truncation radii. We find that increasing the outflow velocity reduces the reflection fraction through relativistic beaming, because fewer Comptonized photons irradiate the disk. For $β\lesssim 0.5$, the high-frequency soft lag is only weakly affected by the outflow velocity, whereas increasing the disk truncation radius shifts the zero-crossing frequency($ν_0$) to substantially lower frequencies. Thus, timing properties can help distinguish the coronal-outflow scenario from the disk-truncation scenario. We further apply the model to MAXI J1820+070 and find that its unusual $R-Γ$ anti-correlation during the plateau phase is qualitatively consistent with a contracting corona accompanied by increasing bulk velocity. Disk recession alone would predict the opposite evolution of $ν_0$ under the assumptions of our model. Future polarization calculations will provide an additional test of extended outflowing-corona models.

</details>


### [155] [Core-collapse supernovae 2022prr, 2023ucy, and 2024ljc in the luminous infrared galaxy NGC 6745](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21742)
*K. K. Matilainen,E. Kankare,T. Nagao,T. Reynolds,A. Efstathiou,S. Mattila,C. R. Angus,A. Reguitti,Y. -Z. Cai,M. Fraser,L. Galbany,M. González-Bañuelos,C. P. Gutiérrez,T. Kangas,T. L. Killestein,T. Kravtsov,P. Lundqvist,S. Moran,A. Pastorello,A. Popowicz,I. Salmaso,M. Stritzinger*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对近邻亮红外星系NGC6745中爆发的三颗核心坍缩超新星2022prr、2023ucy和2024ljc进行了光学与近红外分光光度跟踪观测，测定了宿主星系消光并界定其物理性质，同时估算了宿主星暴年龄（40 Myr）与本征核心坍缩超新星率（0.4±0.1 SN/yr）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三颗核心坍缩超新星（CCSNe）罕见地爆发于近邻亮红外星系（LIRG）NGC6745的中心区域，为研究星系核区极端环境下的超新星特性、宿主消光及星暴参数提供了难得的样本。

Method: 获取覆盖光学与近红外的分光光度跟踪数据；与其他富氢CCSNe对比，基于宽带测光导出各目标的宿主星系消光Av；构建宿主星系的光谱能量分布（SED），拟合不同星系组分以估算本征CCSN率和NGC6745星暴年龄；并用偏振观测分析2022prr的星周物质几何结构。

Result: 2022prr为类SN2009ip事件，光变曲线含两次连续明亮事件A、B，峰值分别为Mr=-14.8和-18.3 mag；近最大光时V、R波段偏振度高达P=2.8%，光谱由窄巴尔末发射线主导且随时间复杂演化，并呈现CIII、NIII、HeII的早期"闪光电离"特征。2023ucy为正常IIP型超新星。2024ljc为快速演化的IIb型超新星，+41 d时r波段较峰值下降约3.2 mag，而正常IIb型同期仅下降1.5±0.3 mag。三颗超新星虽爆发于LIRG中，但V波段宿主消光仅0–1 mag（中等程度）。SED建模给出宿主星暴年龄约40 Myr，本征CCSN率为0.4±0.1 SN/yr。

Conclusion: 三颗CCSNe在LIRG核区呈现多样的物理类型（类SN2009ip、正常IIP、快速演化IIb），尽管宿主为亮红外星系，其受到的宿主消光仅为中等水平（Av约0–1 mag）；宿主星暴年龄约40 Myr，本征CCSN率约0.4±0.1 SN/yr。

Abstract: The core-collapse supernovae (CCSNe) 2022prr, 2023ucy, and 2024ljc exploded within the central regions of the nearby luminous infrared galaxy (LIRG) NGC6745. We present our follow-up data with the aim to characterise the spectrophotometric evolution of these CCSNe, determine their host-galaxy extinction, and to define their physical nature. The presented spectrophotometric data set covers the optical and near-infrared region for our three supernovae (SNe). The data are analysed and compared to other hydrogen-rich CCSNe, and a host-galaxy extinction value Av is derived based on broadband photometry for each of the targets. A spectral energy distribution (SED) for the host galaxy is constructed, and a combination of different galactic components are fitted to the SED to estimate the intrinsic CCSN rate and the age of the starburst of NGC6745. 2022prr is a SN2009ip-like event, whose light curve evolution consists of two consecutive luminous events A and B with peak magnitudes of Mr = -14.8 and -18.3 mag, respectively. The high degree of V and R-band polarisation (P=2.8%) near maximum light suggests interaction with highly aspherical circumstellar material. The spectral evolution of the SN is dominated by narrow Balmer emission lines, which have a complex evolution over time. 2022prr joins the growing sample of SN2009ip-like events that show early `flash ionisation' features of CIII, NIII, and HeII. 2023ucy is found to be a normal Type IIP SN. However, 2024ljc is a rapidly evolving Type IIb SN with a post-maximum r-band decline of roughly 3.2 mag by +41 d whereas normal IIb SNe decline typically by 1.5+/-0.3 mag during the same timescale. While the events exploded in a LIRG, the SNe are obscured by only moderate V-band host extinctions ranging from 0 to 1 mag. Based on the SED modelling we estimate an age of the host starburst to be 40Myr, and the intrinsic CCSN rate to be 0.4+/-0.1 SN/year.

</details>


### [156] [Nonlinearly Causal General-Relativistic Two-Fluid Dissipative Magnetohydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21923)
*Elias R. Most,Samuel J. Dunham*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出广义相对论19矩耗散磁流体力学（GR 19-moment dissipative MHD）的完整表述与数值求解方案，涵盖一阶耗散项与电子自由度，给出非线性因果性与强双曲性条件，构建高分辨率激波捕捉格式、物理性强制方案，并通过耗散双流体Bondi吸积精确解及动力学PIC模拟结果进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论天体物理等离子体（如黑洞吸积、磁层）涉及跨多个数量级尺度的多流体耗散过程，现有GRMHD格式多局限于理想或简化耗散描述，难以一致捕捉黏性、热传导、电阻、Hall项及电子自由度，且缺乏严格的因果性、双曲性与物理性约束，故需一套完备且数值稳健的GR耗散多矩框架。

Method: 1) 建立GR 19矩耗散MHD表述，包含全部一阶耗散项（黏性、热传导、电阻、Hall）及电子理想自由度（数密度、动量、能量）；2) 推导受约束一阶19矩系统的非线性因果性充要条件与强双曲性充要条件；3) 发展高性能可移植的高分辨率激波捕捉格式，将所有演化方程（含耗散与电子部分）写成通量-散度形式；4) 采用隐式积分系统性地跨越动理学尺度（回旋频率、等离子体频率）；5) 构造守恒态物理可容许性的充要条件，并据此建立新的物理性强制格式；6) 推导GR耗散双流体Bondi吸积的全解作为精确验证基准。

Result: 该表述与格式能够在不全域分辨动理学尺度的前提下模拟跨数量级尺度系统；精确求解了GR耗散双流体Bondi吸积全解；与黑洞吸积及磁层动力学的动理学PIC模型结果对比，成功再现无量纲重联率约0.1以及类Braginskii各向异性压强和热流等全局反馈关键特征。

Conclusion: 所提出的GR 19矩耗散MHD框架在理论（因果性、双曲性、物理性）与数值（通量-散度格式、隐式跨尺度积分、物理性强制）两方面均具完备性，并通过精确解与PIC基准验证其正确性，可作为研究广义相对论天体物理等离子体全局耗散与电子效应的稳健工具。

Abstract: We present a formulation of general-relativistic (GR) 19-moment dissipative magnetohydrodynamics (MHD), capable of handling all first-order dissipative terms (viscosity, heat conductivity, resistivity and Hall terms), as well as all ideal electron degrees of freedom (number density, momentum and energy) in a GR astrophysical two-fluid plasma. We derive necessary and sufficient nonlinear causality conditions for the constrained first-order 19-moment system, as well as both necessary and sufficient conditions for strong hyperbolicity. To assess the formulation numerically, we develop a high-resolution shock-capturing scheme that solves these equations in a performance-portable fashion. The scheme expresses all evolution equations, including the dissipative and electron sectors, in flux-divergence form, allowing us to model systems with scales separated by orders of magnitude without resolving kinetic scales everywhere on the grid. In addition, we use implicit integration methods to systematically overstep kinetic scales in MHD regions, such as cyclotron and plasma frequencies. To make the scheme as robust as GRMHD codes, we construct necessary and sufficient conditions for a conserved state to be physically admissible, and based on these construct a new physicality-enforcement scheme. As an exact validation comparison, we formulate and derive a full solution to dissipative two-fluid Bondi accretion in general relativity. We then validate the equations against a series of results from kinetic particle-in-cell models of black hole accretion and magnetospheric dynamics, demonstrating that our formulation and scheme can correctly capture major features relevant for feedback on global scales of these solutions, including dimensionless reconnection rates of order $0.1$ and Braginskii-like anisotropic pressures and heat fluxes.

</details>

Weller,O. R. Williams,A. Zacchei,G. Zamorani,F. M. Zerbi,E. Zucca,J. García-Bellido,V. Scottez,F. Tarsitano,J. H. Knapen*

TL;DR: 结合LOFAR与Euclid Q1数据，在Euclid Deep Field North中识别出77个具有热点的强射电星系，其中7–15%可能有红外/光学热点对应体；最清晰样本的能谱与近均分磁场下电子加速至约1 TeV一致，并发现两个新的光学热点候选体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射电星系热点是高能粒子加速场所，但射电观测对电子能量分布限制有限，影响对其物理与能学的理解；光学同步辐射对应体可探测高电子能量，然而以往仅在少数射电极亮天体中研究，样本稀缺。

Method: 将LOFAR低频射电观测与Euclid Q1数据释放（Euclid Deep Field North）结合，交叉匹配识别具有热点的强射电星系，并搜寻其热点的红外与光学对应体；对最清晰样本拟合热点谱能量分布（SED），在近均分磁场假设下推断电子加速上限能量；比较未探测热点所需的电子能谱或亚均分磁场条件。

Result: 在77个强射电星系热点样本中，7–15%可能具有红外和光学对应体；最清晰案例的SED与场强近均分时电子加速至（但不超过）1 TeV一致；未探测热点则要么未达到如此高电子能量，要么需具有显著不同的电子能谱或明显亚均分磁场。发现两个新的光学热点候选体，与高分辨率LOFAR数据吻合；其中一个为迄今已知最高红移光学热点（z≈1.6），且是首次探测到重启源内瓣热点的光学对应体。

Conclusion: 该工作表明光学/红外热点探测可用于约束高能电子加速，并为统计研究奠定基础；未来结合Euclid全宽天区巡天与国际基线LOFAR，可首次对热点中的高能粒子加速进行大规模统计分析。

Abstract: The hotspots of powerful radio galaxies are the sites of high-energy particle acceleration, but radio observations give us limited information on the electron energy distributions that they generate, which affects our overall understanding of their physics and energetics. Optical synchrotron counterparts to radio galaxy hotspots, which probe high electron energies, have been known for many years, but have only been studied in a small number of objects selected as being very bright in the radio. By combining Low-Frequency Array (LOFAR) observations with the Euclid Q1 data release in the Euclid Deep Field North, we identify 77 powerful radio galaxies with hotspots and show that a small but significant fraction of them, 7--15\%, may have detected infrared and optical counterparts to their hotspots. The hotspot spectral energy distributions in our clearest cases are consistent with particle acceleration up to (but not beyond) 1\,TeV in energy on the assumption of field strengths close to equipartition, whereas non-detected hotspots either do not reach such high electron energies or would have to have very different electron energy spectra or significantly sub-equipartition magnetic field strengths. Two new candidate optical hotspots are clearly detected matching high-resolution LOFAR data; one is remarkable as both the highest-redshift optical hotspot known to date ($z \approx 1.6$) and also the first optical detection of the hotspot of the inner lobe of a restarting source. We discuss approaches to performing a large-scale study of hotspots with the full wide-area Euclid surveys, particularly when combined with the international baselines of LOFAR, enabling for the first time a statistical analysis of high-energy particle acceleration in hotspots.

</details>

### [158] [A Probabilistic Modeling Framework for Transient Debris Outcomes in Low Lunar Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21955)
*Arjun Chhabra,Ryne Beeson*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种概率框架，用于评估低月球轨道（LLO）中碎片在事件后数小时至百天时间尺度上的沉降（月面撞击）概率，替代高成本蒙特卡洛仿真，并揭示位置与碎片物理特性对结局的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLO碎片生成事件后的结局评估通常依赖计算昂贵的蒙特卡洛仿真，缺乏在短期瞬态时间尺度上解析化、可快速评估沉降与非沉降概率的手段，难以为风险评估与政策制定提供高效支撑。

Method: 以NASA标准解体模型为基础，构建沉降与非沉降概率的连续分布；引入太阳光压等摄动力，分析不同时间尺度下碎片结局的实现情况，形成对各类结局概率进行界定的解析框架。

Result: 短期时间尺度上，沉降概率随碎片生成事件发生位置的不同而变化；较长时间尺度上，碎片的物理特性与其沉降可能性之间存在关联；框架可给出各结局概率的解析边界，避免蒙特卡洛的高计算成本。

Conclusion: 该概率框架为LLO碎片结局评估提供了高效的解析替代方案，未来可纳入更大尺度轨道环境模型、任务风险评估流程与政策制定。

Abstract: This work presents a novel probabilistic framework for the assessment of orbital debris fragment outcomes in the low lunar orbit (LLO) regime over time horizons ranging from a few hours to a hundred days after a debris generation event. The framework develops continuous distributions for the probability of sinking (lunar collision) and non-sinking over these transient time horizons, building upon NASA's Standard Breakup Model to provide insights into the variations in the likelihood of debris outcomes across the LLO regime and offering an alternative to computationally expensive Monte Carlo simulations. The effects of perturbative forces such as solar radiation pressure are used to assess the realization of debris outcomes over varying time horizons, providing an analytical framework that bounds the likelihood of each outcome. Results indicate variations in the probability of sinking over short time horizons depending on the originating location of the debris generation event, as well as a link between the physical characteristics of fragments and their likelihood of sinking over longer time horizons. The future incorporation of this framework into broader-scale orbital environment models, mission risk assessment procedures, and policy development are briefly discussed.

</details>
