### [159] [Backward log orthogonal functions and their approximation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20901)
*Mahmoud A. Zaky*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类新的后向对数正交函数及其广义形式，用于求解在终端点具有弱奇异性的问题的后向谱逼近，并建立了完整的逼近理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多问题（如分数阶微分方程等）的解在终端点（terminal endpoint）呈现代数或对数型弱奇异性，直接用通常的多项式逼近会丢失指数或高阶收敛率。作者希望构造一组能自然处理终端奇异性、节点在奇异端自动聚集的非多项式加权基函数，从而恢复高阶/指数收敛性。

Method: 对广义Laguerre多项式施加终端点对数映射，构造后向对数正交函数（BLOF）及广义后向对数正交函数（GBLOF）。在此基础上建立系统的逼近理论：递推关系、导数公式、正交性、Sturm--Liouville刻画、映射的Laguerre--Gauss求积规则、加权投影估计、后向Lagrange插值估计、逆不等式，以及在由终端对数伪导数定义的加权Sobolev型空间中的稳定性。广义版本通过引入代数尺度参数增强逼近空间的灵活性，使其能更有效地表示奇异因子。

Result: 误差分析与数值实验表明，所提出的后向对数基函数特别适用于带终端端点奇异性、弱正则的函数，能够恢复通常多项式逼近所丢失的指数或高阶收敛率；广义形式可进一步提升对奇异因子的表示能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为终端弱奇异问题的后向谱逼近提供了有效框架和完备的理论工具，兼具理论完整性与数值有效性。

Abstract: We introduce a new class of backward logarithmic orthogonal functions and generalized backward logarithmic orthogonal functions, constructed by applying a terminal-endpoint logarithmic mapping to generalized Laguerre polynomials. These functions are designed for backward spectral approximations of problems whose solutions exhibit weak singularities at the terminal endpoint. The proposed basis functions generate non-polynomial weighted approximation spaces with nodes naturally clustered near the singular endpoint, and therefore provide an effective framework for resolving algebraic and logarithmic endpoint singularities. We develop the basic approximation theory for these backward logarithmic orthogonal functions, including recurrence relations, derivative formulas, orthogonality, Sturm--Liouville characterization, mapped Laguerre--Gauss quadrature rules, weighted projection estimates, backward Lagrange interpolation estimates, inverse inequalities, and stability properties in weighted Sobolev-type spaces defined through a terminal logarithmic pseudo-derivative. A generalized version of the basis is also introduced by incorporating an algebraic scaling parameter, which improves the flexibility of the approximation space and allows singular factors to be represented more effectively. The error analysis and numerical results show that the proposed backward logarithmic basis is particularly suitable for weakly regular functions with terminal-endpoint singularities and can recover exponential or high-order convergence rates that are typically lost when usual polynomial approximations are applied directly.

</details>


### [160] [Uniform Chebyshev asymptotics for repeated-pole rational approximation of the exponential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21028)
*Fei Xue,Tianqi Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文研究用指定重复极点 q_m>0 的有理函数 P_m(z)/(q_m-z)^m 在 (-∞,0] 上一致逼近 exp(tz)。借助 Möbius 变换化为 [-1,1] 上 F_λ(x)=exp(-λ(1-x)/(1+x)) 的多项式逼近，导出 Chebyshev 系数的双鞍点渐近展开，并通过块状系数论证把系数渐近转化为逼近误差。对 q_m=(α/t)m，0<α<3√3/2，最优一致误差为 m^{-1/2}H_e(α)^m，最优 α=1/√2 时为 m^{-1/2}(√2-1)^m；并给出 L^2 投影误差的显式振荡轮廓、精度-工作量二项定律及矩阵作用与 Krylov 界的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有理逼近 exp(tz)（t>0）在区域分解、指数积分器、Krylov 方法等中具有核心地位，而极点位置（设计）直接决定精度。已有结果多为粗阶估计，缺乏对前置因子、极点标度（fixed、sublinear、linear）和最优极点比的精细刻画，也缺少逼近误差与计算工作量、停机阶数之间的定量联系。因此需要发展前置因子精确的渐近理论。

Method: 1. 用 Möbius 变换把 (-∞,0] 上的有理逼近化为 [-1,1] 上 F_λ(x)=exp(-λ(1-x)/(1+x)) 的多项式逼近，λ=tq_m。2. 对 Chebyshev 系数推导双鞍点一致渐近公式，给出每个局部化复鞍点贡献的显式振幅、相位与相对余项，覆盖 fixed、sublinear、linear 极点标度且避开鞍点合并。3. 由于两个鞍点贡献可在单个系数中相消，转而考虑相邻系数构成的增长块，证明整块不能完全相消，从而把系数渐近传递为逼近误差。4. 对线性标度 q_m=(α/t)m 求最优一致误差的上下界（两侧阶），并分析最优比值 α=1/√2。5. 计算 Chebyshev 加权 L^2 投影误差的显式有界振荡轮廓，进而建立精度-工作量二项定律并量化极点设计与停机阶数的失配。

Result: 对 q_m=(α/t)m，0<α<3√3/2，最优一致误差具两侧阶 m^{-1/2}H_e(α)^m；在最优 α=1/√2 时误差为 m^{-1/2}(√2-1)^m。归一化 Chebyshev 加权 L^2 投影误差有显式有界振荡轮廓。所得前置因子精确估计给出二项精度-工作量定律，并量化极点设计阶数与停机阶数的不匹配。标量误差进一步给出自伴负半定矩阵的精确最坏情形矩阵作用基准，以及维数无关的 shift-and-invert Krylov 界。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了带指定重复极点的 exp(tz) 有理逼近的完整前置因子解析理论：双鞍点渐近、块状不可相消性、最优极点比与精确误差常数、L^2 投影振荡轮廓，并延伸到矩阵作用与 Krylov 复杂度的定量界，为极点设计与停机准则提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study uniform approximation of $\exp(tz)$, $t>0$, on $(-\infty,0]$ by rational functions $P_m(z)/(q_m-z)^m$ with a prescribed repeated pole $q_m>0$. A Möbius transformation reduces the problem to polynomial approximation of $F_λ(x)=\exp\!\left(-λ\frac{1-x}{1+x}\right)$ on $[-1,1]$, where $λ=tq_m$. We derive a uniform two-saddle asymptotic formula for the Chebyshev coefficients, including explicit amplitude and phase and a relative remainder for each localized complex saddle contribution, covering fixed, sublinear, and linear pole scalings away from saddle coalescence. Because the two saddle contributions can cancel in a single coefficient, we pass to a growing block of neighboring coefficients and prove that the whole block cannot cancel. This transfers the coefficient asymptotics to approximation errors. For $q_m=(α/t)m$, $0<α<3\sqrt3/2$, the best uniform error has two-sided order $m^{-1/2}H_e(α)^m$; at the optimal ratio $α=1/\sqrt2$ this becomes $m^{-1/2}(\sqrt2-1)^m$. The normalized Chebyshev-weighted $L^2$ projection error has an explicit bounded oscillatory profile. These prefactor-resolved estimates yield a two-term precision-to-work law and quantify mismatch between pole-design and stopping degrees. Finally, the scalar error gives an exact worst-case matrix-action benchmark for self-adjoint negative semidefinite matrices and dimension-independent shift-and-invert Krylov bounds.

</details>


### [161] [Energy stability and error estimates for a second-order structure-preserving exponential integrator method for smectic-A liquid crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21175)
*Wenshuai Hu,Guanghua Ji,Xiao Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为修饰的Landau--de Gennes型SmA液晶模型构造了二阶、线性、解耦且保结构的GSAV--ETD2数值格式，证明了无条件能量耗散律并在无τ-h耦合条件下得到最优误差估计O(τ²+h²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SmA液晶的Landau--de Gennes模型具有强非线性与高阶耦合特征，亟需兼具高精度、能量稳定性与低计算成本的数值方法；而二阶广义标量辅助变量(GSAV)指数积分器在能量稳定性分析与最优收敛性理论上尚存缺口。

Method: 将广义标量辅助变量(GSAV)方法与二阶指数时间差分Runge--Kutta(ETDRK2)离散相融合，构造二阶GSAV--ETD2格式，实现线性、解耦求解；通过能量方程分析证明无条件能量耗散律，并借助强制的离散重构(coercive discrete reformulation)进行全离散误差估计。

Result: 证明所提格式满足无条件能量耗散律；在τ与h之间无需任何耦合条件下获得全离散最优收敛阶O(τ²+h²)；数值实验验证了理论结果并模拟了SmA相的自组装动力学。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将GSAV与ETDRK2结合，弥补了该类二阶GSAV指数积分器能量稳定性分析的空白，并给出了无耦合约束的严格误差估计，为SmA液晶相变与自组装动力学的高效稳定模拟提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: In this work, we develop a second-order, linear, decoupled, and structure-preserving numerical scheme for the modified Landau--de Gennes model of smectic-A (SmA) liquid crystals. The main contributions are threefold. First, to the best of our knowledge, we propose the first integration of the generalized scalar auxiliary variable (GSAV) approach with a second-order exponential time-differencing Runge--Kutta (ETDRK2) discretization, leading to a second-order GSAV--ETD2 scheme. Second, we prove that the proposed scheme satisfies an unconditional energy-dissipation law, thereby closing the theoretical gap in the energy-stability analysis of second-order GSAV exponential integrators of this class. Third, by deriving a coercive discrete reformulation, we establish a fully discrete error estimate without imposing any coupling condition between $τ$ and $h$, achieving the optimal convergence rate $\mathcal{O}(τ^2+h^2)$. Numerical experiments are presented to verify our theoretical results and to simulate the self-assembly dynamics of the SmA phase.

</details>


### [162] [A Low-rank ADI Algorithm for the Numerical Solution of Large Discrete-time Non-symmetric Algebraic Riccati Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21282)
*Umair Zulfiqar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低秩ADI方法，用于求解解为低秩的大型离散时间非对称代数Riccati方程（DTNARE），无需显式求解任何投影DTNARE，并配备自动移位生成策略，在维度10^6至10^7的问题上验证了精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTNARE出现在博弈论中Nash均衡的计算中，但其大规模求解在计算上往往不可行。因此需要一种能处理大规模、解为低秩情形的数值方法。

Method: 引入低秩交替方向隐式（ADI）方法，递归构造低秩稳定化解，不显式求解任何投影DTNARE；借助低秩ADI迭代的极点配置性质，保证隐式求解的投影DTNARE始终存在稳定化解；并开发了ADI迭代的自动移位生成策略，用户只需提供初始移位。

Result: 在维度介于10^6与10^7之间的DTNARE上进行了数值实验，结果表明该方法具有较高的精度和效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的低秩ADI算法是求解大规模DTNARE的有效求解器，可处理原本计算上不可行的问题。

Abstract: Discrete-time non-symmetric algebraic Riccati equations (DTNAREs) arise in game-theoretic computations of Nash equilibria. Solving such equations at large scale is often computationally prohibitive. This paper develops a numerical approach for large-scale DTNAREs whose solutions are low rank. A low-rank alternating direction implicit (ADI) method is introduced that recursively constructs a low-rank stabilizing solution without explicitly solving any projected DTNARE. Through the pole-placement property of the low-rank ADI iteration, the method ensures that the implicitly solved projected DTNARE always admits a stabilizing solution. An automatic shift-generation strategy is also developed for the ADI iterations. Once an initial shift is provided, the algorithm computes the low-rank solution without further user intervention. Numerical experiments on DTNAREs with dimensions between \(10^6\) and \(10^7\) demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the method. The results confirm that the proposed low-rank ADI algorithm is an effective solver for large-scale DTNAREs that would otherwise be computationally prohibitive.

</details>


### [163] [Kernel-free Boundary Integral Methods for Allen-Cahn and Cahn-Hilliard Equations on Irregular Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21295)
*Xinru Liu,Pensong Yin,Wenjun Ying,Yulin Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的核无关边界积分（KFBI）框架，用于求解二维和三维不规则区域上带齐次无通量边界条件的 Allen-Cahn 与 Cahn-Hilliard 方程，实现二阶空间收敛、一阶时间收敛及离散质量守恒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Allen-Cahn 与 Cahn-Hilliard 方程常定义在不规则区域上，传统边界积分方法依赖核函数解析表达式，对复杂几何与多类子问题缺乏统一、高效的求解途径，且 Cahn-Hilliard 更新中难以兼顾质量守恒与边界处理的精度。

Method: 采用稳定的 IMEX 离散：Allen-Cahn 更新化为 Neumann 修正 Helmholtz 问题；通过辅助变量重构将 Cahn-Hilliard 更新化为同类子问题，避免计算非线性源的 Laplacian。实移位需两次顺序求解，复共轭移位可由单次复求解重构实解。所有子问题由同一 KFBI 求解器通过等价界面问题间接求势。针对 Cahn-Hilliard 更新的离散质量缺陷，构造适配 KFBI 不规则边界处理的几何加权离散 L2 质量投影，将外部重叠权重重分配到物理节点，排除辅助 KFBI 延拓值。

Result: 数值实验表明：空间上达到二阶收敛，成对时间误差指标呈一阶收敛，并保持预设离散质量。无源模拟中能量衰减，投影引起的累积能量扰动相对于观测耗散很小。

Conclusion: 所提统一 KFBI 框架可有效求解不规则区域上的 Allen-Cahn 与 Cahn-Hilliard 方程，兼顾精度、质量守恒与能量稳定性，具有处理复杂几何与多类子问题的通用性。

Abstract: A unified kernel-free boundary integral (KFBI) framework is proposed for the Allen-Cahn and Cahn Hilliard equations with homogeneous no-flux boundary conditions on two- and three-dimensional irregular domains. With a stabilized first-order implici-explicit (IMEX) discretization, the Allen-Cahn update reduces to a Neumann modified Helmholtz problem. An auxiliary-variable reformulation reduces the Cahn-Hilliard update to subproblems of the same type without evaluating the Laplacian of the nonlinear source. The Cahn-Hilliard update requires two sequential solves for real shifts, while complex-conjugate shifts allow the real-valued solution to be reconstructed from a single complex solve. All subproblems are handled by the same KFBI solver that indirectly evaluates potentials through equivalent interface problems. To correct discrete mass defects in the Cahn-Hilliard update, a geometry-weighted discrete $Lš$ mass projection is constructed tailored to the KFBI treatment of irregular boundaries. Exterior overlap weights are redistributed to physical nodes, excluding auxiliary KFBI extension values from the mass calculation. Numerical experiments demonstrate second-order spatial convergence, first order convergence of paired temporal-error indicators, and preservation of the prescribed discrete mass. The reported source-free simulations exhibit energy decay, with cumulative projection-induced energy perturbations remaining small relative to the observed dissipation.

</details>


### [164] [History-Compatible Energy-Stable Finite Element Schemes for Variable-Density Cahn--Hilliard--Navier--Stokes Flows on Evolving Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21328)
*Wenbin Wang,Yunqing Huang,Yin Yang,Huayi Wei*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对网格随时间变化（固定拓扑运动或拓扑改变重网格）的变密度CHNS方程，提出解耦的BE与BDF2格式，通过精确跨网格配对与相容历史表示实现稳定离散，数值实验验证了二阶收敛与能量稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变密度Cahn–Hilliard–Navier–Stokes（CHNS）离散中，若有限元网格在相邻时间层间发生变化，离散空间随时间改变，进入多步格式的相场、动能与压力历史量处于不同离散结构中，一般无法用单一算子传递。现有格式难以在此情形下保持能量稳定与守恒性，故需发展适用于变网格的稳定解耦格式。

Method: 构造解耦的向后Euler（BE）与二阶向后差分公式（BDF2）格式。将精确的跨网格物理配对与和相能、动能、压力梯度存储相容的历史表示相结合；相场更新确定Abels–Garcke–Grün相容的质量通量，用于动量输运；引入标量毛细交换方程分离相场与流体求解，同时保留离散能量交换。所得各场子问题为线性，标量方程具有唯一正解。

Result: 在各场子问题保持线性的前提下，标量毛细交换方程具有唯一正解；格式在所述容许性假设下无需时间步长限制即满足修正能量平衡。数值实验验证：两种网格更新下均达到时间二阶收敛、相质量守恒、无外力情形下修正能量衰减，以及与原格式可比的Rayleigh–Taylor与上升气泡动力学。

Conclusion: 该工作为变网格上的变密度CHNS提供了解耦、线性、能量稳定的BE与BDF2离散框架，兼顾跨网格历史传递的相容性与物理守恒性质，适用于固定拓扑运动与拓扑改变重网格两类网格更新。

Abstract: We consider variable-density Cahn--Hilliard--Navier--Stokes (CHNS) discretizations on finite element meshes that may change between accepted time levels through fixed-topology motion or topology-changing remeshing. When the discrete spaces vary in time, the phase, kinetic, and pressure histories entering a multistep scheme are measured in different discrete structures and cannot, in general, be transferred by a single operator. We develop decoupled backward Euler (BE) and second-order backward differentiation formula (BDF2) schemes by combining exact physical cross-mesh pairings with history representations compatible with the corresponding phase-energy, kinetic-energy, and pressure-gradient storages. The phase update also determines an Abels--Garcke--Grün-consistent mass flux used in the momentum transport. A scalar capillary-exchange equation separates the phase and fluid solves while retaining the discrete energy exchange. The resulting field subproblems are linear, the scalar equation has a unique positive solution, and the schemes satisfy modified energy balances without a time-step restriction under the stated admissibility assumptions. Numerical experiments confirm second-order temporal convergence under both mesh updates, phase-mass conservation, modified-energy decay in the unforced tests, and comparable Rayleigh--Taylor and rising-bubble dynamics.

</details>


### [165] [FrFNO:Injecting the analytic Mittag-Leffler propagator into a resolution-robust neural operator for space-time fractional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21512)
*Guofei Pang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出分数阶傅里叶神经算子（FrFNO），通过将冻结线性部分的Mittag-Leffler解析响应预计算为分辨率无关的传播子表并注入网络，仅对残差进行谱卷积学习，从而实现分辨率鲁棒的分数阶偏微分方程快速求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶偏微分方程同时耦合记忆依赖的时间导数和非局部分数阶拉普拉斯算子，在不同分数阶、初值或扩散系数场下反复求解计算代价高昂；而标准神经算子架构必须从数据中重新学习主导的线性分数阶演化，效率低下。

Method: 提出FrFNO，一种分辨率鲁棒的条件算子。将冻结线性部分的解析Mittag-Leffler响应作为一次预计算、分辨率无关的传播子表注入，仅在由变系数和非线性对流诱导的残差上训练谱卷积网络，并以分数阶连续条件化。理论上证明在零样本超分辨率下，注入的传播子填充了标准FNO置零的带外模态，将带外全场尾部替换为更小的残差尾部（弱扰动下为K无关的常数因子缩减）。

Result: 在非线性二维时空分数阶Burgers问题上，FrFNO在训练分辨率和零样本超分辨率下均取得六种基线（FNO、PINO、PDNO、CNO、DeepONet、U-Net）中最低的相对L2误差，谱相位误差最小，并在整数阶极限和长积分窗口下保持最优。该构造可扩展至耦合分数阶系统，如分数阶Allen-Cahn和Navier-Stokes方程。

Conclusion: FrFNO通过注入解析传播子并仅学习残差，显著提升了分数阶偏微分方程求解的精度与分辨率鲁棒性；残差与全解具有相同的Sobolev阶数，因此优势在于幅度缩减而非更陡的尾部，网格细化使误差收敛至残差控制的误差下限。

Abstract: Fractional partial differential equations couple a memory-dependent time derivative with a nonlocal fractional Laplacian, and their repeated solution under varying fractional orders, initial data, or diffusivity fields is computationally expensive. Neural operators offer a fast surrogate, but standard architectures must relearn the dominant linear fractional evolution from data. We propose the \emph{Fractional Fourier Neural Operator} (FrFNO), a resolution-robust conditional operator. It injects the analytic Mittag--Leffler response of the frozen linear part as a once-precomputed, resolution-independent propagator table, and trains a spectral convolutional network only on the residual induced by variable coefficients and nonlinear advection, conditioned continuously on both fractional orders. The central theoretical result is that under zero-shot super-resolution the injected propagator fills the out-of-band modes that a standard Fourier neural operator sets to zero, replacing the out-of-band full-field tail by the smaller residual tail (a $K$-independent constant-factor reduction under weak perturbation). The residual and the full solution carry the same Sobolev order, so the advantage is amplitude reduction rather than a steeper tail. Mesh refinement drives the error to a residual-controlled floor. On a nonlinear two-dimensional space--time fractional Burgers problem FrFNO achieves the lowest relative $L^2$ error among six baselines (FNO, PINO, PDNO, CNO, DeepONet, U-Net) at the training resolution and under zero-shot super-resolution, yields the smallest spectral phase error, and remains the best at the integer-order limit and for long integration windows. The same construction extends to coupled fractional systems such as fractional Allen-Cahn and Navier--Stokes equations. Code, training scripts, and reference outputs are available at https://github.com/Derek2021Pang/FrFNO.

</details>


### [166] [A Priori and A Posteriori Error Estimates for a Crank Nicolson Type Scheme of an Elliptic Problem with Dynamical Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21524)
*Rola Ali Ahmad,Toufic El Arwadi,Houssam Chrayteh,Jean-Marc Sac-Épée*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对Crank-Nicolson型时间离散与有限元空间离散的格式，建立了先验与后验误差估计，核心成果是分别对应时间离散和空间离散的两类误差指示子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建针对Crank-Nicolson型时间离散、有限元空间离散格式的误差估计框架，以区分并量化时间与空间离散各自引入的误差。

Method: 空间上采用有限元方法离散，时间上采用Crank-Nicolson型格式离散，据此推导先验误差估计与后验误差估计，并构造误差指示子。

Result: 建立了先验和后验两类误差估计，并给出两类误差指示子：一类与时间离散相关，另一类与空间离散相关。

Conclusion: 可为该数值格式提供误差控制依据，通过时间与空间误差指示子实现分别的误差评估，适用于自适应求解。

Abstract: In this article we claim that we are going to give a priori and a posteriori error estimates for a Crank Nicolson type scheme. The problem is discretized by the finite elements in space. The main result of this paper consists in establishing two types of error indicators, the first one linked to the time discretization and the second one to the space discretization.

</details>


### [167] [Element-wise Convergence Behavior of Subspace Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21540)
*Mingyang Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究子空间迭代的逐元素收敛行为，分别给出无Rayleigh–Ritz与有Rayleigh–Ritz情形下迭代矩阵元素模的精确渐近表达式与逐元素界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有子空间迭代收敛性分析多基于整体范数或子空间距离，缺乏对迭代矩阵各元素收敛速度的刻画。本文旨在给出逐元素层面的收敛行为描述。

Method: 设对角矩阵Λ=diag(λ_1,…,λ_n)，|λ_1|>…>|λ_n|>0，对初始矩阵X∈F^{n×m}施加温和假设：1）无Rayleigh–Ritz时，推导|Q_k(i,j)|的精确渐近表达式，系数由X的LU分解确定；2）有Rayleigh–Ritz时，对标准化Ritz向量Z_k给出逐元素界。结果对F=R与F=C均成立，经正交或酉基变换后适用于实对称或复正规矩阵。

Result: 无Rayleigh–Ritz：i≠j的元素按(|λ_max{i,j}|/|λ_min{i,j}|)^k衰减；|Q_k(j,j)|与1的偏差按max{|λ_j|/|λ_{j-1}|,|λ_{j+1}|/|λ_j|}^{2k}衰减。有Rayleigh–Ritz：i≥m+1的非对角元按(|λ_i|/|λ_j|)^k衰减；i≤m的非对角元按(|λ_{m+1}|^2/(|λ_i||λ_j|))^k衰减；|Z_k(j,j)|与1的偏差按(|λ_{m+1}|/|λ_j|)^{2k}衰减。

Conclusion: 本文给出了子空间迭代收敛行为的逐元素刻画，揭示了各元素独立的收敛速率；结果经正交或酉基变换可推广至实对称或复正规矩阵。

Abstract: This paper studies the element-wise convergence behavior of subspace iteration. All results are established for both \(\mathbb{F}=\mathbb{R}\) and \(\mathbb{F}=\mathbb{C}\). For a diagonal matrix \(Λ=\mathrm{diag}(λ_1,\ldots,λ_n)\) with \(|λ_1|>\cdots>|λ_n|>0\), we analyze the modulus of each entry of the iterative matrix sequence generated by subspace iteration, both without and with the Rayleigh--Ritz procedure. Under mild assumptions on the initial matrix \(X\in\mathbb{F}^{n\times m}\), we first derive exact asymptotic expressions for \(|Q_k(i,j)|\) in the subspace iteration without the Rayleigh--Ritz procedure: entries with \(i\neq j\) decay as \((|λ_{\max\{i,j\}}|/|λ_{\min\{i,j\}}|)^k\), and the deviation of \(|Q_k(j,j)|\) from \(1\) decays as \(\max\{|λ_j|/|λ_{j-1}|,|λ_{j+1}|/|λ_j|\}^{2k}\), with explicit coefficients determined by the LU factorization of \(X\). For the Rayleigh--Ritz variant, we obtain element-wise bounds for the normalized Ritz vectors \(Z_k\). Specifically, for \(|Z_k(i,j)|\), off-diagonal entries with \(i\geq m+1\) decay as \((|λ_i|/|λ_j|)^k\), off-diagonal entries with \(i\leq m\) decay as \((|λ_{m+1}|^2/(|λ_i||λ_j|))^k\), and the deviation of \(|Z_k(j,j)|\) from \(1\) decays as \((|λ_{m+1}|/|λ_j|)^{2k}\). These results give an element-wise description of the convergence behavior of subspace iteration. After an orthogonal or unitary change of basis, the results apply to real symmetric or complex normal matrices.

</details>


### [168] [Integral chemical reaction neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21715)
*Abraham Reyes-Velázquez,Stefan Güttel*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出iCRNN框架，用积分配点公式替代ODE前向求解，实现全代数前向传播，在基准化学反应网络中训练速度提升约2–4倍且更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从时间序列浓度数据中辨识化学反应网络（CRN）的结构与动力学是化学动力学的核心难题。现有方法依赖先验机理假设，或面临计算代价高、对噪声敏感的问题。标准CRNN需在每个训练步反复进行自适应ODE求解，计算昂贵且训练不稳定。

Method: 提出积分化学反应神经网络（iCRNN）：将CRNN的可解释、物理约束架构与动力学控制方程的积分配点格式相结合。不再逐步前向求解ODE，而是用数值求积直接近似所学速率函数的积分，使整个前向传播仅由矩阵运算构成，为纯代数形式；并给出恢复误差分析，刻画导致网络可辨识性受限的病态结构来源。

Result: 恢复误差分析揭示了限制参数可辨识性的结构来源：守恒律、反应可逆性以及共享反应物集合。在两个基准CRN上，对四物种机理，iCRNN训练墙钟时间约为基线CRNN的一半；对五物种机理，快3–4倍，同时每次训练均可靠完成，损失相当或更低。

Conclusion: iCRNN通过积分配点公式将ODE求解替换为纯代数运算，在保持CRNN可解释性与物理约束的同时，显著提升训练效率与稳健性，为从时间序列数据中辨识CRN结构与动力学提供了高效且可分析的途径。

Abstract: Discovering the structure and kinetics of chemical reaction networks (CRNs) from time-series concentration data is a fundamental challenge in chemical kinetics, with existing approaches relying on prior mechanistic assumptions or suffering from high computational cost and noise sensitivity. In this work, we present integral chemical reaction neural networks (iCRNNs), a framework that combines the interpretable, physics-constrained, architecture of chemical reaction neural networks (CRNNs) with an integral collocation formulation of the governing dynamics. Rather than solving ODEs forward in time at each training step, we approximate the integral of the learned rate functions directly using numerical quadrature. This yields an entirely algebraic forward pass consisting only of matrix operations, eliminating the repeated adaptive ODE solves of standard CRNN and producing smoother, more predictable training. We provide a recovery error analysis characterising the structural sources of ill-conditioning (conservation laws, reaction reversibility, and shared reactant sets) that limit network identifiability. On two benchmark CRNs, iCRNN trains in roughly half the wall-clock time of the baseline CRNN method on a four-species mechanism and between three and four times faster on a five-species mechanism, while completing every training run reliably and attaining comparable or lower loss.

</details>


### [169] [Finite Volume Element Method on Curved-Edge Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21719)
*Xiaoxiao Chen,Zexi Hu,Zhiming Gao,Junliang Lv,Xiang Wang,Hongtao Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文首次在曲边四边形网格上建立了高阶有限体积元方法的稳定性与最优收敛性分析。通过基于高斯点构造对偶网格，克服了几何形变与雅可比非均匀性导致的精度退化问题，证明了弱网格正则性条件下离散双线性形式的强制性，并得到能量范数与 $L^2$ 范数下的最优误差估计。数值实验验证了方法在多种曲边网格上的最优收敛性、对复杂曲边边界的逼近优势以及对畸变和界面大形变的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有限体积法理论主要基于直边网格，缺乏对曲边四边形网格上的稳定性与收敛性分析。曲边网格在逼近复杂曲边边界、处理系数突变和界面大形变方面具有潜在优势，但几何形变与雅可比非均匀性会导致精度退化，因此需要建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 提出曲边四边形网格上的高阶有限体积元方法；基于高斯点构造对偶网格以克服几何形变与雅可比非均匀性；在弱网格正则性条件下证明离散双线性形式的强制性，得到能量范数下的最优误差估计；利用正交性与 Aubin-Nitsche 技巧推导最优 $L^2$ 误差估计。

Result: 在能量范数（$H^1$）和 $L^2$ 范数下均获得最优误差估计。数值实验涵盖常系数与各向异性系数问题、不同对偶剖分策略、复杂曲边边界区域以及界面大形变问题，结果表明方法在多种曲边网格上一致达到最优收敛阶；相比直边网格，曲边网格在逼近复杂曲边边界上优势显著，并在系数突变与界面大形变情形下具有更好的抗畸变能力。

Conclusion: 本文为曲边网格上的有限体积元方法提供了统一的理论框架，验证了所提方法的效率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper proposes and analyzes a high-order finite volume element method on curved-edge quadrilateral meshes for elliptic equations. Unlike existing theories, which are primarily based on straight-edge meshes, this study is the first to establish an analysis of the stability and optimal convergence of the finite volume method on curved-edge meshes. By constructing a dual mesh based on Gaussian points, we overcome the accuracy degradation issues caused by geometric deformation and Jacobian non-uniformity of curved-edge meshes. We prove the coercivity of the discrete bilinear form under weak mesh regularity conditions, thereby obtaining an optimal error estimate in the energy norm. Furthermore, using orthogonality and the Aubin-Nitsche technique, we derive an optimal $L^2$ error estimate. Numerical experiments cover problems with constant and anisotropic coefficients, different dual partition strategies, complex curved boundary domains, and interfaces with large deformations. Numerical results indicate that this method consistently achieves the optimal convergence order in both the $H^1$ and $L^2$ norms on a variety of curved-edge meshes. Compared to straight-edge meshes, curved-edge meshes offer significant advantages in approximating complex curved boundaries and demonstrate better resistance to distortion in cases involving sudden changes in coefficients and large deformations at interfaces. This paper provides a unified theoretical framework for the finite volume element method on curved-edge meshes and verifies the efficiency and robustness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [170] [A Second-Order Maximum-Bound-Preserving and Energy-Stable Exponential Time-Differencing Method for Allen--Cahn-Type Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21820)
*Wenshuai Hu,Guanghua Ji,Xiao Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于广义标量辅助变量（GSAV）与二阶指数时间差分Runge–Kutta（ETDRK2）的无条件保能量稳定与最大模原理（MBP）的二阶线性格式，用于Allen–Cahn型梯度流，并在无τ-h耦合条件下建立时空二阶误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Allen–Cahn方程具有能量耗散律与最大模原理两大物理特性。已有方法难以将GSAV与ETDRK2有效结合，同时保持MBP与能量稳定性，因此需要发展新的二阶线性格式以克服这一长期困难。

Method: 采用广义标量辅助变量（GSAV）方法结合新型二阶指数时间差分Runge–Kutta（ETDRK2）方法构造二阶线性数值格式；理论上证明该格式无条件保持最大模原理与能量稳定性，并进行严格误差分析。

Result: 证明了所提格式无条件保持MBP与能量稳定性；误差分析表明在时间与空间上均达到二阶精度，且无需时间步长τ与空间网格尺寸h之间的耦合条件；数值实验验证了格式效率及理论性质的保持。

Conclusion: 所提GSAV-ETDRK2格式成功克服了GSAV与ETDRK2结合时保持MBP与能量稳定的长期困难，为Allen–Cahn型梯度流提供了兼具无条件稳定性和二阶精度的有效数值方法。

Abstract: The energy dissipation law and the maximum bound principle (MBP) are two important physical features of the well-known Allen--Cahn equation. In this paper, we develop and analyze novel second-order linear numerical schemes for a class of Allen--Cahn type gradient flows. Our scheme is based on the generalized scalar auxiliary variable (GSAV) approach and a novel second-order exponential time-differencing Runge--Kutta (ETDRK2) method. The resulting formulation overcomes a longstanding difficulty in combining these two techniques while retaining both the MBP and energy stability. We prove that the proposed scheme unconditionally preserves both the MBP and energy stability. In addition, rigorous error analysis is carried out for the proposed scheme, establishing second-order accuracy in both time and space without imposing any coupling condition between the time step $τ$ and the spatial mesh size $h$. We also present some numerical experiments to demonstrate the efficiency of the proposed scheme and its preservation of the theoretical properties.

</details>


### [171] [Isogeometric analysis for the Helmholtz transmission eigenvalue problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21862)
*Nizheng Liao,Guanghui Hu,Xucheng Meng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种几何灵活、$H^2$协调的等几何方法，用于求解Helmholtz透射特征值问题导出的四阶、二次、非自伴特征值问题，并给出了最优误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透射特征值问题在逆散射理论中日益重要，过去二十年虽已发展了高效数值方法，但针对$\mathbb{R}^d$（$d=2,3$）中曲边区域的数值处理仍具挑战性。

Method: 引入并分析一种几何灵活且$H^2$协调的等几何方法，求解由Helmholtz透射特征值问题产生的四阶、二次、非自伴特征值问题；利用紧非自伴算子的谱逼近理论推导离散特征值与特征函数的最优误差估计。

Result: 给出了二维和三维基准问题（包括曲边区域问题）的数值结果，验证了理论结果，并展示了该方法在精度和几何灵活性方面优于现有数值方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的$H^2$协调等几何方法能有效求解曲边区域上的Helmholtz透射特征值问题，具有良好的精度与几何灵活性。

Abstract: The transmission eigenvalue problem plays an increasingly important role in inverse scattering theory. Although significant progress has been made in developing efficient numerical methods for the problem over the last two decades, its numerical treatment for curved domains in $\mathbb{R}^d$ ($d=2,3$) remains challenging. In this paper, we introduce and analyze a geometrically flexible and $H^2$-conforming isogeometric method for solving a fourth-order, quadratic and non-self-adjoint eigenvalue problem arising from the Helmholtz transmission eigenvalue problem. Using the spectral approximation theory for compact non-self-adjoint operators, we derive optimal error estimates for the discrete eigenvalues and eigenfunctions. Numerical results for the two- and three-dimensional benchmark problems, including those defined in curved domains, are presented to verify our theoretical results, and to demonstrate the advantages of the method over existing numerical methods in terms of both accuracy and geometric flexibility.

</details>


### [172] [Tangential stability and fully discrete convergence of the classical BGN scheme for curve shortening flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21868)
*Qiqi Rao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 证明经典BGN曲线缩短流格式在无额外稳定化下的全离散收敛，误差阶为 $C(\tau+h^k)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典BGN格式的全离散收敛此前未被证明，主要障碍是质量形式只控制法向运动，切向运动由曲率方程隐式决定并支配参数化，导致通常的长度衰减估计无法控制完整位置更新的扰动。

Method: 通过时间半离散BGN解分离时间与空间误差：利用标量法向预解式及精确的曲率与长度恒等式获得一致正则性与一阶时间收敛；空间分析中引入适配的法向-切向范数，对线性化更新给出近压缩估计，并借助Gauss-Lobatto消去与组装节点法向的精确协方差恒等式得到阶为 $h^{k+1}$ 的 $H^1$ 单步缺陷，再用精确差分恒等式控制非线性余项。

Result: 对任意固定 $k\ge1$（含原始分片线性方法），在周期准均匀网格且 $h\le c\tau^2$ 条件下，得到匹配的 $W^{1,\infty}$ 误差界 $C(\tau+h^k)$；所有离散步唯一可解，数值曲线保持正则与嵌入。

Conclusion: 这是经典BGN曲线缩短格式在无额外稳定化情形下的首个全离散收敛结果。

Abstract: We prove fully discrete convergence of the classical Barrett--Garcke--Nürnberg (BGN) scheme for curve-shortening flow of smooth embedded closed planar curves. The main obstruction is that the mass form controls only normal motion, whereas the tangential motion is determined implicitly by the curvature equation and governs the parametrization. The usual length-decay estimate therefore does not control perturbations of the full position update. We separate temporal and spatial errors through the time-semidiscrete BGN solution. A scalar normal resolvent and exact curvature and length identities yield uniform regularity and first-order time convergence. For the spatial analysis, an adapted normal--tangential norm gives a near-contractive estimate for the linearized update. Gauss--Lobatto cancellations and an exact covariance identity for the assembled nodal normals produce an $H^1$ one-step defect of order $h^{k+1}$, while an exact difference identity controls the nonlinear remainder. For every fixed $k\ge1$, including the original piecewise linear method, we obtain the matched $W^{1,\infty}$ error bound $C(τ+h^k)$ on periodic quasi-uniform meshes with $h\le cτ^2$. All discrete steps are uniquely solvable, and the numerical curves remain regular and embedded. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first fully discrete convergence result for the classical BGN curve-shortening scheme without additional stabilization.

</details>


### [173] [A filtered time stepping scheme for curve shortening flow for open and closed curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21871)
*Klaus Deckelnick,Robert Nürnberg*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种滤波时间步进有限元格式，用于任意余维数下开、闭曲线的曲线缩短流，时间上二阶精确且最优误差估计成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有曲线缩短流的数值方法在二阶时间精度、任意余维开曲线正交边界条件及每步计算效率等方面仍有改进空间，本文旨在构造兼具高精度与实用性的有限元格式。

Method: 采用滤波（filtered）时间步进有限元法；开曲线假设在区域Ω（n≥2）内演化并正交接触外边界∂Ω。每个时间步只需解一个线性系统。

Result: 对L²范数和H¹范数证明了最优误差界；数值实验验证了方法的精度与实用性，并展示了渐近等分布性质。

Conclusion: 该滤波时间步进有限元格式在时间上二阶精确，误差最优，每步仅需求解单个线性系统，数值结果支持其精度与实用性。

Abstract: We propose a filtered time stepping finite element scheme for curve shortening flow of open and closed curves in arbitrary codimension that is second-order accurate in time. Open curves are assumed to evolve inside a given domain $Ω\subset \mathbb R^n$, $n\geq2$, and meet the external boundary $\partialΩ$ orthogonally. We prove optimal error bounds for the $L^2$-- and $H^1$--norms. In practice only a single linear system needs to be solved at each time step. Numerical experiments confirm the accuracy and practicality of the introduced method, including an asymptotic equidistribution property.

</details>


### [174] [High-Moment Stability and Error Analysis of a Fully Discrete LDG-IMEX Method for High Dimensional Nonlinear Stochastic Convection-Diffusion Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21920)
*Yiming Chen,Yunzhang Li,Yulong Xing*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出并分析了一类高维非线性随机对流扩散方程的LDG+IMEX Euler全离散格式，通过递归嵌套子集实现高阶矩稳定性与误差分析，空间收敛阶接近r+1、时间阶1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非线性随机对流扩散方程（含非线性主系数、非线性对流项、耗散源项及梯度依赖噪声），需要一种适用于高维、处理乘性Q-Wiener噪声的全离散数值方法，并给出严格的稳定性与误差理论保障。核心困难在于非线性项导致的路径增长因子无法在整个样本空间上一致控制。

Method: 采用局部间断Galerkin（LDG）方法处理扩散算子（隐式），非线性对流、低阶漂移和随机项采用IMEX Euler显式处理，构造全离散格式。为处理无法一致控制的路径增长因子，引入依数值解递归定义的嵌套子集，在给定的随机抛物性与加密条件下证明这些子集的概率趋于1，并在其上建立高阶矩稳定性与误差估计；进一步结合离散Kolmogorov论证导出路径误差估计。

Result: 在递归嵌套子集上，数值解满足高阶矩稳定性；全离散误差在空间上收敛阶任意接近r+1，时间上为1/2阶。并获得了路径误差估计。对随机Burgers方程和Allen-Cahn方程的数值实验验证了理论收敛阶，并展示了方法对非线性随机模型的稳健性。

Conclusion: 所提出的LDG-IMEX全离散格式能有效处理高维非线性随机对流扩散问题，配合递归嵌套子集技术克服了路径增长因子不可一致控制的困难，理论收敛阶与数值实验一致，方法具有稳健性。

Abstract: A fully discrete local discontinuous Galerkin (LDG) method coupled with an implicit-explicit (IMEX) Euler time discretization is presented and analyzed for a class of high dimensional nonlinear stochastic convection-diffusion equations driven by multiplicative $\mathcal Q$-Wiener noise. The model allows nonlinear leading coefficients, nonlinear convection terms, dissipative source terms, and gradient-dependent noise. The diffusion operator is treated implicitly through the LDG formulation, while the nonlinear convection, lower-order drift, and stochastic terms are evaluated explicitly. The main contribution is a high-moment stability and error analysis for the fully discrete scheme. A central difficulty is that the nonlinear terms lead to pathwise growth factors that cannot be controlled uniformly on the full sample space. To provide the stability and error estimate, we introduce recursively defined nested subsets adapted to the numerical solution. Under the stated stochastic parabolicity and refinement conditions, we prove that these subsets have probabilities converging to one. On these subsets, the numerical solution satisfies high-moment stability, and the fully discrete error converges with order arbitrarily close to $r+1$ in space and $1/2$ in time. We also derive a pathwise error estimate by combining the high-moment error bound with a discrete Kolmogorov argument. Numerical experiments for stochastic Burgers' and Allen-Cahn equations confirm the theoretical rates and demonstrate the robustness of the proposed method for nonlinear stochastic models.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [On (Directed) Width-Parameters of Geometric Spanners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22082)
*Kevin Buchin,Carolin Rehs,Torben Scheele*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 研究在欧氏点集上构造受图参数约束的t-spanner，给出路径宽、分支宽、割宽（含其有向对应）等的最优渐近界，并揭示树深情形下膨胀率不可界的本质困难与近似复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为加速几何图上的算法，通常需要近似完全欧氏图，同时保持几何性质。本文关注将t-spanner的图结构限制在特定图参数（如路径宽、分支宽、割宽、平面图的团宽/秩宽、树深及各有向宽度）之下，探究在参数取值为k时膨胀率的最优界及其计算复杂性。

Method: 设κ为图参数，构造满足κ(G)=k的t-spanner G并分析其膨胀率t与n、k、维度d的关系；对树深参数，证明膨胀率不可界，转而研究给定树深k下的最小膨胀率计算问题，利用NP-hard性归约与XP算法进行刻画；进一步将框架推广至有向情形（有向树宽、有向路径宽、DAG宽）。

Result: 对路径宽、分支宽、割宽，存在膨胀率为O(n/k^{d/(d-1)})、参数值为k的spanner，且该界在渐近最坏情况下最优；在R^2中平面图的团宽或秩宽k也得到相同界。对树深，存在点集使膨胀率无法被界住；即使在树深3，近似到任意小于√2的因子也是NP-hard，并给出XP算法使得膨胀率至多2t*（t*为最小膨胀率）。有向情形下，对有向树宽、有向路径宽、DAG宽同样得到O(n/k^{d/(d-1)})的界且渐近最坏最优。

Conclusion: 图参数对欧氏spanner膨胀率的约束能力存在显著分层：路径宽、分支宽、割宽及其有向版本能给出最优渐近界，树深则导致膨胀率不可界且相关问题计算困难，但有XP算法提供近似保障。

Abstract: To speed up algorithms on geometric graphs, it is common to approximate the complete Euclidean graph while maintaining certain geometric properties. A (directed) $t$-spanner $G$ for a point set $P$ in the Euclidean space is a (directed) graph such that for every pair of points, the shortest path in $G$ is at most a factor $t$ longer than the Euclidean distance between those points.
  In this paper, we investigate $t$-spanners that are bounded by certain graph parameters. Let $κ$ be a graph parameter. We show that for path-width, branch-width and cut-width there is an $\mathcal{O}(n/k^{d/(d-1)})$-spanner $G$ on $P$ with $κ(G)=k$ and that this is asymptotically worst-case optimal. In $\mathbb{R}^2$ we show the same bounds for planar graphs of clique-width or rank-width $k$. In contrast, for tree-depth, we show that there are sets of points for which the dilation cannot be bounded. Therefore, we investigate computing a spanner with tree-depth $k$ and minimum dilation. We show that already for tree-depth $3$ this problem is NP-hard to approximate within any factor strictly less than $\sqrt{2}$, and present an XP-algorithm to compute for a given tree-depth $k$ a graph with dilation at most $2t^*$, where $t^*$ is the minimum dilation.
  We further extend these results to obtain directed $\mathcal{O}(n/k^{d/(d-1)})$-spanners $G$ with $κ(G)=k$ for $κ$ being directed tree-width, directed path-width or DAG-width and show that also in the directed case, this is asymptotically worst-case optimal.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Bootstrapping Disordered Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20916)
*Yaprak Önder,Michael G. Scheer,Minjae Cho,Eslam Khalaf*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种针对淬火无序量子系统的自举框架，通过对无序变量做矩编码，直接给出无序平均基态观测量严格的双侧界，无需对无序实现抽样，并可揭示RTFIM相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统处理淬火无序量子系统需对大量无序实现抽样，计算代价高且难以利用仅在平均意义上成立的对称性。作者希望建立一种无需抽样的、严格的解析/数值界方法，从而高效且可靠地约束无序平均基态观测量及无序涨落。

Method: 将算子代数扩展至包含经典无序变量，并以一组矩来编码无序分布；在此扩展代数内构造自举不等式，直接约束无序平均期望值，适用于有限或无限格点系统以及任意无序分布。进一步引入多副本（multi-replica）算子以刻画无序涨落，如某观测量的方差。以含离散、均匀和高斯无序的一维随机横场Ising模型（RTFIM）为对象，计算无序平均基态能量密度与短程自旋关联函数，并给出能量密度无序方差的界。

Result: 对一维RTFIM得到无序平均基态能量密度和短程自旋-自旋关联函数的紧致双侧界，以及能量密度无序方差的界。定义上、下界之差为“自举间隙”（bootstrap gap），发现该间隙在弱无序的顺磁相和铁磁相内小且平坦，进入Griffiths区后增大，并在临界线上达到峰值，据此可勾画RTFIM相图。

Conclusion: 该自举框架为淬火无序量子系统提供了无需抽样的严格双侧界方法，可有效利用平均意义上的对称性并访问无序涨落；自举间隙可作为识别相与相变的诊断量，为无序量子系统相图研究提供新工具。

Abstract: We present a bootstrap framework that yields rigorous two-sided bounds on ground-state observables of quantum systems with quenched disorder. The key idea is to extend the operator algebra to include classical disorder variables and to encode the disorder distribution through a set of moments. The method directly bounds disorder-averaged expectation values for finite or infinite lattice systems and for arbitrary disorder distributions. In contrast to conventional approaches, we do not need to sample disorder realizations, which enables us to make thorough use of symmetries that hold only on average rather than for each disorder realization. Additionally, we show that multi-replica operators can be used to access disorder fluctuations such as the variance of an observable. We apply the method to the one-dimensional random transverse-field Ising model (RTFIM) with discrete, uniform, and Gaussian disorder, obtaining tight bounds on the disorder-averaged ground-state energy density and short-range spin-spin correlators, as well as bounds on the disorder variance of the energy density. Additionally, we show that the difference between the upper and lower bounds on the disorder-averaged ground-state energy density, which we term the bootstrap gap, can be used to map out the phase diagram of the RTFIM. The bootstrap gap is small and flat within the paramagnetic and ferromagnetic phases at weak disorder, grows upon entering the Griffiths regions, and peaks along the critical line.

</details>


### [177] [Shape Deformation and Braid Statistics of Fractional Quantum Hall Quasiparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20985)
*Mytraya Gattu,J. K. Jain*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文分析分数量子霍尔准粒子的Berry相位，指出其包含Aharonov-Bohm项、形状依赖项与编织统计项三部分，形状项可能淹没编织统计信号，并对干涉实验中的分数相位跳变给出新解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想任意子的Berry相位与回路大小形状无关，可直接给出编织统计；但分数量子霍尔准粒子带电且具有有限尺寸，其形状不是拓扑性质并随局域势变化。作者因此希望厘清准粒子回路Berry相位对形状的依赖，并评估精确测定编织统计的可行性。

Method: 从理论上分析一个分数电荷准粒子绕另一个准粒子闭合回路所获得的Berry相位，将其分解为Aharonov-Bohm项、形状依赖项$\Theta_{\rm shape}$与编织统计项$\Theta_{\rm braid}$，并考察各贡献的相对大小及其对轨迹和形状不确定性的敏感度，进而结合干涉实验进行讨论。

Result: Berry相位可分解为$\Theta=\Theta_{\rm AB}+\Theta_{\rm shape}+\Theta_{\rm braid}$。其中$\Theta_{\rm AB}$占主导，与回路面积成正比；本工作识别出的形状依赖项$\Theta_{\rm shape}$可能大于量级为1的编织统计贡献$\Theta_{\rm braid}$。因此精确确定编织统计很困难，因为它可能被实际无法探测的轨迹与形状不确定性所掩盖。

Conclusion: 在理想任意子中稳健的Berry相位，对分数量子霍尔准粒子并不成立，形状效应可显著污染编织统计的测量。作者进一步指出，干涉实验中的分数相位跳变无需假设边缘存在分数电荷高度量子化的准粒子即可理解，这些跳变是体态中准粒子分数量子化涡量的直接量度。

Abstract: For ``ideal anyons,'' the Berry phase associated with a closed loop of an anyon around another is robust, that is, independent of the size or the shape of the loop, and directly yields the braid statistics. That is not the case for the fractional quantum Hall (FQH) quasiparticles (QPs), which are charged and have finite size. We consider here how the Berry phase depends on the shape of the QP, which, unlike its charge, is not a topological property and varies along the path in response to the local potential. We show that the Berry phase $Θ$ associated with the loop of a fractionally charged QP around another contains three distinct contributions: $Θ=Θ_{\rm AB}+Θ_{\rm shape}+Θ_{\rm braid}$. The Aharonov-Bohm phase $Θ_{\rm AB}$ is dominant, being proportional to the area of the loop, and the shape-dependent term $Θ_{\rm shape}$, identified in this work, can be larger than the order-one contribution from the braid statistics $Θ_{\rm braid}$. A precise determination of the braid statistics is challenging because it can be swamped by practically undetectable uncertainties in its trajectory and shape. We discuss these results in the context of the interference experiments. We also note that the fractional phase jumps in these experiments can be understood without assuming the existence of QPs with sharply quantized fractional charges at the edges of the FQH system, wherein these phase jumps are a direct measure of the fractionally quantized vorticity of the QPs in the bulk of the fractional quantum Hall state.

</details>


### [178] [Resonant spin mode from retarded relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21623)
*J. Beater,G. S. Uhrig*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出用延迟 Lindbladian 弛豫（retarded Lindbladian relaxation）解释超快自旋动力学中常规 Landau-Lifshitz 方程无法描述的 THz 附加模式，并与实验数据取得较好吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快自旋动力学中出现的 THz 附加模式无法被传统 Landau-Lifshitz 方程捕获；通过增加时间导数（惯性 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 方程）扩展方程也只能部分解决问题，因此需要新的物理解释机制。

Method: 采用现象学的延迟 Lindbladian 弛豫方法，对自旋弛豫过程引入延迟效应，并与实验数据进行对比验证。

Result: 尽管方法本身简洁，但与实验数据吻合良好：物理上合理的参数能够描述这些共振峰的位置和较大展宽；多个近似等间距共振峰这一显著特征也与实验证据一致。

Conclusion: 延迟 Lindbladian 弛豫可作为解释超快自旋动力学中 THz 附加模式的有效现象学框架，其预测与实验在共振位置、展宽及等间距峰结构方面均相符。

Abstract: In ultrafast spin dynamics additional modes in the THz range appear which cannot be captured by conventional Landau-Lifshitz equations. Extending these equations by further time derivatives (inertial Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation) solves the issue only partly. Here we suggest to explain these modes by retarded Lindbladian relaxation. In spite of the simplicity of this phenomenological approach the agreement with the experimental data is promising: physically plausible parameters describe the position and the large width of these resonances. The salient feature of several almost equidistant resonance peaks is in-line with experimental evidence as well.

</details>


### [179] [Reformulating Pfaffian Quantum Monte Carlo with the Hybrid Monte Carlo formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21631)
*Thomas Hauschild,Lin Wang,Finn L. Temmen,Thomas Luu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出将全局更新的 Hybrid Monte Carlo (HMC) 与 Pfaffian 辅助场方法结合，构建复杂度为 O(N_τL^3) 的 Pfaffian HMC 算法，实现对反常费米子哈密顿量的高效采样，并在相互作用 Kitaev 链上验证了正确性与低能边界模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pfaffian 量子蒙特卡洛将辅助场方法推广到反常费米子哈密顿量，可处理行列式形式无法描述的配对系统与 Hubbard-Stratonovich 分解。然而，如何在这种灵活框架下实现高效的大范围（全局）采样更新，仍是一个待解决的问题；作者希望把 Pfaffian 的灵活性与 HMC 的全局更新能力结合，达到与 DQMC 相当的计算效率。

Method: 核心观察是分子动力学力只依赖于 Pfaffian 权重的模，而该模可写成行列式表示，从而得到简化的力表达式。基于此，作者将 HMC 的全局更新与 Pfaffian 辅助场结合，给出标度为 O(N_τL^3) 的算法（与无赝费米子的 DQMC 对应）。该构造还以几乎零额外代价给出不等时 Majorana 关联函数。对于缺乏便利解析指数导数的相互作用核，采用截断力展开，其误差由精确的 Metropolis 接受-拒绝步骤消除。

Result: 在相互作用 Kitaev 链上的应用表明：该方法的结果与精确对角化一致，并分辨出局域在两端边界的低能模式，以及它们在接近拓扑相变时不断增大的重叠。算法复杂度与 DQMC 相当，验证了其数值正确性与可行性。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了 HMC 作为 Pfaffian 辅助场模拟的高效采样框架，拓展了反常费米子体系量子蒙特卡洛的应用范围。

Abstract: Pfaffian quantum Monte Carlo extends auxiliary field methods to anomalous fermionic Hamiltonians, opening access to pairing systems and Hubbard-Stratonovich decompositions that are unavailable in determinant formulations. We show that this flexibility can be combined with the global updates of Hybrid Monte Carlo (HMC), leading to an algorithm scaling as $\mathcal{O}(N_τL^3)$, similar to its determinant quantum Monte Carlo (DQMC) counterpart without pseudofermions. The central observation is that the molecular dynamics force depends only on the magnitude of the Pfaffian weight, which admits a determinant representation and leads to a simplified force expression. This construction also yields unequal-time Majorana correlators at essentially no additional cost. For interaction kernels with no convenient analytic exponential derivative, we employ a truncated force expansion whose errors are removed by the exact Metropolis accept-reject step. Applied to the interacting Kitaev chain, the method agrees with exact diagonalization and resolves low-energy modes localized at opposite boundaries, together with their increasing overlap near the topological transition. These results establish HMC as an efficient sampling framework for Pfaffian auxiliary-field simulations.

</details>


### [180] [Orbital character and photoemission signatures of the A-pocket in infinite-layer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21665)
*Leonard M. Verhoff,Michael Seidl,Liang Si,Karsten Held*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合DFT+DMFT与ARPES模拟，将无限层镍氧化物NdNiO₂的A口袋识别为间隙s、Nd-dxy与Ni-dxz/yz强杂化态，并强调矩阵元与轨道杂化须联合处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限层镍氧化物中A口袋的轨道来源长期存在争议，需明确其微观轨道组成及光谱特征的成因。

Method: 对体相NdNiO₂开展密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场（DFT+DMFT）计算，并进行角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）模拟，通过逐一移除间隙s、Nd-dxy、Ni-dxz/yz矩阵元来检验对费米面图样的影响。

Result: A口袋为强杂化态，含显著间隙s、Nd-dxy和Ni-dxz/yz成分；模拟再现了实验的主要偏振依赖特征。移除间隙s矩阵元导致与实测费米面图样明显不符（尽管其贡献并非主导）；而抑制Nd-dxy或Ni-dxz/yz矩阵元几乎不改变图样，因其在布里渊区角处数值很小。

Conclusion: 指认无限层镍氧化物光谱特征的微观来源，必须同时考虑轨道杂化与光电子发射矩阵元。

Abstract: The orbital character of the so-called $A$-pocket in infinite-layer nickelates remains under debate. We investigate bulk NdNiO$_2$ using density-functional theory combined with dynamical mean-field theory and identify the $A$-pocket as a strongly hybridized state with substantial interstitial-$s$, Nd-$d_{xy}$ and Ni-d_${xz/yz}$ contributions. Our angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy simulations reproduce the main polarization-dependent features measured experimentally. Removing the interstitial-$s$ matrix element leads to a clear disagreement with the measured Fermi surface maps, even though the interstitial-$s$ contribution is not dominant. Suppressing instead the Nd-d$_{xy}$ or Ni-$_{dxz/yz}$ matrix elements leaves the maps almost unchanged, because these are small at the zone corner. More generally, our results demonstrate that assigning the microscopic origin of spectral features in infinite-layer nickelates requires treating orbital hybridizations and photoemission matrix elements together.

</details>


### [181] [Machine learning magnetic interactions from neutron powder diffraction data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21970)
*Adit S. Desai,Yongqiang Cheng,Joseph A. M. Paddison*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出用机器学习从粉末磁漫散射数据中反演磁相互作用参数，在八种高对称晶格上实现约2%的高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从中子衍射数据确定磁相互作用参数本质上是一个逆散射问题，通常难以求解；传统非线性最小二乘拟合还易陷入伪极小值，因此需要更稳健高效的反演方法。

Method: 针对八种高对称晶格，系统考察各向同性相互作用，利用机器学习方法建立从粉末样品的磁漫散射数据到相互作用参数的映射。

Result: 在所有考察的晶格上，机器学习方法对相互作用参数的估计精度约达2%，并避免了非线性最小二乘精修中出现的伪极小值问题。

Conclusion: 粉末漫散射数据可为许多材料的磁相互作用提供紧凑的“指纹”，表明机器学习是解决磁相互作用参数反演问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Neutron diffraction is a versatile experimental technique capable of probing a material's magnetic properties. While diffraction is typically used to determine the magnetic structure of a material, magnetic diffuse scattering data from a diffraction experiment are also sensitive to the magnetic interactions in its Hamiltonian. However, accurately determining magnetic interaction parameters from neutron-scattering data involves an inverse scattering problem that is challenging to solve in general. Here, we investigate the effectiveness of a machine learning approach to predict the interaction parameters given magnetic diffuse-scattering data measured on powder samples, for a comprehensive survey of isotropic interactions on eight high-symmetry lattices. Across all lattices we considered, the machine-learning approach estimates the interaction parameters with high (~2%) accuracy, while avoiding the issue of false minima that is encountered with non-linear least squares refinement. Our results highlight that powder diffuse-scattering data can provide a compact "fingerprint" of the magnetic interactions for many materials.

</details>


### [182] [Unified Field Bosonization Technique for strongly inhomogenous Luttinger Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21973)
*Soundarya P,Venkata Suryanarayana M,Joy Prakash Das*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出统一场玻色化技术（UFBT），无需重整化群或微扰展开，即可解析处理含静态杂质的强非均匀一维Luttinger液体，并给出任意N点关联函数的闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强非均匀一维Luttinger液体（含静态杂质）的解析处理通常依赖重整化群分析或微扰展开，缺乏在玻色化层面直接、统一且非微扰的描述框架。

Method: UFBT通过玻色相位场的对称化组合引入杂质散射，针对包括delta势垒、有限势垒与有限势井在内的广泛静态杂质势，推导任意N点关联函数的闭式表达式；该形式化不依赖重整化群分析或微扰展开。

Result: 该技术通过恢复已知极限情形、与相互作用强度的一阶微扰展开一致、以及与Schwinger-Dyson方程自洽而得到验证。关键结果是关联函数指数与杂质强度无关，杂质的依赖体现在关联函数的空间结构和幅度上。

Conclusion: UFBT为非均匀Luttinger液体的解析研究奠定基础，可用于输运、Friedel振荡以及局域和动态态密度的分析。

Abstract: We introduce the Unified Field Bosonization Technique (UFBT), a direct bosonization framework for strongly inhomogeneous one-dimensional Luttinger liquids (LLs) containing static impurities. UFBT incorporates impurity scattering through a symmetrized combination of bosonic phase fields and yields closed-form expressions for arbitrary N-point correlation functions for a broad class of static impurity potentials, including delta barriers, finite barriers and finite wells. The formalism requires neither renormalization-group analysis nor perturbative expansions, providing an analytical description of the inhomogeneous system at the bosonized level. The technique is validated by recovering known limiting cases, showing agreement with the first-order perturbative expansion in the interaction strength, and demonstrating consistency with the Schwinger-Dyson equations. A key result is that the correlation function exponents remain independent of the impurity strength, while the impurity dependence is captured by the spatial structure and amplitudes of the correlation functions. The resulting correlation functions establish a foundation for analytical studies of transport, Friedel oscillations, and the local and dynamical density of states in strongly inhomogeneous Luttinger liquids.

</details>


### [183] [Correlation enhanced magnetism at a superconducting cuprate/manganite interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22051)
*Vitor A. M. Lima,Marcello Civelli,Walber Hugo de Brito*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在欠掺杂铜氧化物区，用团簇动力学平均场研究界面交换场下的关联双层Hubbard模型，发现超导与界面诱导的Cu自旋极化共存，且超导态磁矩大于受约束的正常态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受铜氧化物-锰氧化物异质结实验研究启发，探究强电子关联下超导与界面磁性之间的相互影响。

Method: 采用团簇动力学平均场理论，研究处于欠掺杂铜氧化物区、受局域界面交换场作用的关联双层Hubbard模型。

Result: 超导与锰氧化物诱导的Cu自旋极化共存；在整个共存区，超导解的磁矩大于对应的受约束正常态；磁响应分析表明该反转由关联驱动，关联在正常态已增强磁性，在超导态增强更显著；更强交换场下超导突然崩溃，伴随与一级相变一致的迟滞，并出现强自旋极化赝能隙。

Conclusion: 在强电子关联下，超导可以增强而非抑制界面磁性。

Abstract: Motivated by experimental studies on cuprate-manganite heterostructures, we study a correlated bilayer Hubbard model subject to a local interfacial exchange field using cluster dynamical mean-field theory in the underdoped cuprate regime. We find that superconductivity coexists with a manganite-induced Cu spin polarization. Remarkably, throughout this coexistence regime, the superconducting solution carries a larger magnetic moment than the corresponding constrained normal state. Analysis of the magnetic response shows that this reversal is driven by correlations, which enhance magnetism already in the normal state and even more strongly in the superconducting solution. At stronger exchange fields, superconductivity collapses abruptly, displaying hysteresis consistent with a first-order transition, and a strongly spin-polarized pseudogap emerges. These results show that, under strong electronic correlations, superconductivity can enhance rather than suppress interfacial magnetism.

</details>

Wresilo,W. Wu,E. Yandel,T. Yang,L. E. Yates,H. W. Yu,G. P. Zeller,J. Zennamo,C. Zhang,Y. Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次测量了氩核上电子中微子相互作用通量积分微分截面随运动学失衡变量的变化，拓展了该变量至电子中微子通道，并用MicroBooNE全数据集与多个中微子事件发生器对比，发现模型与数据存在张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动学失衡变量对费米运动、多核子过程及末态相互作用等效应敏感，可提供研究（反）中微子-核相互作用的细致视角。此前该变量主要应用于缪子中微子相互作用，本文首次将其应用于电子中微子相互作用，以检验和改进核效应建模。

Method: 利用费米国家加速器实验室Booster中微子束，采用MicroBooNE全部数据集（$1.3 \times 10^{21}$质子打靶），测量氩核上电子中微子相互作用的通量积分微分截面（作为运动学失衡变量的函数），并与多种中微子事件发生器配置进行对比。

Result: 获得了通量积分的电子中微子-氩核微分截面随运动学失衡变量的测量结果；与多个事件发生器配置对比后发现，在预期末态相互作用主导较弱的区域，模型与数据之间存在张力（不一致）。

Conclusion: 本文首次在电子中微子相互作用中应用运动学失衡变量并给出截面测量，揭示现有中微子事件发生器在末态相互作用较弱区域仍与数据不符，说明当前核效应建模存在不足，需进一步改进。

Abstract: We report the first measurement of flux-integrated cross sections as a function of kinematic imbalance variables for electron neutrino interactions on argon. Kinematic imbalance variables, applied here to electron neutrino interactions for the first time, are particularly interesting due to their sensitivity to effects such as Fermi motion, multinucleon processes, and final-state interactions, providing a detailed lens to study neutrino-nucleus interactions. These measurements are performed using MicroBooNE's full dataset of $1.3 \times 10^{21}$ protons on target from the Booster Neutrino Beam at the Fermi National Accelerator Laboratory. The measured cross sections are compared to several neutrino event generator configurations, revealing tensions between these models and data in the regions that are expected to be less dominated by final-state interactions.

</details>


### [185] [Dark photons in exotic Higgs boson decays at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21648)
*Axel Gallén,Sarah Ben Abdesselem,Giulia Ripellino,Rebeca Gonzalez Suarez*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出在FCC-ee希格斯运行阶段（240 GeV）前瞻性搜寻暗光子衰变为准直、位移缪子对，通过运动学选择实现零背景并给出希格斯到暗光子分支比预期限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗光子作为暗扇区与标准模型之间的可能媒介粒子，其探测是新物理搜寻的重要方向；FCC-ee的高亮度与清洁环境为在希格斯衰变中搜寻弱耦合、长寿命的暗光子提供了独特机会。

Method: 假设FCC-ee在240 GeV希格斯运行阶段的全部亮度，针对暗光子衰变为准直、位移缪子对进行前瞻性搜寻。信号特征为Z玻色子衰变产生的两个喷注加上希格斯衰变中暗光子产生的两对缪子，采用基于运动学的筛选策略。

Result: 基于运动学的筛选实现了零背景，同时保持高信号效率；给出了希格斯玻色子到暗光子分支比作为暗光子质量和耦合函数的预期上限。

Conclusion: 该研究表明FCC-ee希格斯运行阶段可对宽范围的暗光子质量与耦合进行灵敏探测，为暗扇区物理提供有力的搜寻途径。

Abstract: A prospective search for dark photons decaying into collimated, displaced muon pairs is presented for the electron-positron stage of the Future Circular Collider, FCC-ee. Assuming the full luminosity of the Higgs run at 240 GeV, the study probes a broad range of dark photon masses and couplings. The signature comprises two jets from a Z boson decay and two muon pairs from dark photons produced in Higgs decays. A kinematics-based selection achieves zero background while retaining high signal efficiency. Expected limits are set on the Higgs boson branching ratio to dark photons as a function of the dark photon mass and coupling.

</details>


### [186] [Search for resonant and nonresonant production of pairs of dijet resonances with b jets in the final state in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21850)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验在13 TeV质子-质子碰撞中搜寻成对产生的、等质量且各自衰变为b夸克与轻味夸克的双喷注共振态，使用136 fb⁻¹数据，分别给出非共振产生与经由中间共振态的四个喷注产生的结果，并报告四个喷注和含b喷注双喷注共振态产生的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次在LHC上搜寻由完全分辨的双喷注共振态成对构成的共振产生过程，且末态含b喷注，所有考虑的共振态均对应非标准模型态；同时扩展此前对R宇称破坏衰变至奇异夸克和b夸克情形的stop对产生搜寻。

Method: 利用CMS探测器在√s = 13 TeV质子-质子碰撞中采集的对应积分亮度136 fb⁻¹的数据样本，针对成对产生的等质量双喷注共振态（各自衰变为b夸克和轻味夸克）进行搜寻，结果分别针对非共振产生以及四喷注通过中间共振态产生的情形给出，并对含b喷注的四喷注与双喷注共振态产生报告上限。

Result: 非共振产生情形下最显著超出出现在平均双喷注质量1.0 TeV处，局域显著性为2.1个标准差；排除质量介于0.5至0.8 TeV的stop对产生，扩展了此前对R宇称破坏衰变至奇异夸克和b夸克的搜寻。共振产生搜寻中最显著超出出现在四喷注共振质量3.1 TeV、双喷注共振质量1.3 TeV处，局域显著性为2.6个标准差；结果还用于在95%置信水平上对双夸克-双夸克模型设置限制，排除2至7 TeV之间较重的双夸克质量。

Conclusion: 该搜寻首次在LHC覆盖全分辨、含b喷注的双喷注共振态成对共振产生，未发现显著信号，并给出含b喷注的四喷注与双喷注共振态产生上限，同时排除0.5–0.8 TeV的stop对产生以及2–7 TeV的较重双夸克质量。

Abstract: A search is presented for pair-produced dijet resonances of identical mass, each decaying into a bottom (b) quark and a light-flavor quark. The search uses a data sample corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 136 fb$^{-1}$ collected with the CMS detector in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV. Results are presented separately for nonresonant production and for the case where the four-jet production proceeds via an intermediate resonant state. Upper limits are reported on the production of four-jet and dijet resonances with b jets in the final state. This is the first LHC search for resonant production of pairs of fully resolved dijet resonances with b jets in the final state, in which all resonances considered correspond to non-standard-model states. For nonresonant production, the most significant excess occurs at an average dijet mass of 1.0 TeV, with a local significance of 2.1 standard deviations. Additionally, the results exclude pair production of top squarks with masses between 0.5 and 0.8 TeV, extending previous searches for $R$-parity-violating decays to strange and b quarks. In the search for resonant production, the most significant excess occurs at a four-jet resonance mass of 3.1 TeV and a dijet resonance mass of 1.3 TeV, with a local significance of 2.6 standard deviations. The results are also used to set limits at 95% confidence level on the diquark-to-diquarks model and exclude the heavier diquark masses between 2 and 7 TeV.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用LHCb 2024年13.6 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（6.3 fb⁻¹），在Ω⁰_cπ⁺衰变道中观测到双粲重子Ω⁺_cc，全局显著性8.7σ，质量测定为3725.9 MeV/c²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双粲重子Ω⁺_cc的搜寻与质量精确测量对检验重味夸克强子谱、QCD非微扰动力学以及双粲四夸克/重子系统理论模型具有重要意义，此前尚缺乏该粒子的确证观测。

Method: 使用升级后LHCb探测器在2024年采集的质心能量13.6 TeV、积分亮度6.3 fb⁻¹的质子-质子对撞数据，在Ω⁰_cπ⁺衰变道中搜寻Ω⁺_cc，其中Ω⁰_c重建于pK⁻K⁻π⁺末态；对Ω⁰_cπ⁺质量谱进行拟合，并分析其是否为弱衰变粒子。

Result: 在Ω⁰_cπ⁺质量谱中观测到全局显著性为8.7σ的峰状结构，与弱衰变粒子一致，被判定为双粲重子Ω⁺_cc；测得质量为3725.9 ± 1.0(stat) ± 0.2(syst) ± 0.4(lifetime) ± 0.6(ext) MeV/c²，其中第三项系统误差源自选择偏置对未知Ω⁺_cc寿命的依赖，第四项来自Ω⁰_c、Ξ⁺_c与Ξ⁺⁺_cc重子质量的不确定性。

Conclusion: 该工作以8.7σ显著性首次（在所述数据集中）确证了双粲重子Ω⁺_cc的存在，并给出其质量的精确测定，为双粲重子谱学研究提供了关键实验依据。

Abstract: A search for the doubly charmed baryon $\varOmega^+_{cc}$ in the $\varOmega^0_cπ^+$ decay channel is performed using proton-proton collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $6.3\text{fb}^{-1}$, collected with the upgraded LHCb detector in 2024 at a center-of-mass energy of 13.6$\text{TeV}$. A peaking structure with a global significance of $8.7σ$ is observed in the $\varOmega^0_cπ^+$ mass spectrum, where the $\varOmega^0_c$ baryon is reconstructed in the $pK^-K^-π^+$ final state. The structure is consistent with originating from a weakly decaying particle and is identified as the doubly charmed baryon $\varOmega^+_{cc}$. Its mass is determined to be $3725.9 \pm 1.0 \,(\mathrm{stat}) \pm 0.2 \,(\mathrm{syst}) \pm 0.4 \,(\mathrm{lifetime}) \pm 0.6 \,(\mathrm{ext})\,\text{MeV/}c^2$, where the third uncertainty arises from the dependence of the selection-induced bias on the unknown $\varOmega^+_{cc}$ lifetime, and the fourth is due to the uncertainties on the masses of the $\varOmega^0_c$, $\varXi^+_c$, and $\varXi^{++}_{cc}$ baryons.

</details>

Zhao,V. Zhilich,J. S. Zhou,Q. D. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,L. Zhu,R. Žlebčík*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: Belle II利用387×10⁶ Υ(4S)衰变数据，通过强子标记法测量分支比比值 R(D*)=0.242±0.019(stat)±0.016(syst) 与 R(D)=0.439±0.055(stat)±0.046(syst)，结果与标准模型预言及此前测量一致，是采用强子标记的最精确 R(D(*)) 测定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验轻子味普适性（LFU）。B 介子半轻衰变中的 R(D(*)) 是检验标准模型 LFU 的关键可观测量，此前测量与标准模型预言存在一定张力，需更高精度实验检验。

Method: 使用 Belle II 探测器在能量非对称 e⁺e⁻ 对撞中采集的 387×10⁶ Υ(4S) 衰变。采用强子标记：一个 B 介子全重建于强子衰变模式，另一个重建为 B̄→D(*)τ⁻ν̄_τ（τ⁻→ℓ⁻ν̄_ℓν_τ）或 B̄→D(*)ℓ⁻ν̄_ℓ。通过残余量能器能量与未探测粒子质量平方的分布提取信号。

Result: R(D*) = 0.242 ± 0.019(stat) ± 0.016(syst)；R(D) = 0.439 ± 0.055(stat) ± 0.046(syst)。结果与标准模型预言和此前测量均一致，为采用强子标记的最精确 R(D(*)) 测定。

Conclusion: 测量支持轻子味普适性，未观察到显著偏离标准模型的迹象；该结果为强子标记方法下迄今最精确的 R(D(*)) 结果。

Abstract: We test lepton flavor universality with a measurement of the branching-fraction ratios $R(D^{(*)}) \equiv \mathcal{B}(\bar{B} \rightarrow D^{(*)} τ^{-} \barν_τ)/\mathcal{B}(\bar{B} \rightarrow D^{(*)} \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell})$, where $\ell$ denotes an electron or muon. The analysis uses $387\times 10^6$ $Υ(\mathrm{4S})$ decays collected with the Belle II detector in energy-asymmetric $e^+e^-$ collisions. One $B$ meson is fully reconstructed in a hadronic decay mode, while the other is reconstructed either in $\bar{B}\rightarrow D^{(*)}τ^{-}\barν_τ$, with $τ^- \rightarrow \ell^- \barν_{\ell}ν_τ$, or in $\bar{B}\rightarrow D^{(*)}\ell^{-}\barν_{\ell}$. We extract the signal from the distributions of the residual calorimeter energy and the squared mass of the undetected particles, obtaining $R(D^{*}) = 0.242 \pm0.019(\mathrm{stat}) \pm0.016(\mathrm{syst})$ and $R(D) = 0.439 \pm 0.055(\mathrm{stat}) \pm 0.046(\mathrm{syst})$. These results are consistent with both the standard model predictions and previous measurements, and constitute the most precise determination of $R(D^{(*)})$ with hadronic tagging.

</details>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Best Matches in Phylogenetic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21700)
*Patricia A. Ebert,Peter F. Stadler,Marc Hellmuth*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该文将最佳匹配图（BMGs）从叶色有根树推广到叶色有根网络，利用"sicor-in-hub"性质刻画并识别BMGs，给出线性时间识别与二次时间网络构造算法，并得到RBMGs的类似结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最佳匹配图（BMGs）最初在数学系统发育学中用于描述有根树（叶代表相关基因，叶色代表不同生物）中最近亲属的概念。然而，真实的进化关系常涉及网状结构而非单纯的树，因此有必要将BMGs的概念从有根树推广到有根网络，以处理最近共同祖先通常既不唯一也不可比较的情形。

Method: 作者在有根网络（顶点带颜色）的框架下重新定义最佳匹配图。通过分析最近共同祖先在一般网络中的非唯一性与不可比较性，刻画BMGs为一类恰当着色（properly colored）的有向图，需满足一个易于检验的条件，称为"sicor-in-hub"性质。基于该刻画设计识别与构造算法。

Result: BMGs可在线性时间内被识别，且可在二次时间内构造出解释网络。对于相互最佳匹配图（RBMGs）——其中边{x,y}对应颜色不同且互为最近亲属的顶点对——也获得了类似的结果。

Conclusion: BMGs概念可从叶色有根树成功推广至叶色有根网络，其通过sicor-in-hub性质得到简洁刻画，并具备高效的识别与网络构造算法；相应结论亦可延伸至RBMGs。

Abstract: Best match graphs (BMGs) were introduced in mathematical phylogenetics to describe the concept of closest relatives for related genes (leaves of rooted tree) in different organisms (defining leaf colors). We generalize this concept here to leaf-colored rooted networks, where least common ancestors are in general neither unique nor comparable. We characterize BMGs of rooted networks as those vertex-colored digraphs that are properly colored and satisfy an easy-to-check condition that we call the sicor-in-hub property. BMGs can be recognized in linear time and an explaining network can be constructed in quadratic time. Analogous results are obtained for reciprocal best match graphs (RBMGs), where an edge $\{x,y\}$ corresponds to pairs of vertices with different color that are mutually closest relatives.

</details>


### [190] [Exact Counts of Binary Phylogenetic Networks with Four Reticulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21772)
*Hao Yu,Louxin Zhang*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文给出了含4个网结（reticulation）的无限制有根二叉系统发育网络在n个标记物种上的显式闭式计数公式，将此前结果从至多3个网结扩展到4个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育网络可刻画杂交、基因渗入、重组与水平基因转移等网状进化过程，但其组合复杂性使基本枚举问题都很困难；作者希望在既有至多3个网结工作基础上，进一步推进无限制有根二叉系统发育网络的精确枚举。

Method: 基于树成分图（tree-component graph）方法，将4个网结网络对应的79种成分图分为十组，再对每组网络进行枚举：组合使用已知的单成分网络计数、森林计数以及含更少网结的网络计数，最后对各类贡献求和得到闭式公式。

Result: 推导出含4个网结、n个标记物种的无限制有根二叉系统发育网络的显式闭式计数公式，把该类的精确枚举从3个网结推广到4个网结。

Conclusion: 结果扩展了无限制二叉系统发育网络的精确枚举范围，并表明成分图方法在系统组织与计数日益复杂的网络类方面是有效的。

Abstract: Phylogenetic networks provide a flexible framework for representing reticulate evolutionary processes, such as hybridization, introgression, recombination, and horizontal gene transfer. However, their combinatorial complexity makes even basic enumeration problems difficult. Building on our previous work for networks with up to three reticulations, we derive an explicit closed-form formula for the number of unrestricted rooted binary phylogenetic networks with four reticulations on \(n\) labeled taxa.
  Our approach is based on tree-component graphs. We classify the 79 possible component graphs corresponding to networks with four reticulations into ten groups. We then enumerate the networks associated with each group by combining known counts of one-component networks, forests, and networks with fewer reticulations. Summing these contributions yields the desired formula. This result extends the exact enumeration of unrestricted binary phylogenetic networks to four reticulations and further demonstrates the effectiveness of component graphs for systematically organizing and counting increasingly complex network classes.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Double flag varieties of Levi type with a finite number of orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21092)
*Lucas Fresse,Kyo Nishiyama*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 研究双旗簇 L/Q × G/P 在 Levi 子群 L 对角作用下的轨道分类问题；对一般线性群 G，利用箭图表示理论完全解决了何时轨道有限的判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双旗簇上 Levi 子群对角作用的轨道有限性判定是轨道理论中的基本问题，但一般情形难以处理，需要一个可计算的判定准则。

Method: 设 G 连通约化群，L 为其 Levi 子群，考虑 L 在 L/Q × G/P 上的对角作用。将"轨道有限"的判别转化为箭图表示问题：对 G = GL(n)，把抛物子群 P ⊂ G 与 Q ⊂ L 的配对编码为某个箭图，利用其表示论（如不可分解表示、根系统或 Tits 型）来判断有限性。

Result: 对 G 为一般线性群的情形，给出了 P ⊂ G 与 Q ⊂ L 使得 L 在对角作用下的轨道仅有有限多个的完全分类（充分必要条件）。

Conclusion: 通过箭图表示这一组合工具，成功刻画了一般线性群情形下双旗簇 Levi 对角作用的有限轨道对 (P, Q)，为更一般约化群的情形提供了方法和思路。

Abstract: We consider a double flag variety of the form $L/Q\times G/P$ where $G$ is a connected reductive group and $L$ is a Levi subgroup of $G$. The Levi subgroup $L$ acts diagonally on this double flag variety and a basic problem is to classify pairs of parabolic subgroups $P\subset G$ and $Q\subset L$ such that there are finitely many orbits for the considered action. We solve this problem in the case where $G$ is the general linear group by using representations of quivers.

</details>


### [192] [Minuscule Relations in Quantum $K$-Theory of Flag Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21360)
*Koushik Brahma,Yicen Huang,Takeshi Ikeda,Takafumi Kouno,Kohei Yamaguchi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究旗簇 $G/B$ 的量子 $K$-理论，对每个极小基本权 $\varpi$ 在环面等变量子 $K$-理论 $QK_T(G/B)$ 中构造了一个显式关系，该关系可视为以 $\varpi$ 为最高权的不可约表示特征的量子形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旗簇的量子 $K$-理论中，显式关系的构造（尤其是与表示论对象相对应的关系）是理解其结构的关键。极小基本权对应的不可约表示特征具有简洁形式，其量子形变有望揭示量子 $K$-理论的表示论结构。

Method: 对每个极小基本权 $\varpi$，在环面等变量子 $K$-理论 $QK_T(G/B)$ 中显式构造一个关系式，并将其与最高权为 $\varpi$ 的不可约表示的特征进行对照，将其解释为该特征的量子形变。

Result: 对每个极小基本权 $\varpi$，成功构造出 $QK_T(G/B)$ 中的一个显式关系，该关系是最高权 $\varpi$ 不可约表示特征的量子形变版本。

Conclusion: 极小基本权对应的表示特征可在量子 $K$-理论框架下自然量子化，为旗簇量子 $K$-理论提供了新的显式关系与表示论解释。

Abstract: We study the quantum $K$-theory of the flag variety $G/B$. For each minuscule fundamental weight $\varpi$, we construct an explicit relation in the torus-equivariant quantum $K$-theory $QK_T(G/B)$. The relation can be regarded as a quantum deformation of the character of the irreducible representation with highest weight $\varpi$.

</details>


### [193] [A Gelfand model for the Okada algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21607)
*Harikrishnan T R*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为含一般参数 X、Y 的 Okada 代数 O_n(X,Y) 在对称 Okada 弧图空间上构造了一个 Gelfand 模型，并据此得到其全部不可约表示，这些表示由 Young--Fibonacci 格中秩为 n 的元素标号，并与 O_n(X,Y) 的胞腔模相一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Okada 代数 O_n(X,Y) 的表示论结构，希望显式地构造其 Gelfand 模型以刻画全部不可约表示，并厘清其与胞腔模之间的关系。

Method: 将 Okada 代数视为图解代数，利用 Jones 基本构造得到一列代数塔，使各层本身即为低阶 Okada 代数，从而归纳地构造 Gelfand 模型；模型作用取共轭型作用，承载空间为对称 Okada 弧图。

Result: 在对称 Okada 弧图空间上成功构造出 O_n(X,Y) 的 Gelfand 模型，并由此获得 O_n(X,Y) 的全部不可约表示，其标号集为 Young--Fibonacci 格中秩为 n 的元素；同时将这些不可约表示与 O_n(X,Y) 的胞腔模对应起来。

Conclusion: Okada 代数 O_n(X,Y) 具有由弧图与共轭作用实现的 Gelfand 模型，其表示论可由 Young--Fibonacci 格与胞腔模统一描述。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct a Gelfand model for the Okada algebra $O_n(X,Y)$ with generic parameters $X$ and $Y$, on the space of symmetric Okada arc diagrams using a conjugation-type action. The model is constructed inductively by identifying the Okada algebra as a diagram algebra and using the Jones basic construction to obtain a tower of algebras that are themselves Okada algebras at lower levels. We use the model to obtain all the irreducible representations of $O_n(X,Y)$, indexed by the elements of rank $n$ of the Young--Fibonacci lattice, and identify them with the cell modules of $O_n(X,Y)$.

</details>


### [194] [Categorical Lie-Rinehart modules and Shen-Larsson functors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21644)
*Han Dai,Vyacheslav Futorny,Huimin Gao*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在对称幺半范畴中建立Lie-Rinehart幺半群及其弱模的范畴框架，利用交叉同态构造Shen-Larsson函子的范畴版本，并刻画弱模范畴具有幺半结构的条件及相应函子严格幺半的条件。对偶地发展Lie-Rinehart余幺半群与弱余模理论，结合模与余模构造得到双模范畴结构，最后特化到超向量空间范畴恢复Lie-Rinehart超代数及相关构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Lie-Rinehart代数及其Shen-Larsson函子的构造多在具体代数或模论层面进行，缺乏统一的范畴化描述。本文旨在通过对称幺半范畴的抽象语言，将Lie-Rinehart结构、弱模/弱余模及Shen-Larsson函子纳入统一框架，并推广至超情形。

Method: 在对称幺半范畴中定义Lie-Rinehart幺半群与弱模；借助交叉同态构造模范畴对弱Lie-Rinehart模范畴的自然作用，得到范畴版Shen-Larsson函子；刻画弱模范畴具备幺半结构的条件及对应函子严格幺半的条件；利用余交叉同态对偶发展Lie-Rinehart余幺半群与弱余模；结合模与余模构造双模范畴结构；最后特化至超向量空间范畴。

Result: 建立了Lie-Rinehart幺半群及其弱模的范畴框架；构造出Shen-Larsson函子的范畴化版本；给出了弱模范畴具有幺半结构及相应函子严格幺半的充要条件；对偶建立了余幺半群与弱余模理论；由模与余模组合得到双模范畴结构；在超向量空间范畴中恢复Lie-Rinehart超代数及相关Shen-Larsson型构造。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并范畴化了Lie-Rinehart代数、弱模/余模及Shen-Larsson函子，提供了模与余模结合的双模结构，并可自然特化到超代数情形。

Abstract: We develop a categorical framework for Lie-Rinehart monoids and their weak modules in a symmetric monoidal category. Using crossed homomorphisms, we construct a natural action of the monoidal category of modules over a Lie monoid on the category of weak Lie-Rinehart modules, thereby obtaining categorical versions of the Shen-Larsson functors. We further characterize the conditions under which the category of weak modules admits a monoidal structure and identify the corresponding condition for the associated functors to be strict monoidal. A dual theory for Lie- Rinehart comonoids and weak comodules is developed using cocrossed homomorphisms. Combining the module and comodule constructions, we obtain a bimodule category structure on the category of weak modules. Finally, we specialize the general framework to the symmetric monoidal category of super vector spaces, recovering Lie-Rinehart superalgebras and their associated Shen-Larsson-type constructions.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Critical and near-critical influence bounds for ferromagnetic Ising models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20866)
*Yan Ru Pei*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在最大度数为Δ≥3的图上，铁磁Ising模型在树唯一性阈值处影响矩阵每行的界为O(√n)，并由此给出Glauber动力学的多项式混合时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进铁磁Ising模型影响矩阵的现有一般图界（含对数项），并在超临界窗口内获得多项式混合时间。

Method: 结合逐点腔（cavity）界、正级数磁化倾斜（positive-series magnetization tilt）以及Ding–Song–Sun的场比较定理进行证明。

Result: 在树唯一性阈值处，影响矩阵每行界为O(√n)，且对度数、外场及所有pin配置一致；更一般地，当耦合以β为界且ε=((Δ−1)tanhβ−1)_+时，界为C(√n+nε)。该临界估计去除了近期一般图界中的对数因子。

Conclusion: 作为推论，零场单点Glauber动力学在整个超临界窗口ε=O(√(log n/n))内以多项式时间混合，多项式的次数依赖于窗口大小。

Abstract: For a ferromagnetic Ising model on a graph of maximum degree $Δ\ge3$, we prove a bound of order $\sqrt n$ on every row of the influence matrix at the tree uniqueness threshold. The estimate is uniform in the degree, the external fields, and all pinnings. More generally, if the couplings are bounded by $β$ and $\varepsilon=((Δ-1)\tanhβ-1)_+$, the bound is $C(\sqrt n+n\varepsilon)$. The proof combines a pointwise cavity bound with a positive-series magnetization tilt and the field comparison theorem of Ding, Song and Sun. The critical estimate removes the logarithm in recent general graphical bounds for the ferromagnetic case. As a consequence, zero-field single-site Glauber dynamics mixes in polynomial time throughout the supercritical window $\varepsilon=O(\sqrt{\log n/n})$, with the polynomial degree depending on the window size.

</details>


### [196] [A Functorial Theory of Defects in Abelian Chern-Simons Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21414)
*Daniel Galviz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论作为范畴TQFT研究，刻画其Turaev-Viro实现与Alterfold构造，指出有限二次模是不变量，并证明多分量阿贝尔BF理论是离对角阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论的扩展TQFT，同时说明近期提出的阿贝尔Chern-Simons对偶并非真正的TQFT对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作已将阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论构造为范畴TQFT，从而可自然引入范畴缺陷并构造缺陷扩展。作者希望在该框架下厘清单块实现与典范双重实现的区别，建立Alterfold与Chern-Simons的显式对应词典，并统一描述缺陷与对偶性，为阿贝尔拓扑序中的缺陷提供具体模型并指向非阿贝尔情形。

Method: 作者首先在中心和双重指向模情形下识别阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论的Turaev-Viro实现，区分单块实现与典范双重实现；利用Alterfold构造给出相应的拓扑边界、畴壁与凝聚扇区，建立Alterfold/Chern-Simons词典；指出有限二次模是控制体理论、其拓扑对称性、定向反演不变性及缺陷的不变量；并构造离对角阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论的扩展TQFT以获得多分量阿贝尔BF理论；最后检验近期提出的阿贝尔Chern-Simons对偶是否构成真正的TQFT对偶。

Result: 识别出阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论在中心和双重指向模情形的Turaev-Viro实现，并澄清了单块实现与典范双重实现的区别；建立了显式的Alterfold/Chern-Simons词典，给出拓扑边界、畴壁与凝聚扇区；证明有限二次模是控制体理论、其拓扑对称性、定向反演不变性和缺陷的不变量；证明多分量阿贝尔BF理论可作为离对角阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论的扩展TQFT出现在同一扩展框架内；并证明近期提出的阿贝尔Chern-Simons对偶并不定义真正的TQFT对偶。

Conclusion: 该工作为阿贝尔拓扑序中的缺陷提供了具体模型，建立了Alterfold与Chern-Simons之间的对应词典，统一了BF理论与Chern-Simons理论的扩展框架，并澄清了相关对偶的性质，为推广到非阿贝尔情形提供了可行路线。

Abstract: Recent work has constructed Abelian Chern-Simons theories as categorical TQFTs, allowing us to naturally incorporate categorical defects and construct defect extensions of Abelian Chern-Simons TQFTs. We first identify the Turaev-Viro realizations of Abelian Chern-Simons theory in the center and doubled pointed modular cases, clarifying the distinction between single bulk realizations and canonical doubled ones. Alternatively, the Alterfold construction supplies the associated topological boundaries, domain walls, and condensation sectors, establishing an explicit Alterfold/Chern-Simons dictionary. We show that the finite quadratic module is the invariant controlling the bulk theory, its topological symmetries, orientation-reversal invariance, and defects. We further show that multicomponent Abelian BF theory arises as the extended TQFT of an off-diagonal Abelian Chern-Simons theory, placing it naturally within the same extended framework. Finally, we demonstrate that recently proposed Abelian Chern-Simons dualities do not define a genuine TQFT duality. These results provide a concrete model for defects in Abelian topological orders and suggest a route toward the non-Abelian case.

</details>


### [197] [Gradient nature of Laplacian growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21430)
*A. Zabrodin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出将平面上一类Laplacian型增长过程解释为光滑闭曲线空间中的"梯度下降"，并证明增长区域的边界沿某泛函的梯度运动，最简单情形下该泛函为 log(1/r)，其中 r 为增长区域的外部共形半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为平面Laplacian型增长过程（如共形映射驱动的随机增长）提供统一的几何变分解释，将其纳入曲线空间中的梯度流框架，从而以泛函极值/梯度视角理解这类增长现象。

Method: 在光滑闭曲线构成的空间中构造适当的泛函与度量，研究Laplacian型增长过程对应的边界演化，并论证其等价于沿该泛函的梯度下降；借助外部共形半径等共形几何量分析边界运动方向。

Result: 证明了增长区域的边界确实沿所构造泛函的梯度方向移动；并给出该泛函在最简单情形下的显式形式 log(1/r)，其中 r 为增长区域的外部共形半径。

Conclusion: 平面Laplacian型增长过程可统一地视作曲线空间中的梯度下降，泛函 log(1/r)（r 为外部共形半径）是其最简体现，这为研究此类增长的几何与变分性质提供了新框架。

Abstract: For a class of growth processes of Laplacian type in the plane, we suggest an interpretation as a ``gradient descent'' in the space of smooth closed curves. More precisely, we show that boundary of a growing domain moves along a gradient of a certain functional in the space of curves. In the simplest cases this functional is $\log (1/r)$, where $r$ is the external conformal radius of the growing domain.

</details>


### [198] [Entanglement-Inducing Quantum Markov Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21438)
*J. Fransson,A. P. Sowa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种描述格点相互作用玻色子系统的新模型，其核心为非线性非局域演化方程——Schrödinger-Dirichlet方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建与Bose-Hubbard模型及广义玻色子相关的相互作用玻色子系统新模型，突破传统平均场闭合的局限。

Method: 提出非线性、非局域的Schrödinger-Dirichlet演化方程；分析方法基于正有理数乘法群上的调和分析。

Result: 所得非线性动力学无需保持乘积结构，能够从初始可分离态产生纠缠。

Conclusion: 该模型与常规平均场闭合不同，可生成纠缠，为正有理数乘法群上的调和分析提供了应用场景。

Abstract: We introduce a new model for a system of interacting bosons placed in an array of sites. At its core is a nonlinear, nonlocal evolution equation, which we have dubbed the Schrödinger-Dirichlet equation. The construction is closely related to the Bose-Hubbard model and to a specific type of generalized bosons. In contrast to conventional mean-field closures, the resulting nonlinear dynamics need not preserve product structure and can generate entanglement from initially separable states. The relevant methods of analysis are based on harmonic analysis for the multiplicative group of positive rationals.

</details>


### [199] [Generalized Hamiltonian formalism for spatially nonlocal nonlinear differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22066)
*Ali Pazarci,Nadir Ghazanfari,Ilmar Gahramanov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为空间非局域场论（拉格朗日密度显式依赖局域场及其空间反射场）建立了广义哈密顿形式，并将其应用于三类空间非局域非线性薛定谔方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间非局域场论中拉格朗日密度同时依赖场与其空间反射场，传统哈密顿形式难以一致处理反射场贡献，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 从广义变分原理出发，推导广义欧拉-拉格朗日方程，引入能一致计入反射场贡献的广义泛函导数，构建广义哈密顿形式。

Result: 对Ablowitz-Musslimani方程首次构造出以复场直接表示的标准拉格朗日密度并给出完整哈密顿形式；对Velasco-Juan与Fujioka的两个拉格朗日非局域非线性薛定谔方程给出自洽哈密顿形式，且能重现相应广义欧拉-拉格朗日方程。

Conclusion: 结果为一大类空间非局域非线性场论建立了统一的哈密顿框架。

Abstract: In this work, we develop a generalized Hamiltonian formalism for spatially nonlocal field theories whose Lagrangian densities depend explicitly on both the local field and its spatially reflected counterpart. Starting from a generalized variational principle, we derive generalized Euler-Lagrange equations and introduce a generalized functional derivative that consistently accounts for reflected-field contributions. The proposed formalism is applied to three spatially nonlocal nonlinear Schrödinger equations. For the Ablowitz-Musslimani equation, we construct, to the best of our knowledge, the first standard Lagrangian density formulated directly in terms of the complex fields and derive its complete Hamiltonian formulation. The same framework is subsequently applied to the two Lagrangian nonlocal nonlinear Schrödinger equations introduced by Velasco-Juan and Fujioka, yielding consistent Hamiltonian formulations that reproduce the corresponding generalized Euler-Lagrange equations. These results establish a unified Hamiltonian framework for a broad class of spatially nonlocal nonlinear field theories.

</details>
